\textbf{Stale (2026-10-07):} the Knudsen numbers, \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radii and exobase radii in this passage were computed by \texttt{src/utils/collisional\_validity.py} before its resonant H+ + H and He+ + He momentum-transfer coefficients (\texttt{md/Update\_EXHALE\_stage3.md} section 110). On the 102 stored LHS 1140 b states the change moves the \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radius out by 1.0-5.9\%, the exobase out by 0.4-5.7\% and the largest Kn down by 0-16\%; Kn(H2) and the sonic point do not move and no verdict changes. Not re-measured for the state quoted here.

The sonic point lies inside the 30 R\_p domain in every state. The GJ 699
lines are 3.0-3.5 times deeper than observed (width-matched red peak
3.8-4.3 per cent against 1.28), at the GJ 1132 line position (10833.27 A,
0.15 A blueward of the observed peak) and red/blue ratio (7.2-7.3). Thin
flag T in every state but He/H 10 (largest absorbed share 0.08-0.76, the
pure-GJ 699 0.763 from uncondensed water); without the gas above p\_match
0.03-0.04.

\textbf{No crossing in He/H on GJ 699.} The width-matched EW is 3.71 (0.65),
3.39 (2.09), 3.43 (5), 3.33 (10) on the split spectrum and 3.70 at 2.09 on
the pure file: 2.2-2.6 percent-angstrom above the observation everywhere,
weakly dependent on He/H. The observation lies between the two proxy
spectra (GJ 1132 0.71-0.85, GJ 699 3.33-3.71); the crossing is in the
stellar input, not in He/H.

\textbf{Penning loss.} At He/H 2.09, GJ 699 against GJ 1132 at fixed r (1.5-15
R\_p): n(2\^{}3S) x2.3-15.6; recombination gain n\_e n(He+) a\_TR x3.2-26.5;
n(H I) x0.61-1.14 (x(H+) rises 5-7 times but Mdot x6.6), so the Penning
loss rate falls by at most 40 per cent inside 3 R\_p and not beyond 8 R\_p;
the electron-collision loss rises x2.8-11.9 and takes the Penning share of
the loss from 0.5-0.7 to 0.2-0.3. P3 rises x2.12 (1.389e-5 against 6.54e-6
s\^{}-1) and stays below 5 per cent of the loss inside 8 R\_p. The 39x H I
photoionization does not raise n(2\^{}3S) by cutting the Penning loss; the
larger He I photoionization (x6.4) and the hotter, more ionized wind raise
its source.

\textbf{Fixed: the hydrogen carrier split of the escape boundary condition
(\texttt{src/utils/photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py}).} The elemental hydrogen flux
is put on H2 when the atomic-H share of the hydrogen nuclei at the model top
is below 1 per cent and split between H and H2 otherwise, but the share was
measured on the composition the imposition sees, on a fresh start the
initial one (1e-40): the split branch could not be reached, and a converged
top above 1 per cent was refused (split He/H 2.09 k00: 1.009e-2). Now the
converged share is compared with the share the boundary condition carries
(\texttt{LAST\_IMPOSITION}); where they differ by more than 1 per cent the converged
share is imposed (\texttt{CARRIER\_SHARE\_STATED}, needed because a re-solve on a new
temperature re-initializes the composition from the climate grid) and the
chemistry (under conduction, the column iteration) re-solved, up to 8
rounds; from the second round the imposed share is the mean of the imposed
and the converged one (the map has slope about -0.75 at He/H 30: 0 -\textgreater{}
0.098 -\textgreater{} 0.025 -\textgreater{} 0.079 -\textgreater{} 0.039). RED: split 2.09 k00 refused; GREEN: one
round, imposed 0.0101, converged 0.00914. A case needing no round runs the
unchanged path: the GJ 1132 2.09 k00 arguments give a profile
byte-identical to the closure\textquotesingle s (\texttt{cmp}).

\textbf{Found here, resolved in section 96.} \texttt{map\_state\_to\_grid.py\ -\/-reservoir} refused a carry whose first physical cell departs from the
requested C/H by more than 1e-12 relative; the pure GJ 699 closure at 2.09
was stopped twice by it (1.23e-12, 1.03e-12). The cause is not CO: the k00
state carries no OH, H2O or CO column (\texttt{oxychem=F}); its cell-1 C/H stands
1.03e-12 from its own \texttt{\#\ reservoir} line, and the factor new/old carries that
departure onto the new value (section 96).

He/H 10 and 30 jumped directly from 2.09 stalled in the first wind passes
and were stopped (kept); 10 converged from 5, and 30 from 10 was stopped in
its first wind pass after 34 min and not pursued.

\textbf{Not verified.} The k01 wind of the pure GJ 699 2.09 closure; pure GJ 699
at any other He/H; He/H above 10; the region beyond Kn\_bulk = 0.1 (6.8-11.5
R\_p).

\textbf{Stale (2026-10-07):} the Knudsen numbers, \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radii and exobase radii in this passage were computed by \texttt{src/utils/collisional\_validity.py} before its resonant H+ + H and He+ + He momentum-transfer coefficients (\texttt{md/Update\_EXHALE\_stage3.md} section 110). On the 102 stored LHS 1140 b states the change moves the \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radius out by 1.0-5.9\%, the exobase out by 0.4-5.7\% and the largest Kn down by 0-16\%; Kn(H2) and the sonic point do not move and no verdict changes. Not re-measured for the state quoted here.

\subsection{96. The mapper\textquotesingle s base-row check takes the consumer\textquotesingle s tolerance, 1e-6 (2026-10-04)}\label{96-the-mappers-base-row-check-takes-the-consumers-tolerance-1e-6-2026-10-04}

\textbf{What refused the route-B carry (MEASURED).} \texttt{map\_state\_to\_grid.py\ -\/-reservoir\ El/H\ v} multiplies the ionization-stage columns of El by
k = v/old, old the El/H of the source\textquotesingle s \texttt{\#\ reservoir} line, and then refused
the map when the base rows (He: the two inner ghosts and cell 1; a metal:
cell 1) came out more than 1e-12 relative from v. The GJ 699 He/H 2.09 k00
state (\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/route\_xuv/GJ699\_HeH2.09/k00/output})
carries no OH, H2O or CO column (\texttt{\#\ options\ ...\ oxychem=F}), so the CO
explanation of section 95 does not apply. Its cell-1 C/H is
2.77551410664327e-4 against the line\textquotesingle s 2.7755141066461295e-4, a relative
departure of -1.0303e-12 that the solve leaves (C/H varies by 2.5e-10 over
the physical cells); k carries it onto the new value unchanged, and the
check refused at 1.03e-12. The attempt-3 carry refused at 1.23e-12 the same
way. Both reproduced with the script as it stood, on copies.

\textbf{Why 1e-6 (READ, \texttt{src/modules/files\_IO/load\_IC.f90}).} The consumer of the
mapped state accepts the loaded He/H within \texttt{heh\_dev\_tol\ =\ 1.0d-6} (line 76,
the check at line 784), and renormalizes every metal column the handoff
states by one factor (handoff El/H)/(El/H at the base cell) (the "elements
the handoff states" loop, lines 969-1030), so a metal arrives at the run\textquotesingle s
El/H at the base cell whatever the mapper wrote. A 1e-12 check was a
million times stricter than the run that reads the file. The base-row check
now uses one named constant, \texttt{BASE\_ROW\_RATIO\_TOL\ =\ 1.0e-6}, for helium and
for the metals, with a comment pointing to \texttt{heh\_dev\_tol} and to the
base-cell renormalization; the refusal message states the tolerance and its
source. The check still catches the gross failure it exists for: a species
holding nuclei of two elements that the element\textquotesingle s one factor does not move
with it (HeH+; OH, H2O and CO, which the tool does not multiply). The
mapping itself is unchanged.

\textbf{Tests (\texttt{src/tests/state\_mapper/}, MEASURED).} New: a metal state whose
cell-1 C/H stands 1.03e-12 from its reservoir line maps and its departure
comes out unchanged (-1.03040e-12 against the source\textquotesingle s -1.03018e-12; bound
1e-15 on the difference); the same state at 2e-6 is refused and the message
names \texttt{heh\_dev\_tol}; HeH+ at 0.8 n\_H(cell 1) with He/H 1.6 -\textgreater{} 2.13 is
refused (the existing 0.05 case is still refused); the route-B k00 -\textgreater{} k01
carry with its closure arguments maps, every requested El/H at cell 1 within
4.4e-12. RED with the previous script: 5 failures (the 1e-12 case refused,
the route-B carry refused, three message checks). GREEN: \texttt{run.sh} 98 PASS,
0 failures (the stored-state cases included); \texttt{element\_flux\_closure/run.sh}
6 tests OK (it does not call the mapper). Byte identity: the 34 output files
of the suite\textquotesingle s previously passing maps are identical between the two
scripts (\texttt{cmp}), and 43 mapper invocations recorded in the
\texttt{route\_xuv/*/closure.log} and \texttt{phase5/*/closure.log} files, rerun with both
scripts on their recorded sources into scratch directories, give 42
byte-identical pairs of outputs and the route-B carry, refused before, now
written. The attempt-3 carry (1.23e-12) also maps now.

Installed at 2026-10-04 15:17:40 KST by an atomic replace (a temporary file in
\texttt{src/utils/} moved over the script), because the route\_heh\_high closures
invoke the script afresh at each iteration.

\textbf{Not verified.} The k01 wind of the route-B closure from the carried state

\subsection{97. Code audit of 2026-10-04: a non-finite restart center is refused; a zero conducted flux can close (2026-10-04)}\label{97-code-audit-of-2026-10-04-a-non-finite-restart-center-is-refused-a-zero-conducted-flux-can-close-2026-10-04}

Audit \texttt{../EXHALE\_audit\_20261004/CODE\_AUDIT\_20261004.md} (independent copy
of the 15:55 source; read in full).

\textbf{B1, fixed.} \texttt{verify\_restart\_radii} (\texttt{load\_IC.f90}) compared
\textbar r\_file - r\textbar/\textbar r\textbar{} against a running maximum; a NaN center compares false, so
the maximum stayed 0 and a file with a NaN radius passed the grid guard
(reproduced by the audit on the real module). A non-finite relative
difference is now set to huge() before the comparison, so it is refused as
a grid mismatch. Test \texttt{src/tests/restart\_radius\_guard/} (the audit\textquotesingle s driver,
linked against the tree\textquotesingle s objects): finite -\textgreater{} exit 0, different -\textgreater{} exit 1,
NaN -\textgreater{} exit 1 (before the fix NaN returned 0). Valid states are untouched:
the change acts only on non-finite values.

\textbf{B2, fixed.} \texttt{conducted\_flux\_residual} (\texttt{element\_flux\_closure.py})
returned inf whenever the measured conducted flux was zero, so an exact
zero/zero fixed point could never close. It now returns 0 for two exact
zeros, inf for a nonzero trial against a zero measurement, inf for a
missing or non-finite value, and the relative residual otherwise. Tests
added to \texttt{src/tests/element\_flux\_closure/} (10 pass). No stored closure has
a zero flux, so no verdict changes.

\textbf{P1, open (needs a decision).} With \texttt{Low-energy\ electron\ partition:\ Furlanetto2010} on, any n(H2) \textgreater{} 0 switches the atomic partition off in that
cell (\texttt{low\_energy\_electron\_degradation.f90}, the H2 gate), so the deposition
is discontinuous between zero and a trace H2 density, and the tables are
those of an atomic H/He mixture. The option is off in every LHS 1140 b run,
so no stored result is affected. Recorded as TO\_BE\_DONE (AJ).

\textbf{P2, P3} are the documented limitations already recorded: the
dissociative-recombination recipients (section 72 range statement at the
code site) and the NH3/CH4 overlap (\texttt{md/NH3\_CH4\_ISSUES\_20261004.md},
TO\_BE\_DONE (AI)).

The tree\textquotesingle s gfortran build was rebuilt; the running ifx closures use the
already-built binaries and are unaffected (the load\_IC change concerns only
non-finite input).

\subsection{98. Route B step 2: the observed He 10830 EW needs 1.33-1.39 times the GJ 1132 ionizing XUV at He/H 2.09 (2026-10-04)}\label{98-route-b-step-2-the-observed-he-10830-ew-needs-133-139-times-the-gj-1132-ionizing-xuv-at-heh-209-2026-10-04}

Plan \texttt{md/PLAN\_20261004\_lhs1140b\_heh\_high\_and\_xuv.md}, route B step 2 (user
decision 2026-10-04). Records:
\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/route\_xuv\_scaled/NOTES.md},
\texttt{compare\_xuv\_scaled.py}, \texttt{xuv\_scaled\_numbers.txt}, figures (inspected)
\texttt{fig\_xuv\_scaled\_line.png}, \texttt{fig\_xuv\_scaled\_profiles.png},
\texttt{fig\_xuv\_scaled\_ew\_vs\_s.png}. All solves and evaluates on lart3, binary
c37b3671. All numbers MEASURED; "EW wm" is the width-matched window EW in
percent-angstrom (added Gaussian velocity kernel of FWHM f, not a computed
physics term). Observed 1.1084 +- 0.0289.

\textbf{The spectrum (a sensitivity construction, not a stellar model).}
\texttt{make\_xuv\_scaled\_sed.py}: the GJ 1132 product with every row at lambda \textless=
911.75 A (X-ray and hydrogen-ionizing EUV, the band that photoionizes the
H I and He I ground states and in which the two proxies differ by 7x in
F\_EUV) multiplied by s, and every row above by one factor c holding the
bolometric flux (trapezoid over the file) at the GJ 1132 value: c - 1 =
-1.3e-5 at s = 1.36, -1.07e-4 at s = 4; the written files (17 digits)
reproduce the bolometric 5.439384522884e5 erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1 to 0 relative. The
911.75-2600 A band (Lyman alpha, FUV, NUV: the He 2\^{}3S photoionizing band and
the H2 Lyman-Werner band) keeps its shape, so the scan raises the He+
recombination source of 2\^{}3S and not its photoionization loss (P\_He23S at
the orbit +0.6 per cent at s = 1.36, from the part of the 2\^{}3S cross section
below 911.75 A; P\_HI and P\_HeI scale as s). Both the Photochem adapter and
the wind read the scaled file: \texttt{EXHALE\_setup.out} Log EUV / Log X equal the
control\textquotesingle s plus log10 s on every state, and the profile\textquotesingle s \texttt{stellar\_flux} record names the
file with its sha256.

\textbf{Settings} identical to the GJ 1132 He/H 2.09 cooling\_to\_space control of
section 95 (\texttt{route\_xuv/GJ1132\_HeH2.09}, k = 1, EW wm 0.7109) except the SED
path and the binary; continuation in s from the nearest converged s; no
iteration budget raised, no tolerance changed, no climate starting value
needed.

dEW(0.65 - 2.09): A +0.142, B +0.275, \textbf{C -0.275}, D +0.212. The energy
carried through cell N-20 equals Q\_net - L\_cond to 0.983-1.005 in all eight
states.

\textbf{Attribution} (log10 of the Mdot ratio): wind conduction C -\textgreater{} B +0.208
dex (it lowers Mdot by 0.247 dex at 2.09 and 0.038 dex at 0.65); energy
coupling B -\textgreater{} A -0.079 dex (it raises Mdot by 0.084 dex at 2.09, 0.005 at
0.65: it reduces the composition dependence); enthalpy fluxes D -\textgreater{} B +0.026
dex; remainder without conduction and coupling (C) 0.116 dex (x1.31), with
mu(T\_max) 2.61 -\textgreater{} 1.90, consistent with candidate (3) but not isolated.
Heat conduction in the wind is what makes Mdot depend strongly on
composition. Mdot follows the net heat of the gas above 1.02 R\_p (heating -
cooling + conduction source - enthalpy-flux divergence): B/C 0.587 at 2.09
(1.21e20 against 2.06e20 erg/s; Mdot 0.567) and 0.930 at 0.65 (Mdot 0.916).
At 2.09 conduction keeps the cool layer above 1.02 R\_p (1311 K at 1.02 R\_p
against 2304 K in C), where H3+ infrared cooling takes 2.75e19 erg/s (1.3e15
in C), the heating above 1.02 R\_p falls by 0.38e20 and the conduction source
removes 0.15e20; at 0.65 heating is unchanged and conduction removes
0.11e20 (interpretation of these budgets: the heavier gas keeps the hot
layer close to the cold base).

\textbf{Shapley (dEW, 9 factors, \texttt{decompose\_dEW.py} unchanged)}: n\_H +0.353 /
+0.340 / +0.442 / +0.362 (A / B / C / D); y -0.628 / -0.470 / -1.142 /
-0.560; He fractions -0.140 / -0.161 / -0.128 / -0.175; n\_e +0.368 / +0.423
/ +0.203 / +0.419; T +0.195 / +0.150 / +0.348 / +0.172; rest below 0.02.
No factor changes sign; the net flips in C because the helium-abundance
term doubles and the electron term halves: without conduction the 2.09
wind carries more helium (escaping He/H 0.73 against 0.50 in B; F\_He
8.3e7 against 4.2e7 g/s) and the hydrogen flux rises less toward 0.65
(x2.36 against x3.04). Synthesis endpoints equal the EW records in all
eight states.

\textbf{Not verified.} The C winds are subsonic inside the 30 R\_p domain
(isothermal Mach at most 0.66-0.68; A, B, D become supersonic at 18.5-21.5
R\_p), so the C mass-loss rates depend on the outer boundary, which was
held fixed; no larger domain was run. Candidate (3) not isolated by a
fixed-opacity mu solve. The mechanism sentence is an interpretation.

\textbf{Stale (2026-10-07):} the Knudsen numbers, \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radii and exobase radii in this passage were computed by \texttt{src/utils/collisional\_validity.py} before its resonant H+ + H and He+ + He momentum-transfer coefficients (\texttt{md/Update\_EXHALE\_stage3.md} section 110). On the 102 stored LHS 1140 b states the change moves the \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radius out by 1.0-5.9\%, the exobase out by 0.4-5.7\% and the largest Kn down by 0-16\%; Kn(H2) and the sonic point do not move and no verdict changes. Not re-measured for the state quoted here.

\textbf{The EW against He/H.} The width-matched EW passes a minimum at He/H 3-4
(0.676), rises to a local maximum at 14 (0.821), falls to 0.786-0.789 at
40-80 and rises again above 100, about 0.10 per decade above 1000, to 0.9600
at 1e4: 0.148 (5.1 nominal sigma) below the observed EW. \textbf{No He/H between
2.09 and 1e4 reaches the observed EW on the GJ 1132 spectrum.} Two paths
to the same He/H agree (10 from 5 and from 7: 0.8121 / 0.8106; 20 from 10
and from 14: 0.8137 / 0.8141). The lines keep one shape and position
(10833.27 A, 0.15 A blueward of the observed peak); the width-matched peak
grows to 1.107 per cent at 1e4 against the observed 1.28.

\textbf{He 2\^{}3S loss as hydrogen disappears} (the balance terms of
\texttt{phase5/dEW\_decomposition/triplet\_balance.py}; P3 6.54e-6 s\^{}-1 measured):
Penning ionization by H I, 0.64 of the loss at 1.5-3 R\_p at He/H 2.09, gives
way to electron collisions between He/H 3 and 4 (0.03 at 100, 3e-4 at 1e4);
electron collisions then take 0.97-1.00 inside 3 R\_p and radiative decay
A31 0.58-0.61 at 8 R\_p; P3 stays below 0.05 and the H2 quench below 1e-4.
The loss rate at 1.5 R\_p falls only to 0.62 of its 2.09 value (the
electron-collision floor) while n(He+) rises 1.8 times; n(2\^{}3S) there rises
2.7 times. Above He/H 100 the wind above 1.2 R\_p is otherwise unchanged
(T\_max 6886-6915 K) but Mdot rises 15 per cent (log10 7.758 -\textgreater{} 7.819) with
the warming base (T\_match 429 -\textgreater{} 856 K: the conducted flux heats a column
whose CH4, NH3 and H2O scale with hydrogen), and the He+ front and the 2\^{}3S
peak move down to the base (n3 peak 263 cm\^{}-3 at 1.14 R\_p at He/H 100,
2.6e3 at 1.03 R\_p at 1e4). The dEW was not decomposed on the high side; the
mechanism statements are interpretations of the balance terms.

\textbf{Validity.} Column: thin flag T up to He/H 5, F from 7 on (largest
absorbed share 0.20 at 2.09, 0.004-0.006 at 1000-1e4); exchange shift
0.36 -\textgreater{} 0.02 K. Hydrogen-poor molecular network: the gated carrier rows
(H2, H+, He+, He++, He 2\^{}3S) certify in the wind at every He/H (largest
He++, 9.6e-6 against 1e-5); match q\_H2 0.193 -\textgreater{} 5.0e-5 at 1e4. Climate and
Photochem succeeded at every He/H (deep 20-bar temperature 561 K at 2.09,
252 K at 400; tropopause 1.0 -\textgreater{} 9.3 bar). The share of the radial 2\^{}3S
column inside the ungated base band r \textless{} 1.2 R\_p grows from 0.014 (2.09) to
0.231 (100), 0.439 (1000) and 0.598 (1e4); the worst ungated species row of
those states is 0.4e-4 to 1.8e-4 (He++ at 1.15 R\_p), 4-18 times the wind\textquotesingle s
1e-5. The rise above He/H 100 is therefore carried increasingly by cells
the certificate does not gate (reported, not changed).

\textbf{He/H 8000 refused once, by a hydrogen-specific step.} Seeded from 4000,
the k00 wind stopped in its first sweep: "(ioniz\_eq) STOP: the base handoff
partition of a lower ghost did not close against its own ionization
balance" (30 passes, last move of the imposed 2 n(H2)/n\_H 5.10e-4 against
1e-10, residual 3.45e-4, pin error 0; EXHALE exit 128). Budget and tolerance
are compile-time parameters of \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, unchanged. The
same pair closed in at most 4 passes (to 1e-13) at 4000, 6000, 7000, 8000
(seeded from 6000) and 1e4, so the refusal depends on the seed\textquotesingle s radiation
field at the base, not on He/H; kept in \texttt{HeH8000.0/attempt1\_from\_4000/}.
The last good He/H is 1e4; nothing failed for good.

\textbf{Not verified.} The base band below 1.2 R\_p (above); the region beyond
Kn\_bulk = 0.1 (3.8-5.7 R\_p); a dEW decomposition of the minimum at 3-4 and
the maximum at 14; why the 4000-seeded ghost at 8000 did not close.

GJ 1132 has F\_EUV = 10.05, GJ 699 70.18. The required F\_EUV exceeds the
GJ 1132 value at every He/H (by at least 14 per cent, at 1e4), is nearly
flat (12.8-13.7) over He/H 0.45-100, rises steeply below 0.45 (14.6 at 0.3,
16.7 at 0.2: the s = 1 EW falls below He/H 0.65, so the same EUV gives less
line) and falls toward 1e4 (11.5, where the s = 1 EW has turned up, section
101). Interpolated intervals carry the observed 1 sigma only. States at
He/H 0.3 and 0.2 have the sonic point inside the domain (26.2-27.7 and
25.4-27.7 R\_p), the exobase above it, Kn\_bulk = 0.1 at 10.9-12.6 R\_p.

\textbf{He/H 0.1.} The first attempt (cooling\_to\_space) stopped at k01: the
exchange of the emission excess inside the warmed layer, neglected by that
transfer, moves T\_match by 1.003 K to first order against its bound of 1 K
(0.89 K at He/H 0.2). The bound was not changed; the transfer was extended
instead:

\textbf{New option \texttt{-\/-column-band-transfer\ cooling\_to\_space\_exchange}}
(\texttt{conducted\_heat\_column\_balance.py}, \texttt{solve\_with\_included\_exchange}; default
unchanged: \texttt{thin}). It is \texttt{cooling\_to\_space} with the exchange of the
emission excess between all warmed nodes of the domain solved (fixed point
on the exchange heating, the existing \texttt{solve\_with\_exchange}) instead of
neglected, so it has no bound of that kind; the record carries
\texttt{included}, the T\_match shift from the exchange, heating/F and the first
node. Validity as the module docstring states it (exact slab E\_3 fractions
for every g-point; for a two-level band weighted by eps\_beta at both ends,
exact for LTE bands). Tests (\texttt{src/tests/conducted\_heat\_column}, all pass):
bit-identical to \texttt{solve\_with\_exchange}, shift = the history difference,
shift \textgreater= 0, budget closes to 8.5e-15 (lte and nlte), an NH3 x 5 column\textquotesingle s
solved exchange is 1.38 times the first-order estimate that
cooling\_to\_space measures (the audit measured 1.25 on the LHS 1140 b
columns), the thin limit has no exchange. An NH3 x 300 column\textquotesingle s fixed
point settles slowly (30 iterations do not suffice: the increments fall
like n\^{}-1.5); the option\textquotesingle s iteration cap is 100 and a non-settling solve
raises, it does not return. The second attempt at He/H 0.1 with this
transfer passed the column and then stopped in the model-top pinning (12
re-pinned solves left a monotone -8.7e-5 against the 1e-5 test), so
\texttt{-\/-top-pressure-passes} was raised to 24 (the test unchanged), as for He/H
0.5 in section 90. Results at He/H 0.1 (cooling\_to\_space\_exchange, top-pressure passes 24,
\texttt{HeH0.1/xuv\_x*}): the EW (width matched, solution) is 0.7137 at s = 1.3,
0.9829 at 2.1, 1.0422 at 2.3, 1.0995 at 2.5 and 1.1079 at 2.53 (-0.0005
from the observation; closure converged at k = 1-4; sonic point 22.7 R\_p,

2.7 run was stopped by the operator during its first chemistry step to

\subsection{103. The physics-and-algorithms document audited against the source (2026-10-05)}\label{103-the-physics-and-algorithms-document-audited-against-the-source-2026-10-05}

\texttt{docs/EXHALE\_physics\_and\_algorithms.tex} and every \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/*.tex}
were checked statement by statement against \texttt{src/} (and the \texttt{src/utils/}
scripts they describe) and brought to the state of sections 1-102. Corrections
of what the document said the code does, each verified in the routine named:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  A Crank-Nicolson conduction stage that fails (floor, non-finite, pivot) stops
  the run (\texttt{conduction\_report}, \texttt{error\ stop\ 1}); it is not retaken at a shorter
  step as the energy step is. The base conductive flux (\texttt{eq:qbase}), its bath
  at r(0) (\texttt{conduction\_base\_level\_T}, the profile\textquotesingle s temperature where a profile
  is in use) and its \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} record are stated.
\item
  The heating efficiency eta is formed but written to no output file.
\item
  \texttt{momentum\_row\_terms\_of\_cell} is in \texttt{RK\_rhs.f90}; \texttt{sed\_band\_fluxes} replaces a
  routine name that does not exist; the molecular rows no longer carry the
  retired R20; Penning Q31 is Movre \& Meyer (1997) in the closed form of
  Garcia Munoz (2025); the sweep\textquotesingle s XUV field is one block
  (\texttt{xuv\_field\_block\_cells\ =\ 0}); the Ly-alpha default is \texttt{jlya\_mode\ =\ 0}.
\item
  The stationary residual\textquotesingle s energy row carries the carrier and stage enthalpy
  divergence; the certification report holds 33 entries; the PTC start is
  dtau0 = 1 on the marching handoff and the stationary restart, the CFL step
  only on the direct \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC=1} route.
\item
  New text: carried ionization stages and He 2\^{}3S transport (rows, fluxes,
  simplex, certification scales), the 10.2-30 eV partition of Furlanetto \&
  Stoever (2010) with its H2 gate and the (AJ) limitation, the elemental-flux
  closure and its acceptance rules, the escape boundary condition\textquotesingle s hydrogen
  carrier split, the column energy balance under the conducted heat (two-way
  energy coupling), the restart mapper rules and \texttt{verify\_restart\_radii}, the
  element nucleus velocities of the \texttt{\_adv} step, the stored-pair certificate,
  the width-matching velocity kernel (postprocessing, Sec. 10.5), and the
  environment variables that select physics or continuation factors.
  Numbers quoted from earlier sections are labeled with their section; the
  q31g/(q31a+q31b) ratios were re-measured (0.054 at 1e4 K). Source comments
  found stale and left for their owners: the header of \texttt{viscous\_conduction.f90}
  section 5 and the \texttt{conduction\_report} message ("B3a will reject and retry"),
  \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90} sections 2 and 6 (ion stages "not
  transported", "4x4 blocks"), \texttt{ionization\_stage\_transport.f90} "RELATION TO THE
  PROTON CARRIER", \texttt{low\_energy\_electron\_degradation.f90} (parameter name),
  \texttt{species\_table.f90} (f\_sp columns 7..33), \texttt{steady\_newton.f90}
  \texttt{set\_transported\_species\_rows}, the \texttt{-\/-column-band-transfer} help text of
  \texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py} (thin is flagged, not refused), and
  \texttt{EXHALE\_transit.py} lines 97-100 (bulk velocity). PDF rebuilt, 102 pages,
  0 LaTeX errors, no undefined references.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{104. The user documents audited against the source (2026-10-05)}\label{104-the-user-documents-audited-against-the-source-2026-10-05}

\texttt{docs/EXHALE\_user\_manual.tex}, \texttt{docs/transmission\_spectrum.tex}, \texttt{README.md},
\texttt{README\_HOWTO.md}, \texttt{md/input\_schema.md}, \texttt{docs/lhs1140b\_catalog\_state.tex},
\texttt{docs/Update\_EXHALE\_appendix.tex} and the workspace \texttt{CLAUDE.md} were checked
against \texttt{src/}, the \texttt{src/utils/} scripts and the LHS 1140 b EW tools for what
sections 39-102 added or changed; every statement of a key, default, token,
output field or option was read in the routine that implements it. Wrong
before, corrected:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  The option-token count of the restart contract (24 in \texttt{README.md}, 23 in

  atomic-hydrogen value in \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} and \texttt{md/input\_schema.md} K34b;
  it is the mixture value of section 41 (\texttt{thermal\_conductivity}), the
  viscosity alone keeping Watson\textquotesingle s (\texttt{dynamic\_viscosity}).
\item
  The \texttt{\_adv} step was described on the bulk velocity (manual, transit
  section); it steps each element on its own nuclei velocity (section 82,
  \texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90}).
\item
  \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_DTAU0} also replaces the start 1.0 of the stationary restart
  (\texttt{stationary\_state\_of\_the\_loaded\_restart}), not only that of
  \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC=1}.
\item
  \texttt{README.md} called carried ionization "H+ as a fifth carrier"; it is the
  three stages on their element\textquotesingle s nucleus flux, in an atomic gas too.
\item
  The \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} statement of the stationary evaluate (the measured
  state written back) and "the closure driver, which does not exist yet".
\item
  The manual lacked examples 18 and 19, and \texttt{docs/transmission\_spectrum.tex}
  had a wrong \texttt{n2\_populations} signature and notebook path.
  Added: \texttt{He\ 2\^{}3S\ transport}, \texttt{Interdiffusion\ enthalpy\ flux} (with the
  \texttt{carrier\_enth} / \texttt{stage\_enth} tokens), \texttt{H2\ double\ ionization}, \texttt{H2\ neutral\ dissociation} in the schema; the \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} records
  (\texttt{base\_conduction\_*}, \texttt{he23s\_transport}, \texttt{low\_energy\_electron\_partition});
  the \texttt{cert\_reason} values (\texttt{marching\_snapshot\_s\textless{}step\textgreater{}}, \texttt{pass\_snapshot\_p\textless{}k\textgreater{}},
  \texttt{projected\_seed}, \texttt{option\_change\_seed}, \texttt{legacy\_factor\_token},

  budget and evaluation dump keys; the column energy balance options of
  \texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py}, the profile header keys and which of them
  enter the \texttt{solution\_id}; the energy coupling, acceptance and resume rules of
  \texttt{element\_flux\_closure.py}; the mapper\textquotesingle s first-cell rule and
  \texttt{BASE\_ROW\_RATIO\_TOL}; the width-matching kernel, the EW operator and the

  observed line profile", manual and README for every planet). The appendix
  was re-measured with \texttt{codesize.py} (core 236 files, 109,539 code lines,
  31.2 times ATES\textquotesingle s 3,514); the catalog summary gained a section with the
  energy-coupled He/H and EUV scans (values read from the run files). Dated
  memos that stated a current state that is no longer true carry a one-line
  stale note: \texttt{kompot\_comparison}, \texttt{koskinen2022\_model\_a\_comparison},
  \texttt{composition\_restart\_and\_base\_handoff}, \texttt{lower\_atmosphere\_coupling}.

Found in sources outside these files and left for their owners: section 87
above calls column 3 of \texttt{tpm\_He10830.txt} the "instrument- and
rotation-convolved" curve, and so does the step-1 comment of

\subsection{105. The lower-ghost chemistry of LHS 1140 b at He/H 2e4: the reservoir H2 partition solved with its own ionization, and the molecular rows normalized by the turnover of their element (2026-10-06)}\label{105-the-lower-ghost-chemistry-of-lhs-1140-b-at-heh-2e4-the-reservoir-h2-partition-solved-with-its-own-ionization-and-the-molecular-rows-normalized-by-the-turnover-of-their-element-2026-10-06}

Request of 2026-10-06: the He/H 2e4 rung of the LHS 1140 b He/H ascent at
the fixed XUV scale s = 1.14 (GJ 1132 SED) stopped in its first closure
iteration (\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/route\_xuv\_scaled/HeH20000/xuv\_x1p14/k00},
binary \texttt{EXHALE\_c37b3671.x}) with \texttt{ioniz\_eq:\ persistent\ non-root\ chemical\ equilibrium}: the two lower ghost cells (0 and -1, 900 K, n\_H 4.07e8,
n\_He 8.13e12 cm\^{}-3) were non-roots from the first sweep, 2000 NON-ROOT lines
in \texttt{run.log}, the stop after 1000 consecutive sweeps, while the interior JFNK
reached \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} 5e-8. He/H 1e4 had none. Two defects, both in the code, each
measured before it was fixed. Scratch runs: \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/heh2e4\_diag/},
lart4. All numbers MEASURED unless marked READ.

\subsubsection{105.1 The reservoir H2 partition was pinned at the ionization of the previous state}\label{1051-the-reservoir-h2-partition-was-pinned-at-the-ionization-of-the-previous-state}

The base handoff states x2 = 2q(1 + He/H)/(1 + q) of the NON-IONIZED
hydrogen (\texttt{base\_h2\_nuclei\_fraction}), so the ghost owes
x\_H2 = x2 (1 - x\_ion), x\_ion the hydrogen in H+, H2+, H3+ and HeH+. The sweep
pinned row 4 at x2 (1 - x\_ion) with the x\_ion of the state it was HANDED and
closed the pair by passes with a secant step. A pinned x(4) leaves 1 - x(4)
of the hydrogen for H I and every ion together, and where the ionization
balance asks for more ions than that, no composition satisfies the rows. At
He/H 2e4 the H+ of the ghost is made by He+ + H2 -\textgreater{} H+ + H + He at the rate
the helium is photoionized, which doubles against the hydrogen from He/H 1e4
to 2e4. At the first sweep the pin was x(4) = 0.9698 (room 0.0302); the root
of the same rows with the partition solved together with the ionization has
x\_ion = 0.03136 (test below). The only root hybrd1 found with the pin
(attempt 1, info 1, residual 2e-16) had x(H+) + x(H2) = 1.0011, negative
H I, and O+/O = 1.044 (element violation 4.4e-2); every bounded iterate of
the three attempts was a non-root (residual 1e-4 to 5e-3), and the retained
non-root (x\_ion 3.6e-4) fed the next pin x(4) = 0.991, which left less room
still.

Fix (\texttt{ion\_cell\_state.f90}, \texttt{System\_HeH\_mol.f90}, \texttt{System\_HeH\_mol\_metals.f90},
\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, \texttt{constrained\_chemical\_equilibrium.f90}): the
row IS the equation, \texttt{fvec(4)\ =\ x(4)\ -\ x2\ (1\ -\ x\_ion(x))}, with x\_ion an
unknown of the same solve (\texttt{x\_h2\_neutral\_partition\_fixed},
\texttt{h2\_fraction\_of\_reservoir}, \texttt{ionized\_hydrogen\_nuclei\_fraction}), in the
fraction systems and in the constrained continuation, where H2 is now an
unknown with that row instead of a held density. The neutral hydrogen left,
(1 - x2)(1 - x\_ion), is never negative. \texttt{impose\_transported\_fractions}
places a starting point and a returned state on the row; the closure passes,
the secant and the infeasibility stop are removed (the closure check on the
returned state stays). With this alone the ghosts reached roots, but the
JFNK refused 275 trial states for ghosts without a root (worst residual
5e-5, just above the 1e-6 tolerance) and stopped with no descent at
\textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} = 1.9, which is the second defect.

\subsubsection{105.2 The rows were scaled by a bound that places trace ions at the whole of their element}\label{1052-the-rows-were-scaled-by-a-bound-that-places-trace-ions-at-the-whole-of-their-element}

The molecular rows were divided by the sum of the magnitudes their terms
reach with every species at the whole of its element (\texttt{set\_mol\_turnover\_rates}).
Where a trace ion of one element reacts with another element, the bound
exceeds the turnover by the inverse of the ion\textquotesingle s fraction. The H+ row carries
He+ + H2 (R17) and He(2\^{}3S) + H (Q31) with He+ and the metastable at all of
the helium: at the ghost root the bound was 3.9e12 cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1 against a gross
H+ rate P + L of 1.3e3, so the row reached the solve and the acceptance test
at 3e-10 of its weight. The Jacobian of the scaled system, its columns times
\textbar x\textbar, had its singular value along H+ at 9.8e-11; from the converged ghost at
one JFNK perturbation the Newton step asked for 12 per cent of H+, its full
length raised the residual from 1.3e-5 to 1.0, and hybrd1 returned info 4 at
the starting point. Ten per cent of H+ off the root moved the scaled H+ row
by 2.7e-11: the tolerance 1e-6 admitted an H+ imbalance of 3e3 times its
gross rate. He+, H2+, H3+ and HeH+ carry the same construction.

Fix: every row of the molecular system is divided by the CHEMICAL TURNOVER
OF ITS ELEMENT, G\_E = sum over the rows of element E of (P\_j + L\_j)
(hydrogen: H+, H2, H2+, H3+, HeH+; helium: He+, He++, He 2\^{}3S; each metal its
two stage rows, oxygen with OH and H2O). The gross rates come from the row
routines themselves (\texttt{mol\_heh\_rows}, \texttt{oxygen\_carrier\_rows}, \texttt{metal\_rows},
\texttt{cx\_add\_to\_fvec}, \texttt{he\_h\_cx\_fvec}, optional argument \texttt{gross});
\texttt{element\_turnover\_reciprocal} and \texttt{scale\_molecular\_rows} (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol.f90})
and \texttt{set\_element\_turnover\_scales} (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol\_metals.f90}) apply them.
The scale is taken at the starting point of each solve and HELD through it,
and a candidate is judged with the scale taken at the candidate
(\texttt{normalized\_reaction\_residual}, which no longer widens the molecular rows
by a separate charge-exchange bound: the gross rates contain it). A row with
no reaction keeps the old bound (1 for an identity row). The zeros of the
rows are unchanged. Two variants were measured and rejected: (i) each row by
its own P + L asks a species at 1e-22 of its element (H3+ in the outer wind)
to balance to 1e-6 of itself, and 350 cells of the He/H 2e4 run were left
without a root (hybrd1 info 1 with H2+, H3+, HeH+ rows at 1e-6 to 5e-1);
(ii) the element turnover re-taken at every iterate bounds every row by one
and leaves no gradient back from an iterate far outside the simplex: on the
cold start of the \texttt{mol\_metals} regression gate 214 cells were left without a
root, attempts went out to fractions of 2e5, and the ghost fixed point
stopped the run. The constrained continuation keeps the bound on its own
path; its candidate is judged by the same measure as every other. The rate
matrix of the decay-rate diagnostic now asks the systems for their rows as
volume rates (\texttt{mol\_rows\_as\_volume\_rates}) instead of undoing a scale.

Also fixed: \texttt{cce\_probe.x} stopped at once (\texttt{keq\_H\_H\_to\_H2\ called\ before\ h2\_thermochemistry\_init}); the driver now builds the table first. The two
charge-exchange reservoir tests that undid the scale by setting
\texttt{mol\_inv\_turnover\ =\ 1} ask for volume rates instead. The cell-state hash of
\texttt{attempted\_step.f90} covers the two new fields. Documents: the normalization
and the reservoir row are written into \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/ionization.tex}
and \texttt{molecules\_metals.tex}; \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_physics\_and\_algorithms.pdf} rebuilt.

\subsubsection{105.3 Tests}\label{1053-tests}

New suite \texttt{src/tests/ghost\_reservoir\_chemistry/} (fixture: ghost 0 of the
failing run at its first sweep, from its \texttt{EXHALE\_CCE\_DUMP}): the reservoir
row is x(4) - x2 (1 - x\_ion) to the bit; from the state the earlier code

divisor; the continuation solves H2 as an unknown, its layout is square, and
its H2 row is the reservoir row. GREEN: 9 of 9. RED, the same assertions on

for volume rates), \texttt{ionization\_imposed\_fractions} 48/48,

\texttt{charge\_exchange\_detailed\_balance} 298/298, \texttt{physics\_probe} 2499/2499,
\texttt{acceptance\_classes} 22/22, \texttt{boundary\_state} 43/43, \texttt{molecular\_seed} 29/29,
\texttt{attempted\_step} 70/70, \texttt{carrier\_returned\_state\_acceptance} 36/36,
\texttt{carrier\_retry} 156/156, \texttt{helium\_metastable\_transport} 24/24,
\texttt{carrier\_constraint\_attribution} 10/10, \texttt{carrier\_reference\_scales} 16/16,
\texttt{carrier\_helium\_inventory} 15/15, \texttt{carrier\_boundary\_jacobian} 12/12, and
\texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 424 PASS, 3 FAIL: \texttt{stationary\_evaluate\_rebuilt\_within\_sweep\_budget}
(5.832e-5), \texttt{stationary\_evaluate\_certification\_unchanged} and
\texttt{stationary\_evaluate\_exit\_status} (2) on the \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} fixture, the
same three with the same numbers from the source of \texttt{EXHALE\_c37b3671.x}, so
not of this change (an atomic fixture no longer certified by the current
code; reported, not touched).

\subsubsection{105.4 The actual cases}\label{1054-the-actual-cases}

New frozen binary \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_f6945afb.x} (ifx 2026.1.0, md5

\subsection{106. A failed conduction stage is a refused and retaken step; the handoff-free base-level conflict warns; stale source comments corrected (2026-10-06)}\label{106-a-failed-conduction-stage-is-a-refused-and-retaken-step-the-handoff-free-base-level-conflict-warns-stale-source-comments-corrected-2026-10-06}

User decisions of 2026-10-05 (items A and B) and the source comments the two
documentation audits of sections 103 and 104 left for their owners (item C).
Scratch runs: \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/condretry/}, lart4. Numbers
MEASURED unless marked READ.

\subsubsection{106.1 A failed Crank-Nicolson stage refuses the attempted step}\label{1061-a-failed-crank-nicolson-stage-refuses-the-attempted-step}

Before: \texttt{conduction\_report} (\texttt{viscous\_conduction.f90}) ended a failed stage
(floor, non-finite input or solution, pivot breakdown) with \texttt{error\ stop\ 1};
the header (section 5) and the message said "B3a will reject and retry",
which the marching loop never did. Now the stage prints its diagnostics,
leaves its verdict in \texttt{conduction\_last\_status} and the \texttt{status} argument and
returns no state; \texttt{EXHALE\_main} passes \texttt{status}, and on a failure refuses the
attempt with \texttt{as\_reject\_conduction} at \texttt{as\_op\_conduction} and leaves the trial,
exactly as a failed energy update does (both run modes). The controller then
restores the checkpoint and retakes the step at half dt
(\texttt{attempted\_step\_reduced\_dt}) within its budget of \texttt{n\_step\_retry\_max} = 8
halvings; at dt/2\^{}8 \texttt{stop\_on\_exhausted\_retry\_budget} stops the run with exit
status 2 and the retry history. The refusal text names the stage\textquotesingle s reason
and cell. \texttt{conduction\_stop\_on\_failure} is removed (only the unit driver set
it). Why a shorter step is the remedy (header section 5): a mode of the
conduction operator with decay rate lambda/dt is multiplied by
(1 - lambda/2)/(1 + lambda/2), negative for lambda \textgreater{} 2, so a step longer than
twice the diffusion time of a cell overshoots; halving dt halves lambda. The
two remaining \texttt{error\ stop} statements of the module are not stage failures
and stay: an \texttt{EXHALE\_CONDUCTION\_SCALE} outside {[}0, 1{]} (input) and
\texttt{h2\_conductivity} asked for at a non-positive or non-finite temperature (an
argument guard also reached from the stationary residual; the stage\textquotesingle s entry
test covers every cell its stencil reads).

Test hook, default off: \texttt{EXHALE\_CONDUCTION\_REFUSE\_DT\_ABOVE\ =\ t} {[}s{]} makes the
stage return the new status \texttt{CONDUCTION\_INJECTED} (reason
\texttt{CONDUCTION\_REASON\_INJECTED}) for every attempt whose longest cell interval
exceeds t, and print that interval. New suite \texttt{src/tests/conduction\_retry}
(hot-Uranus gate \texttt{mol\_carrier} with \texttt{Conduction:\ True}, 1 or 2 steps, 40 s),
registered in \texttt{executable\_identity}: (1) t = 1e-300: 9 refusals at the
conduction step, every interval exactly half the one before (worst
\textbar ratio - 1/2\textbar{} = 0), the exhaustion report naming the stage, exit 2; (2)
t = 0.3 of the first interval (1.684 s): two refusals, the third attempt
(1/4) accepted, exit 0; (3) t = 1e300: no refusal, \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt} and
\texttt{Ion\_species.txt} byte-identical to the run without the hook. 20 PASS with the
tree build; with the control build (the tree before these edits) the rows of
(1) and (2) FAIL (no refusal, exit 0). Unit level: \texttt{energy\_update}
\texttt{conduction\_floor\_tests} no longer sets the flag; with the new objects all 69
rows pass, with the control objects the floor case ends in \texttt{ERROR\ STOP\ 1}.

Byte identity where no stage fails: \texttt{mol\_carrier} + \texttt{Conduction:\ True}, 300

(\texttt{exhaustion\_on\_the\_integration\_error\_*},

\subsubsection{106.4 The atomic acceptance scale (measured, not changed)}\label{1064-the-atomic-acceptance-scale-measured-not-changed}

\texttt{normalized\_reaction\_residual}, atomic branch, on the certified LHS 1140 b
atomic state \texttt{ew\_fid\_atomic\_nocond} (He/H 9.7, He 2\^{}3S on), rows recorded with
\texttt{EXHALE\_STAGE\_CHANNELS} at eight cells: the H+ row scale is 3.9e12 (cell 2)
to 2.1e2 (cell 495, 26 R\_p) times the gross H+ rate, and the term that sets
it is the Penning bound f Q31 n\_He n\_H (He 2\^{}3S at all of the helium), 0.99 to
1.00 of the scale; the He \textless-\textgreater{} H charge exchange term that section 105 named is
1.6e-6 to 2.4e-6 of it. The He 2\^{}3S row scale is 1.4e14 to 3.9e3 times its
gross rate. With \texttt{ieq\_res\_tol} = 1e-6 the test cannot refuse a non-root in
these rows inside about 1.35 R\_p. The roots on this state are tight (row
residual 1e-16 of the gross rate). Recorded as \texttt{md/TO\_BE\_DONE.md} (AM).

Build: bare \texttt{make}, tree \texttt{EXHALE.x} md5 \texttt{3042c8726291e0b02d796c3325fa95cf}.

changed pages 4, 12 and 62 inspected) and the manual (125).

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{107. The helium carry of \texttt{map\_state\_to\_grid.py\ -\/-reservoir} holds the hydrogen-nucleus count of every row (2026-10-06)}{107. The helium carry of map\_state\_to\_grid.py -\/-reservoir holds the hydrogen-nucleus count of every row (2026-10-06)}}\label{107-the-helium-carry-of-map_state_to_gridpy---reservoir-holds-the-hydrogen-nucleus-count-of-every-row-2026-10-06}

The elemental-flux closure of the LHS 1140 b He/H ascent stopped at the
He/H 7e4 rung: the seed \texttt{route\_xuv\_scaled/HeH40000/xuv\_x1p14/k01/output}
(He/H 3.9974e4) carried onto He/H 69936.32, C/H 2.758973e-4, N/H 8.133062e-5,
O/H 6.023026e-4 was refused, "C/H ... 2.758969E-04 ... instead of
2.758973E-04 (relative departure 1.52E-06, above the tolerance 1E-06)".

\subsubsection{107.1 Cause}\label{1071-cause}

Not the file precision: the mapper writes every column at \texttt{\%24.16E} and the
check runs on its own float64 arrays before writing. The departure was a
real inconsistency of the helium carry. \texttt{-\/-reservoir\ He/H} multiplies every
helium column by k = new/old, HeH+ included (\texttt{element\_stage\_columns}), and
HeH+ holds one hydrogen nucleus, so every row gained (k - 1) n(HeH+)
hydrogen nuclei and every El/H of the row moved by -(k - 1) n(HeH+)/n\_H:
the metals that the same call carried by their own factor new/old, and
He/H itself. The seed holds HeH+/H = 2.024e-6 at cell 1 (6.0e-7 and 6.2e-7
in the two lower ghosts; MEASURED from its \texttt{Ion\_species.txt}), and the
departure followed (k - 1) x 2.024e-6 exactly (MEASURED with the entry
mapper):

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
{\small
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}lll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
target He/H & k - 1 & departure (C/H, cell 1) \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
4.5e4 & 0.126 & passed \\
5e4 & 0.251 & passed \\
6e4 & 0.501 & 1.01e-6 \\
69936.32 & 0.750 & 1.52e-6 \\
1e5 & 1.502 & 3.04e-6 \\
\end{longtable}
}
}

Why the 2e4 and 4e4 rungs passed was not measured (their seeds\textquotesingle{} HeH+/H at
cell 1 was not read).

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{107.2 Fix (\texttt{src/utils/map\_state\_to\_grid.py})}{107.2 Fix (src/utils/map\_state\_to\_grid.py)}}\label{1072-fix-srcutilsmap_state_to_gridpy}

The hydrogen-nucleus count of every row is held. After the columns of an
element are multiplied, the (k - 1) Hs hydrogen nuclei they brought (Hs the
hydrogen nuclei of the multiplied columns: HeH+ for helium, none for a
metal) are taken out of the species holding only hydrogen (H I, H II, H2,
H2+, H3+), all multiplied by one factor g = 1 - (k - 1) Hs/P, P their
hydrogen nuclei, which keeps their ionization and molecular split; their
mass (1 per hydrogen nucleus, \texttt{bsp\_mass}) and particles plus electrons
(H I 1, H II 2, H2 1, H2+ 2, H3+ 2) enter the density and the temperature
written at fixed pressure. Every row then carries k times its source He/H
and its source ratio of every other element exactly, in whatever order the
elements are named. The \texttt{-\/-uniform} row factor becomes
(value n\_H - X)/S with n\_H held. A HeH+ carry that the hydrogen-only species
cannot supply (g \textless= 0) is refused. The base-row test and its tolerance are
unchanged (\texttt{BASE\_ROW\_RATIO\_TOL} = 1e-6, \texttt{heh\_dev\_tol} of \texttt{load\_IC.f90}); it
now catches only the oxygen carriers OH, H2O and CO, which hold nuclei of
two elements and are not multiplied. Before this, a HeH+-bearing state was
refused by design ("no single factor sets both counts"); it is now carried.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{107.3 Tests (\texttt{src/tests/state\_mapper/})}{107.3 Tests (src/tests/state\_mapper/)}}\label{1073-tests-srctestsstate_mapper}

The two assertions that a HeH+ state is refused are replaced by the
carry with n\_H held (HeH+/H 0.05 and 0.3: n\_H equal to the control mapping to
4e-16, HeH+ multiplied by k, base rows at the new He/H to 2e-16, the
diffused profile shape and the H II/H I ratio kept, T = p/((n\_tot + n\_e) k\_B)
and rho the species mass sum to 3e-15), a refusal when the hydrogen-only
species cannot supply the hydrogen (HeH+/H 0.3 carried by 6/1.6), the
\texttt{-\/-uniform} carry with HeH+, and a synthetic LHS 1140 b state (He/H 3.9974e4,
HeH+/H 2.024e-6, C/H, O/H) carried to He/H 69936.32 and 1e5 together with
C/H and O/H. RED with the entry mapper: 5 failures (the two LHS carries
refused at 1.52e-6 and 3.04e-6, the two HeH+ carries refused, n\_H not held
under \texttt{-\/-uniform}, 0.29). GREEN after: 131 PASS, 0 failures. The suites that
call the mapper without \texttt{-\/-reservoir} (\texttt{boundary\_state},
\texttt{coupled\_block\_jacobian}, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates}) were not run: the change lies
inside the \texttt{-\/-reservoir} branch only, and the suite\textquotesingle s own no-option
byte-identity assertions pass.

\subsubsection{107.4 The real seeds (MEASURED)}\label{1074-the-real-seeds-measured}

The closure\textquotesingle s command (the seed \texttt{output} of \texttt{HeH40000/xuv\_x1p14/k01} and
of \texttt{k00}, onto the target grid header of \texttt{HeH70000/xuv\_x1p14/k00}, C/H,
N/H, O/H as in the closure log) for He/H 69936.32 and 1e5 now writes the pair; recomputed
from the written \texttt{Ion\_species\_IC.txt} the first-cell departures are, for k01:
C/H 1.3e-14, N/H 5e-15, O/H 5e-15, He/H 8.6e-13 (base rows); for k00:
C/H 1.9e-14, N/H 2.7e-14, O/H 1.7e-14, He/H 3.2e-12; the same at 1e5.
The frozen binary \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_f6945afb.x}, run in scratch on
the He/H 7e4 pairs of both seeds with the rung\textquotesingle s \texttt{input\_template.inp} and
the k00 \texttt{lower\_atmosphere\_profile.dat}, loads them: no restart-contract or
He/H refusal, "base cells set to the input He/H = 6.9936E+04", C/H, O/H and
N/H "column rescaled by 1.000000E+00". The 1e5 pair was not loaded: no
profile at He/H 1e5 exists, and a copy of the 7e4 profile with X\_He
multiplied is refused by \texttt{input\_read} ("the base H2 fraction is not
attainable at this He/H").

Noticed, not changed: \texttt{input\_read.f90:3159} and \texttt{:3309} print He/H with
\texttt{F8.5}, which shows \texttt{********} at He/H \textgreater= 1e3 (the LHS 1140 b ascent) and
\texttt{q\_H2(base)\ -\textgreater{}\ 0.00001} for 7.1e-6.

Documents: \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/boundaries\_initial.tex} (the mapper
paragraph) and \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_user\_manual.tex} (the mapper paragraph and the
closure paragraph) state the held n\_H; rebuilt without LaTeX errors, the

\subsection{108. The EUV that matches the observed EW reaches He/H 1e5 (2026-10-06)}\label{108-the-euv-that-matches-the-observed-ew-reaches-heh-1e5-2026-10-06}

The He/H ascent of section 102 above 1e4 at fixed s = 1.14 (the s that matched
at 1e4), each rung seeded from the converged one: 2e4 and 4e4 converged at
k = 1, 7e4 and 1e5 at k = 2 (max residual 0.0095, 0.0125, 0.0071, 0.0045; no
non-root cell). It could be run only after two code defects were removed: the
lower ghost chemistry at 2e4 (section 105) and the mapper\textquotesingle s helium carry at
7e4 (section 107); the closures use the binary \texttt{EXHALE\_f6945afb.x}. The
width-matched solution EW at s = 1.14 is 1.1285 (2e4), 1.1607 (4e4), 1.2083
(7e4), 1.2425 (1e5) against 1.1084 observed: the s = 1 EW has turned up above
1e4, so the matching s falls. At He/H 1e5, s = 1.06 gives 1.1575, s = 1.03
gives 1.1247 (EW proportional to s\^{}1.0 here) and the crossing at 1.015 was
confirmed by a converged state: s = 1.015 gives 1.1082 (-0.0002; closure
k = 1, residual 0.0108). F\_EUV(100-911) = 10.20 (1.5 per cent above the
unscaled GJ 1132 value 10.05); the 1-sigma interval of s is 0.989-1.042 (lower
end extrapolated). The He/H = 0.1 crossing was confirmed too: s = 2.53 gives
1.1079 (-0.0005) and s = 2.6 gives 1.1273, so the table has no extrapolated
point left (He/H 1e3 at s = 1.25: 1.1053). Figure 6 of
\texttt{docs/lhs1140b\_exhale\_vs\_pwinds.tex} now has thirteen He/H, 0.1 to 1e5.
The EW of states above He/H 1e4 was evaluated with f6945afb
(\texttt{measure\_case\_f6945afb.sh}, \texttt{run\_ew\_f6945afb.sh}, copies of the control
scripts with the binary name changed). Record: \texttt{NOTES.md} section 7 of
\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/route\_xuv\_scaled/}.

\subsection{109. The equivalent width and the width-matching kernel read the planet-rotation column (user decision 2026-10-06)}\label{109-the-equivalent-width-and-the-width-matching-kernel-read-the-planet-rotation-column-user-decision-2026-10-06}

\texttt{tpm\_He10830.txt} carries four columns: \texttt{T\_theo}, \texttt{T\_instr} (instrument
profile only) and \texttt{T\_rot+instr} (the tidally locked planet\textquotesingle s rotation, an
azimuthal disk average, and the instrument profile). The EW operator, the
width-matching kernel and the memo figures read column 3 (\texttt{T\_instr}); a
literature check (Huang et al. 2023 section 4.3; Taylor et al. 2025 and 2026;
Biassoni et al. 2024 section 2, whose transit tool is the ancestor of
\texttt{EXHALE\_transit.py}) shows that published He and metal-line transit models do
add the planet\textquotesingle s rotation as a computed broadening, and keep any remaining
width shortfall as a separate non-thermal term. The convention of CLAUDE.md
("Line width ... every planet") therefore now reads column 4: the rotation is
a computed term of the model and the Gaussian velocity kernel stands only for
the velocity field the one-dimensional model does not contain.

Code: \texttt{LHS1140b/he10830\_equivalent\_width.py} defines \texttt{TRANSIT\_COLUMN\ =\ 3}
(0-based) and uses it in \texttt{transit\_file\_excess} and \texttt{transit\_file\_air\_excess}
(and so in the EW, \texttt{-\/-width-matched} and \texttt{ew\_of\_state.sh});
\texttt{LHS1140b/make\_memo\_figures.py} \texttt{curve\_file} reads the same column;
\texttt{EXHALE\_transit.py} fits the \texttt{tpm\_He10830\_metrics.txt} three-Gaussian metrics
on the rotation-and-instrument curve as well (it fitted the instrument-only
curve). All 1560 stored \texttt{tpm\_He10830.txt} files have four columns. Tests
\texttt{transit\_state} and \texttt{transit\_census} pass.

Effect, MEASURED: on the converged He/H 1e5, s = 1.015 state the width-matched
EW is 1.10816 (column 3: 1.1082) and f = 21.4743 km/s (21.4803); at He/H 0.1,
s = 2.53 the unbroadened EW and the kernel differ by 1e-6 \%A and 0.01 km/s
(LHS 1140 b: v\_rot = 0.03 km/s). Every EW and every figure of this memo series
is therefore unchanged at the printed precision; they were computed from
column 3 and are not recomputed. For a planet with a fast spin the two
columns differ materially and column 4 is the one to use. The documents that
described the column (\texttt{CLAUDE.md}, README, README\_HOWTO, user manual,
\texttt{transmission\_spectrum.tex}, \texttt{physics\_overview/postprocessing.tex}, the
LHS 1140 b memo) are updated.

\subsection{110. Resonant H+/H and He+/He coefficients in the collisional diagnostic; HITRAN band coefficients and two comments of the conducted-heat column corrected (2026-10-07)}\label{110-resonant-hh-and-hehe-coefficients-in-the-collisional-diagnostic-hitran-band-coefficients-and-two-comments-of-the-conducted-heat-column-corrected-2026-10-07}

Two defects found by the 2026-10-06 audits, each checked against the cited
source before the change.

\textbf{1. \texttt{src/utils/collisional\_validity.py}: resonant charge exchange.} The
tool computed the ion-parent-atom pairs H+ + H and He+ + He with the
non-resonant polarization + rigid-core coefficient of the element operator.
That is physically wrong for these pairs: their momentum transfer is set by
resonant charge exchange, and the non-resonant form gives 0.52 (H+ + H) and
0.68 (He+ + He) of the published values at 1e4 K (MEASURED from both
formulas; 0.70-0.52 and 0.77-0.67 over 500-2e4 K). The published
coefficients now REPLACE the non-resonant one for these two pairs
(\texttt{RESONANT\_PAIRS}, \texttt{resonant\_charge\_exchange\_rate\_coefficient}, dispatched in
\texttt{momentum\_transfer\_frequency}), in the tool\textquotesingle s convention \texttt{nu\_st\ =\ n\_t\ K}:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  H+ + H: Glassgold, Krstic \& Schultz (2005, ApJ 621, 808; publisher PDF via
  ADS, read), eq. (3.19), \texttt{K\ =\ 2.13e-9\ (v\_rms/km\ s\^{}-1)\^{}0.75\ cm\^{}3\ s\^{}-1}, with
  \texttt{v\_rms\ =\ (vbar\^{}2\ +\ w\^{}2)\^{}(1/2)} (their eq. 3.15) and \texttt{vbar\ =\ (8\ a\^{}2/pi)\^{}(1/2)},
  \texttt{a\ =\ (kT/m)\^{}(1/2)} with m the reduced mass m\_H/2 (their \texttt{vbar(100\ K)\ =\ 2.05\ km/s} confirms it); their drag force \texttt{f\_d\ =\ n\_j\ n\_k\ m\_jk\ w\_jk\ K\_jk}
  (eq. 3.1) is the tool\textquotesingle s convention. Accuracy 10-15\% for v\_rms \textgreater{} 1 km/s
  (their section 3.3). The drift w is not known to the tool; w = 0 is used,
  the smallest K.
\item
  He+ + He: Schunk \& Nagy (1980, Rev. Geophys. Space Phys. 18, 813;
  \texttt{references/Schunk\_1980RGSP\_18\_813.pdf}), Table 5, \texttt{nu\_in\ =\ 8.73e-11\ n(He)\ T\_r\^{}(1/2)\ (1\ -\ 0.093\ log10\ T\_r)\^{}2\ s\^{}-1}, T\_r \textgreater{} 50 K, with their eq. (19)
  convention \texttt{n\_s\ m\_s\ nu\_st\ =\ n\_t\ m\_t\ nu\_ts}, so \texttt{K\ =\ 2\ nu\_in/n(He)}
  (conversion of \texttt{md/AK\_ambipolar\_stage\_drift\_derivation\_20261006.md}
  section 6.1). The K values reproduce that note\textquotesingle s Table 6.4 to its digits
  (H+ + H 6.67e-9, 2.05e-8; He+ + He 2.19e-9, 6.89e-9 at 500 and 1e4 K).
\end{itemize}

H2+ + H2 keeps the non-resonant coefficient (no published resonant value
checked); the header and \texttt{md/collisional\_validity.md} (section 1 table, the
omitted-channel paragraph) say so.

Effect, MEASURED: on \texttt{route\_xuv\_scaled/HeH0.65/xuv\_x1p3/k01} (as
\texttt{measure\_case.sh} calls it) the \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radius moves from 8.779 to
8.883 R\_p, \texttt{Kn\_bulk} at the top from 0.993 to 0.963, the largest Kn of the
critical region from 0.982 to 0.944 (H I 0.98 to 0.94); the sonic point
(26.196 R\_p), the exobase (above the 29.03 R\_p boundary), the electron-ion
energy-coupling ratio (0.0216) and the verdict do not move. Re-run on all
102 stored LHS 1140 b states of \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/} that have a
\texttt{collisional*.txt} and their run directory (output to scratch; the stored
files were not regenerated; 9 \texttt{compare/} records whose run directory is
gone were skipped): \texttt{Kn\_bulk\ =\ 0.1} radius +1.0 to +5.9\%, exobase (72 states
with one in the domain) +0.4 to +5.7\%, largest Kn of the critical region 0
to -16\%; no verdict changes (every state stays above 0.1); two exobases at
28.9-29.0 R\_p move above the outer boundary (\texttt{GJ699split\_HeH5.0},
\texttt{HeH0.65/xuv\_x1p25}). The largest changes are on the most ionized states
(GJ 699 spectrum, He/H \textgreater= 1e3). On the ionized regression case
\texttt{backup/regression/wasp\_full\_newton} (WASP-121 b) the largest Kn falls 31\%
(2.58e-5 to 1.78e-5).

Documents quoting this tool\textquotesingle s numbers are marked stale in place (a "Stale
(2026-10-07)" paragraph after each quoting passage; values left as they
were, not re-measured for the states quoted): \texttt{md/collisional\_validity.md}
(section 4 header), \texttt{md/Update\_EXHALE\_stage1.md} (3), \texttt{md/Update\_EXHALE\_stage2.md}
(2), this log (22 passages between sections 2 and 104), \texttt{md/lhs1140b\_stationary\_L4f},
\texttt{\_L8} (2), \texttt{\_L10}, \texttt{\_L12b\_derivation}, \texttt{\_L22}, \texttt{\_L36}, \texttt{\_L36e},
\texttt{md/TO\_BE\_DONE.md}, \texttt{md/PLAN\_20260923\_rev2.md} (2),
\texttt{md/lhs1140b\_p14\_outer\_closure\_20260923.md}, \texttt{md/session\_handoff\_20260930.md}
(2), \texttt{md/PLAN\_20260930\_rev1/rev2/rev3.md},
\texttt{md/lhs1140b\_width\_nonthermal\_candidates.md},
\texttt{md/PLAN\_20261003\_lhs1140b\_ew.md} (5), \texttt{md/PLAN\_20261004\_lhs1140b\_heh\_high\_and\_xuv.md},
\texttt{md/lhs1140b\_ew\_codex\_review\_20261003.md},
\texttt{md/LHS1140b\_NH3\_CH4\_EW\_REVIEW\_20261004.md},
\texttt{docs/lhs1140b\_catalog\_state.tex} (1) and \texttt{docs/lhs1140b\_exhale\_vs\_pwinds.tex}
(15). The typeset twins \texttt{docs/Update\_EXHALE\_stage\textless{}N\textgreater{}.tex} were not edited
(generated). Not re-typeset: the two \texttt{.tex} documents above.

\textbf{2. \texttt{src/utils/conducted\_heat\_column\_balance.py}.}

(a) The comment "NH3 relaxes 16 times faster with He than CD4 does" is wrong
against the cited tables: Hovis \& Moore (1980, J. Chem. Phys. 72, 2397)
Table I, NH3(nu2) + He 1.1e-13 (198 K) and 2.8e-13 (293 K), against Siddles,
Wilson \& Simpson (1994, Chem. Phys. 188, 99) Table 1, CD4(nu2, nu4) + 4He
7.5e-15 (190 K) and 2.8e-14 (295 K) (both read in \texttt{references/}): ratios 10.0
(293/295 K) and 14.7 (198/190 K); with the code\textquotesingle s Landau-Teller NH3 rate at
the CD4 temperatures 13.4, 12.6, 10.1 at 190, 240, 295 K (MEASURED). The
comment now says 10 to 15 and gives the four numbers.

(b) The two CH4 A10 values at 185 K (1.96 s\^{}-1 in \texttt{NLTE\_BANDS}, 2.16 s\^{}-1 in
\texttt{TWO\_LEVEL\_CHECK}) are different quantities: the first is the average over
the dyad population (nu4 and nu2 levels), the second the nu4 level alone
(the check\textquotesingle s formula uses g1 = 3 and nu0 = 1306). Recomputing both from the
HITRAN line list (hitran.org line-by-line API, retrieved 2026-10-07, main
isotopologue; the selected line sets reproduce the code\textquotesingle s band intensities,
nu4 5.141e-18 and NH3 nu2 2.245e-17) gives A(nu4) 2.25 and A(dyad) 2.04 s\^{}-1
at 185 K, i.e. both code values were 4\% low, and the dyad table 4-10\% low
over 150-700 K. The published value is Yelle (1991, ApJ 383, 380) Table 2,
A(nu4) = 2.12 s\^{}-1, 6\% below the line sum; the band-intensity relation
\texttt{A\ =\ 8\ pi\ c\ nu0\^{}2\ S/(3\ x\ abundance)} gives 2.23 s\^{}-1 at 296 K, agreeing with
the line sum. The same recomputation for NH3 nu2 gives 14.64, 14.69, 14.85,
15.11, 15.36 s\^{}-1 at 150, 185, 296, 500, 700 K against the code\textquotesingle s 13.46,
13.21, 12.56, 11.89, 11.37 (9-26\% low, with the wrong temperature trend);
published 15 s\^{}-1 (Weaver \& Mumma 1984, ApJ 276, 782). New values (MEASURED):
CH4 dyad 2.11, 2.04, 1.86, 1.68, 1.59 s\^{}-1; NH3 nu2 as above;
\texttt{TWO\_LEVEL\_CHECK} A10 2.25 (CH4 nu4, labeled as the nu4 level alone) and
14.69 (NH3). The method and the published comparisons are in the comments.
How the earlier table was produced is not recorded, so the origin of its
lower values is not identified. The non-LTE efficiency eps = C10/(C10 + A10)
falls by (C10 + A\_old)/(C10 + A\_new): estimated with He 0.35 and H2 0.6 of
the particles at 415 and 645 K, 0.1-1.2\% at 1e-5 to 2e-6 bar (C10/A10 8.7-97)
and 4.5-13\% at 1e-7 bar (C10/A10 0.4-1.0). The change to T\_match of the stored
closure states was NOT measured (it needs the Photochem closure re-run);
\texttt{conducted\_heat\_column.txt} files of earlier states carry the old values.
\texttt{md/AI\_stage1\_inventory\_20261006.md} section 4.1 quotes the old A10 (12.56,
1.77 s\^{}-1 at 296 K, "READ from code") and was not edited (another worker\textquotesingle s
file).

(c) The rotational-LTE argument compared the NH3 rotation-inversion A values
(\textless= 4.8 s\^{}-1) with the HITRAN pressure-broadening rate (1.4e4 s\^{}-1 at 1
microbar), as if broadening were rotational population relaxation. It is
not: broadening counts every phase-interrupting collision. The module header
and \texttt{ROTATIONAL\_LTE\_NOTE} now state that rotational LTE is an estimate, give
the critical density of about 5e10 cm\^{}-3 from NH3-H2 state-to-state rates of
about 1e-10 cm\^{}3 s\^{}-1 (Demes et al. 2023) against about 2e13 cm\^{}-3 at
1 microbar and 400 K, and point to \texttt{md/AI\_stage1\_inventory\_20261006.md}
section 4 for the excitation data now in hand. \texttt{ROTATIONAL\_LTE\_NOTE} is not
written to any output (grep).

\textbf{Tests.} \texttt{src/tests/conducted\_heat\_column/run.sh} (the only suite that imports the
module; \texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py} imports it but has no suite here):
77 PASS before and 77 PASS after (MEASURED). Values that move with the A10
tables, before -\textgreater{} after: the \texttt{nlte\_partial} warming of the test column
195.12 -\textgreater{} 198.56 K (+1.8\%); the water-analog T\_match 362.00 -\textgreater{} 364.75 K
(its reference bracket moves with it); the two-level-with-published-rates
margin below LTE 15.37 -\textgreater{} 17.09; the included-exchange record (nlte)
0.4655 -\textgreater{} 0.4599; the \texttt{lte} (151.99 K) and \texttt{rotational} (370.11 K) warmings
are unchanged, as they must be. \texttt{collisional\_validity.py} has no suite; it
was checked by the K values against section 6.4 of the derivation note and
by the before/after runs above. No Fortran file was touched; no build.

\subsection{111. The recomputed NH3/CH4 band coefficients barely move the EW-matched state (2026-10-07)}\label{111-the-recomputed-nh3ch4-band-coefficients-barely-move-the-ew-matched-state-2026-10-07}

The A10 tables of \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column\_balance.py} were recomputed from
HITRAN in section 110 (NH3 nu2 9-26\% and CH4 dyad 4-10\% higher than the old
tables). Every energy-coupled state before it was computed with the old
tables. MEASURED on the route-B EW-matched state He/H 0.65, s = 1.3
(\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/route\_xuv\_scaled/HeH0.65/xuv\_x1p3}), rerun with
the same closure configuration and binary (c37b3671), seeded from its
converged k01, only the tables changed
(\texttt{HeH0.65/xuv\_x1p3\_A10recomputed}, closure converged at k = 1, max residual
9.3e-5): T\_match 414.864 -\textgreater{} 416.048 K (+1.18 K), conducted flux into the
column 25.654 -\textgreater{} 25.627 erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1 (-0.1\%), elemental fluxes F\_H +0.010\%,
F\_He +0.020\%, log Mdot 8.240 unchanged; width-matched EW 1.1084 -\textgreater{} 1.1086 \%A
(+0.0002, against the 0.0058 numerical target), unbroadened EW 1.1463 -\textgreater{}
1.1465, kernel f 22.1985 -\textgreater{} 22.1902 km/s, red/blue 7.601 unchanged. The
earlier energy-coupled results (the He/H scans, the EUV match of sections
102 and 108) are therefore not recomputed; their numbers stand within the
quoted precision.

\subsection{112. The acceptance of the ionization sweep measures every kinetic row against the turnover of its element group; constraint rows are judged in their own units (item (AM), 2026-10-07)}\label{112-the-acceptance-of-the-ionization-sweep-measures-every-kinetic-row-against-the-turnover-of-its-element-group-constraint-rows-are-judged-in-their-own-units-item-am-2026-10-07}

Item (AM) of \texttt{md/TO\_BE\_DONE.md}; plan \texttt{md/PLAN\_20261006\_to\_be\_done\_AI\_AM\_rev3.md}
section 2; inventory \texttt{md/AM\_row\_layout\_inventory\_20261006.md}; interface
proposal \texttt{md/AM\_interface\_proposal\_20261006.md}, whose questions Q1-Q6 the
user approved on 2026-10-07. Two separately verified changes.

\subsubsection{112.1 What was wrong}\label{1121-what-was-wrong}

\texttt{normalized\_reaction\_residual} (\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}) judged the
atomic layouts against an upper bound of each row\textquotesingle s rates with every reactant
at the whole of its element (the Penning term put He 2\^{}3S at all of the
helium): on the LHS 1140 b atomic state \texttt{ew\_fid\_atomic\_nocond} the H+ scale
exceeded the gross H+ rate by 3.9e12 at the base and 2.1e2 at 26 R\_p, so a
1 per cent move of H+ was admitted inside about 1.35 R\_p (section 106.4).
Four further defects, from the inventory: \texttt{res\ =\ max(res,\ a)} drops a NaN row
(compiler- and order-dependently), so a state with a nonfinite balance row
could be accepted; a zero scale counted as 1, so a row with no turnover was
compared with 1e-6 in cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1; the atomic pinned rows (\texttt{x\ -\ x\_fix}) were
divided by a kinetic bound, against the comment of the function; in the
molecular layouts the gross rate of a balance that a constraint row replaced
(transported H+, carrier H2, the reservoir partition of a lower ghost) still
entered the hydrogen turnover the other rows were measured against.
\texttt{scale\_out} was 1 in the molecular branch. And the turnover aggregation
\texttt{element\_turnover\_reciprocal} had no layout argument: on the atomic triplet
layout it grouped He 2\^{}3S (row 4) with hydrogen.

\subsubsection{112.2 Change (a): the explicit row layout (no number moves)}\label{1122-change-a-the-explicit-row-layout-no-number-moves}

New module \texttt{src/modules/nonlinear\_system\_solver/chemical\_row\_layout.f90}
(in \texttt{SRC} of the Makefile): the type \texttt{chemical\_row\_layout\_of\_cell} (kind,
units, species slot, acceptance group and constraint tolerance of every row),
\texttt{ionization\_row\_layout} (the static layout from the switches; it stops on a
row count that does not match them, and checks that every row is grouped with
an element its species carries), \texttt{mark\_constraint\_rows\_of\_cell} (the
constraint rows of one cell from the same flags and element densities the
systems read), \texttt{nuclei\_of\_species} (the nuclear stoichiometry: HeH+ one H and
one He, OH one H and one O, H2O two H and one O, CO one C and one O; kept
apart from the grouping), \texttt{element\_group\_turnover\_reciprocal} (G\_E summed in
row order, a status for every row: of group, row without reaction, group
without reaction, not finite, constraint row; constraint rows optionally
excluded), \texttt{constraint\_row\_tol\ =\ 1e-6}, \texttt{absent\_element\_density\ =\ 1e-30}
(now the one definition read by \texttt{metal\_rows}, \texttt{System\_HeH\_metals},
\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol\_metals}, \texttt{constrained\_chemical\_equilibrium} and
\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium}). \texttt{element\_turnover\_reciprocal} is removed; the
molecular solve scale (\texttt{set\_element\_turnover\_scales}) is taken through
\texttt{turnover\_or\_bound\_reciprocal} (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol.f90}): every row counted, the
bound where the status is not "of group", which reproduces the old grouping
and fallback exactly for finite rates (a gross rate of +Inf, which used to
give a reciprocal of 0, now gives the bound).

Verification of (a): \texttt{src/tests/acceptance\_scale} PIN 36/36 PASS (FUTURE 20 of
30 still failing, the 6 grouping and 1 status assertions now passing); every
suite under \texttt{src/tests} as before (55 suites; the pre-existing failures
\texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 3 stationary-evaluate assertions, \texttt{steady\_completion\_flag}
and \texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual} needing run logs, unchanged). The
molecular regression cases run on scratch copies with the control build and
the (a) build (gfortran, single thread): \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, \texttt{mol\_metals},
\texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner}, \texttt{mol\_diffusion}, \texttt{mol\_ir\_bands}, \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion},
\texttt{mol\_carrier}, \texttt{lower\_profile}, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry}, \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed},
\texttt{hp\_trace\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_front}: Hydro\_ioniz, Ion\_species and both \texttt{\_adv} files
BITWISE identical, and the acceptance record of every cell identical (MEASURED).

\subsubsection{112.3 Change (b): the acceptance correction}\label{1123-change-b-the-acceptance-correction}

\texttt{normalized\_reaction\_residual} (signature: the unused \texttt{n\_e\_ref} argument is
removed; optional \texttt{refusal\_reason}, \texttt{turnover\_share\_out}, \texttt{own\_row\_out}
added) evaluates the system the cell solve used at the candidate, the
molecular ones as volume rates, and reads the gross rate of every row from
the new threadprivate \texttt{ionization\_row\_gross} (\texttt{ion\_residual\_core.f90}), which
every system now fills from the same factors as its rows: \texttt{heh\_rows} and
\texttt{heh\_tr\_rows} gained a \texttt{gross} output (He 2\^{}3S row from
\texttt{tr\_triplet\_row\_channels}, as the molecular systems form it), \texttt{System\_H}
writes its one row\textquotesingle s, and the molecular systems\textquotesingle{} \texttt{mol\_row\_gross} became this
array. The atomic solves stay unscaled and the molecular solve scale is
unchanged (user decision Q3). Then, with the layout and the cell\textquotesingle s
constraint rows:

\begin{verbatim}
kinetic row i of group E:  m_i = |f_i| / G_E,  G_E = sum of P + L over
                           the KINETIC rows of E at the candidate
constraint row:            m_i = |f_i| ieq_res_tol / constraint_row_tol
group with G_E = 0:        m_i = 0 if f_i = 0, else refused (huge(),
                           refusal_reason 2, class 6)
nonfinite f_i or G_E:      the measure is NaN (refusal_reason 1)
\end{verbatim}

and the maximum is taken after both tests. Atomic groups: H+ alone; He rows
2, 3 and He 2\^{}3S; each metal its two rows. \texttt{scale\_out} is the group turnover
(the constraint tolerance ratio, 1, for a constraint row), which changes the
scale column of \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_DUMP\_CELL} (user decision Q6). Certification
entries "eliminated-species closure" and "level balance He 2\^{}3S" name the new
scale. New measurement switch \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_CLASS\_RECORD}: after every sweep
\texttt{output/ieq\_acceptance\_classes.txt} holds each cell\textquotesingle s class, residual, and row
by row the turnover share and the row against its own gross rate.

Tests: \texttt{acceptance\_scale} PIN 45/45 and FUTURE 47/47 PASS (the 27 FUTURE
assertions that failed on the old source, now passing; new assertions:
H only, H/He with charge exchange, H/He + metals with an absent and a
two-stage element, the H+ row kinetic and pinned at one candidate, a helium
group without turnover and without imbalance, the stoichiometry and the slot
numbering against the continuation\textquotesingle s). The new assertions call the new
interface and cannot be compiled against the old one; their failure on the
old source is the 27 FUTURE assertions\textquotesingle{} (same defects). All 56 suites as
after (a) except \texttt{run\_mode} (see 112.5).

\subsubsection{112.4 Measurement (control build against the (b) build)}\label{1124-measurement-control-build-against-the-b-build}

Scratch copies of every case of \texttt{DEFAULT\_CASES}, the control build (the tree
before (a) plus the class record) against the (b) build, gfortran, one
thread, \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_CLASS\_RECORD=1} (\texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/am\_eval/};
\texttt{iontrans\_metals} needs \texttt{stage\_enth} named on its "Restart option change:"
line to start at all, in both builds, see 112.7). Outputs: Hydro\_ioniz,
Ion\_species and the \texttt{\_adv} pair. Classes: the last sweep cell by cell, and
the run-wide ledger (MEASURED).
