LHS 1140 b, stationary evaluate of the stored state (scratch copies): the
atomic \texttt{ew\_fid\_atomic\_nocond} 504 class-1 cells before and after (one
sweep); the stored pair\textquotesingle s "eliminated-species closure" measure 2.2e-10 -\textgreater{}
9.6e-7 (tolerance 1e-6, at cell 258), after the sweep 1.1e-15; the molecular
\texttt{...\_HeH2.09\_ref\_merged01} and \texttt{...\_cond\_iontrans01} 1512 class-1
acceptances (three sweeps) before and after. The certification verdict of
these stored pairs is NOT REPRODUCED with the present tree in both builds
(the hydrodynamic energy row, 1.48e-6 against 1e-6 on the atomic state), a
change of the tree since those states were certified and not of this item
(the ifx build of (b) gives the same).

\subsubsection{112.5 Newly refused states, and what they are}\label{1125-newly-refused-states-and-what-they-are}

Every newly refused state was checked: its scales are finite (no case
logged a class-6 refusal, "CANDIDATE REFUSED", which a NaN or a zero-turnover
refusal would end in; every refused state is class 4 with a finite measure
at or below 1), its constraint rows are the
flags the system imposed, and its measure was compared with the rounding of
the row (eps times its gross rate, \textasciitilde1e-16).

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Molecular layouts with a transported carrier} (\texttt{hp\_*},
  \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry}, the lower ghosts of every handoff case). The refused
  states are dominated by the H3+ row (MEASURED with the class record taken
  every sweep: hp\_trace\_seed, 983 of 986 refused records; oxygen\_chemistry,
  65 of 91), out of balance by 1e-5 to 3.5e-2 of its OWN gross rate while it
  carries 1 to 50 per cent of the hydrogen KINETIC turnover once the pinned
  H+ and H2 balances are left out. These are real non-roots under the stated
  semantics (the imbalance is 1e11 times the rounding of the row); the old
  measure hid them by counting the replaced H+/H2 balances, which dominate
  the hydrogen turnover. Their cause is the solve, not the judgment: the held
  molecular solve scale (unchanged, Q3) still divides the H3+ row by a
  turnover that includes the pinned balances, so hybrd1 stops at its step
  tolerance (info 1) with that row underweighted. Excluding the constraint
  rows from the solve scale as well is the measured change that would
  address it (open, 112.7).
\item
  \textbf{A column without ionizing radiation} (\texttt{hydrostatic\_column}, H/He,
  T 1140 K). The H+ row of cells above r = 1.0097 R\_p is refused with
  measures 1.4e-6 to 1.0: x(H+) is \textasciitilde1e-43 of the hydrogen, the only reactions
  are collisional ionization (b \textasciitilde{} exp(-138)) and recombination, and the
  state sits above the root, so the row is all recombination and its imbalance
  is its whole turnover. By the row\textquotesingle s own rounding it is a real non-root; in
  the unknowns it is not resolvable (the Newton step to the root is of the
  order of x, 1e-43, against the 1e-16 resolution of a fraction of order
  one). The measure has no absolute floor, by design point 4 of the plan, so
  such a state is refused. Consequence (MEASURED): the \texttt{run\_mode} suite\textquotesingle s
  physical run of this column (\texttt{Run\ mode:\ phys}, which refuses a step with any
  class-4 cell) now exhausts its step retries at step 0, and 14 of its
  assertions fail that passed after (a). Whether the acceptance should accept
  a row whose distance to its root is below the resolution of the unknowns is
  a decision for the user (112.7); it is not taken here.
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection{112.6 Q5: slow rows inside fast groups (measurement and decision)}\label{1126-q5-slow-rows-inside-fast-groups-measurement-and-decision}

Rule (proposal section 4, values approved 2026-10-07): for a kinetic row with
share s\_i = (P\_i + L\_i)/G\_E the group norm admits a relative error of its
species up to delta\_adm \textasciitilde{} 1e-6/s\_i; where delta\_adm \textgreater{} delta\_req = 1e-3 in a
cell in which the species carries at least w\_min = 1e-2 of the energy term or
observable it feeds, a row criterion \textbar f\_i\textbar/(P\_i + L\_i) \textless= 1e-3 is to be added
for that species in those cells. MEASURED on the accepted (last-sweep) states
of \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, \texttt{mol\_metals}, \texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner}, \texttt{mol\_diffusion},
\texttt{mol\_ir\_bands}, \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion}, \texttt{mol\_carrier}, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry},
\texttt{hp\_front} and the LHS 1140 b states \texttt{ref\_merged01} and \texttt{cond\_iontrans01}
(script \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/am\_eval/ana/q5.py}; weights: H3+ and H2
their IR cooling share, He++ and the molecular ions their share of n\_e, He
2\^{}3S the share of its column the cell carries, the metal stages the larger of
their cooling and n\_e shares):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  slow rows are common: shares down to 4e-12 (He++), 2e-11 (H3+), 6e-9 (He
  2\^{}3S), 9e-7 (H2), 1e-9 (HeH+), 1e-8 (O III on LHS);
\item
  at every accepted state the own-row residual of every kinetic row is at or
  below 7e-13 (in the cells above, 4e-14 or less): no accepted state carries a
  masked imbalance;
\item
  the trigger of the rule is met for H3+ only: in \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry}, 44
  cells (r = 1.017-1.040 R\_p) where H3+ carries 1 to 100 per cent of the
  cooling, delta\_adm up to 0.36; in \texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner}, 5 cells (r =
  1.078-1.083) with up to 12 per cent of the cooling, delta\_adm up to 3.1e-3.
  No other species, case or LHS state meets it (He 2\^{}3S: the cells with
  delta\_adm \textgreater{} 1e-3 carry at most 1.6e-7 of the column).
\end{itemize}

Decision: the group norm alone is adopted for every species except H3+. For
H3+ the rule calls for the row criterion in cells where its cooling is
material, and it is NOT implemented here: the materiality test needs the H3+
cooling share of the candidate inside the cell acceptance, which the
acceptance does not form, and how to form it (which cooling, at which
composition, or a materiality proxy such as the H3+ share of the hydrogen
nuclei) is a design choice the measurement does not settle. It is recorded
as open (112.7) with these numbers; no accepted state of the measured cases
is affected (own-row residual of H3+ there \textless= 4e-14).

\subsubsection{112.7 Open}\label{1127-open}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  The molecular solve scale and the transported carriers (112.5 item 1): the
  measured change Q3 left for later.
\item
  A resolution floor for rows whose root is below the resolution of the
  unknowns (112.5 item 2); \texttt{run\_mode} fails until it is decided.
\item
  The H3+ row criterion where its cooling is material (112.6).
\item
  A charge exchange term on the identity row of a two-stage element\textquotesingle s X++:
  \texttt{cx\_add\_to\_fvec} adds the X++ charge exchange (a volume rate) to that
  dimensionless row when x(X++) is nonzero (acceptance\_scale RECORD: x = 1e-3
  gives f = -2.4e3 for oxygen); the root x = 0 is unchanged, the units of
  the row are mixed. Reported, not changed.
\item
  \texttt{backup/regression/iontrans\_metals} refuses to start with the present tree
  (its IC predates the stage enthalpy flux token \texttt{stage\_enth}); its input
  needs \texttt{stage\_enth} on the "Restart option change:" line, or a new IC.
\item
  \texttt{CCE} keeps its own species-slot numbering (\texttt{is\_*}); the test asserts
  that it agrees with \texttt{chemical\_row\_layout}\textquotesingle s, and moving it onto the module
  is a separate change.
\end{enumerate}

Frozen binary (ifx 2026.1, a copy of the tree with (a) and (b)):
\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_15440b7c.x}, manifest
\texttt{BINARY\_MANIFEST\_15440b7c0a0b.txt}. Documentation:
\texttt{docs/physics\_overview/ionization.tex} (paragraph "The acceptance measure",
Eq. eq:ion:acceptance; the normalization paragraph; class 6 for a NaN),
\texttt{steady\_state.tex} (the chemistry gate), the user manual
(\texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_CLASS\_RECORD}, the scale of \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_DUMP\_CELL}).

\subsection{113. The carrier source runs the oxygen cycle only with the oxygen chemistry on (2026-10-07)}\label{113-the-carrier-source-runs-the-oxygen-cycle-only-with-the-oxygen-chemistry-on-2026-10-07}

Found by the (AN) diagnosis (\texttt{md/AN\_matching\_surface\_diagnosis\_20261007.md},
summary item 1); fixed as a change separate from section 112 and measured on
its own (control: the build of section 112).

\textbf{Defect.} \texttt{carrier\_source} (\texttt{src/modules/lower\_atmosphere/diffusive\_photochemistry.f90})
called \texttt{oxygen\_carrier\_rows} inside \texttt{if\ (thereis\_mol)} with no
\texttt{thereis\_oxychem} guard; the two other callers (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol\_metals},
\texttt{constrained\_chemical\_equilibrium}) are guarded. With the oxygen chemistry
off, OH and H2O are not carried (zero), so of the oxygen cycle only the loss
O + H2 -\textgreater{} OH + H (k2 n\_O n\_H2) acted, on the H2 row, with no product carried,
no reverse and no reaction heat: the free oxygen broke H2 at no energy cost.
On the LHS 1140 b energy-coupled states that term was 72-95 per cent of the
column H2 destruction, and the H + H + M recombination of the atoms it freed
63-69 per cent of the wind\textquotesingle s heating (READ, the (AN) diagnosis).

\textbf{Fix.} The call is made only when \texttt{thereis\_oxychem}; otherwise rows 9 and
10 (OH, H2O) and the oxygen terms of the H2 row stay at the zero they are
initialized to, so \texttt{src(ic\_OH)}, \texttt{src(ic\_H2O)} and \texttt{pH2ox}, \texttt{lH2ox} are zero
(no uninitialized read: \texttt{fv}, the p/l terms and \texttt{src} are zeroed before the
branch). \texttt{set\_oxygen\_coeffs} is still called (its coefficients are then not
read).

\textbf{Test.} \texttt{src/tests/carrier\_constraint\_attribution}, assertion block (4):
with the oxygen chemistry off and no OH/H2O carried, the H2 source of a cell
must not depend on the free oxygen, and the OH and H2O sources are zero.
RED before the fix (MEASURED): H2 source -6.848e7 cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1 with the free
oxygen 4.8e10 cm\^{}-3 against -50.21 without it, OH + H2O sources 6.85e7;
GREEN after: -50.21 both ways, OH + H2O 0. The carrier suites
(\texttt{carrier\_boundary\_jacobian}, \texttt{carrier\_constraint\_attribution},
\texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_flux}, \texttt{carrier\_helium\_inventory}, \texttt{carrier\_outer\_boundary},
\texttt{carrier\_reference\_scales}, \texttt{carrier\_retry},
\texttt{carrier\_returned\_state\_acceptance}), \texttt{stage\_row\_balance},
\texttt{ionization\_stage\_flux}, \texttt{helium\_metastable\_transport},
\texttt{ghost\_reservoir\_chemistry} and \texttt{acceptance\_scale} pass.

\textbf{Regression cases it reaches.} The carrier operator with molecular
chemistry and the oxygen chemistry off: \texttt{mol\_carrier}, \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed},
\texttt{hp\_trace\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_front} (molecular carrier transport). All four carry no
metals (no \texttt{metals.inp}), so their free oxygen is zero and the removed term
was zero: outputs BITWISE identical to the section-112 build, and the
acceptance record identical (MEASURED). \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} (oxygen chemistry
on, the guarded call unchanged): bitwise. No other default case runs the
molecular carrier operator.

\textbf{Where it acts: the LHS 1140 b molecular states} (metals from the lower
profile, oxygen chemistry off). Stationary evaluate of the stored
\texttt{...\_HeH2.09\_ref\_merged01} state: the carrier balance H2 measure
2.2e-7 (within 1e-5) with the section-112 build against 8.4e-3 (ABOVE, at
cell 117; 2.3e-5 at the wind cell 217) with this one; on
\texttt{...\_cond\_iontrans01}, 1.8e-3 -\textgreater{} 8.4e-3. Every LHS molecular state solved
with the defect is therefore not a stationary state of the corrected
equations and has to be recomputed; the effect on the base temperature and
composition is the one the (AN) scratch run estimated (first cell x\_H
0.58 -\textgreater{} 0.13, 575 -\textgreater{} 387 K, READ). Not rerun here.

Frozen binary (ifx 2026.1, the tree of section 112 plus this fix):
\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_17c04ea2.x}, manifest
\texttt{BINARY\_MANIFEST\_17c04ea22cea.txt} (differs from \texttt{EXHALE\_15440b7c.x} in
\texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90} alone).

\subsection{115. The element operator settles on the pressure gradient of the mixture, and its non-resonant ion-neutral pairs are the polarization form alone (2026-10-07)}\label{115-the-element-operator-settles-on-the-pressure-gradient-of-the-mixture-and-its-non-resonant-ion-neutral-pairs-are-the-polarization-form-alone-2026-10-07}

Both defects were found by the (AK) derivation note
(\texttt{md/AK\_ambipolar\_stage\_drift\_derivation\_20261006.md}, sections 2.1 and 6.3);
user decision 2026-10-07: fix both, no option key, the results change.

\textbf{1. Defect: settling on gravity instead of the pressure gradient.} Each
species obeys n\_s m\_s Du\_s/Dt = -grad p\_s + n\_s m\_s G + n\_s e\_s E + friction
(Schunk 1977, RvGSP 15, 429, eq. 24b). With the drift inertia neglected the
sum over all species is the mixture equation rho Du/Dt = -grad p + rho G, and
eliminating the bulk acceleration leaves the force on species s against the
mixture as -grad p\_s + (rho\_s/rho) grad p + n\_s e\_s E (note eq. 2.1): gravity
cancels, and the settling acceleration is g\_eff = -(1/rho) dp/dr = g + Du/Dt.
The code used g (\texttt{Dphi}) alone in \texttt{settling\_coefficient}, in the trace-metal
coefficients and in the photochemical carrier settling, i.e. it left out the
acceleration of the wind (MEASURED on the LHS 1140 b atomic state:
v dv/dr up to 0.050 g at 18.8 R\_p; O(1) through a sonic point).

\textbf{Fix.} New \texttt{mixture\_pressure\_acceleration} (\texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90})
forms g\_eff = -(p/rho) dln p/dr from the state\textquotesingle s own total pressure
p = (n\_tot + n\_e) k T, the particle count being the equation of state\textquotesingle s
(\texttt{gas\_particle\_density}, new in \texttt{composition.f90}, which shares one routine,
\texttt{electron\_and\_particle\_densities}, with \texttt{get\_species\_densities}, so the two
cannot count particles differently; the existing path is arithmetically
unchanged), by the centred logarithmic difference over the two neighbours,
the same stencil and form as the ambipolar field eE = -kT dln(n\_e T)/dr. An
isothermal barometric column returns g exactly, and in a column of uniform
composition eE = mbar m\_H g\_eff, so the ambipolar limits of the relative
settling mass (now normalized to g\_eff) hold whatever the density profile.
Every place the settling force is formed now uses it:
\texttt{settling\_coefficient} (element step, fixed-wind relaxation, stationary
residual \texttt{element\_transport\_residual}, \texttt{helium\_diffusive\_face\_flux} and with
it \texttt{post\_process\_adv}, the element flux profile of the certification, the
base flux record), \texttt{trace\_element\_transport\_coefficients} (one \texttt{geff}
argument, from the same \texttt{settling\_coefficient} call) and the carrier
settling of \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90} (\texttt{carrier\_face\_coefficients};
\texttt{carrier\_geometry} no longer returns a gravity). The JFNK Jacobian of the
element rows is a finite difference of the residual, and g\_eff reaches the
same neighbours j +- 1 as eE, so the stencil is unchanged. \texttt{grav\_func} is no
longer used by either module. Viscous stress is not part of this
first-order closure and is left out of the mixture balance (stated in the
module header).

\textbf{2. Defect: rigid core added to the non-resonant ion-neutral friction.}
\texttt{ion\_neutral\_pair\_diffusion} combined the induced-dipole coefficient with a
rigid sphere of the neutral-neutral diameter, 1/D = 1/D\_pol + 1/D\_hs. Schunk \&
Nagy (1980, RvGSP 18, 813) use the polarization form alone for every
non-resonant pair (their eq. 17, after Dalgarno et al. 1958, tabulated in
Table 6, both checked on the rendered page of the published article), and the
quantum-mechanical H+/He coefficient of Kimura et al. (1993, ApJ 405, 801,
Table 2) is 1.05-1.17e-9 cm\^{}3/s against the code\textquotesingle s 3.2-7.4e-9.

\textbf{Fix.} \texttt{ion\_neutral\_pair\_diffusion} is removed; \texttt{stage\_pair\_diffusion}
calls \texttt{polarization\_pair\_diffusion(TK,\ ntot,\ alpha\_n,\ Z\_ion,\ mu\_g)},
D = kT/(2.21 pi Z e n (alpha\_n mu)\^{}1/2), the ion charge entering the
induced-dipole potential as Z\^{}2 e\^{}2 (so He++ + H and the doubly charged
metal stages against H carry twice the singly charged friction; before, the
charge was absent). Every non-resonant pair of the operator changes: H+/He,
H2+/He, H3+/He, He+/H, He++/H, He+/H2, He++/H2 and every metal stage
against H and H2. Resonant pairs never enter the element operator (both
partners carry one element). The Python twin
\texttt{src/utils/collisional\_validity.py} is changed the same way (its
\texttt{pair\_diffusion} reproduces the Fortran K(H+,He) = 1.3057e-9 cm\^{}3/s,
MEASURED). Against Kimura et al. (1993) the polarization form is 1.243
(1000 K), 1.232 (5000 K) and 1.116 (1e4 K) times the published value
(MEASURED, test T12e); that 12-24\% residual is not explained by the published
sources, which do not define the thermal average of the table (note
section 6.3), and is stated at the code site.

\textbf{Tests} (\texttt{src/tests/diffusion\_tests.f90}, run against the old operator with
only the accessors added, and against the new one; MEASURED):

Fixtures that assumed the old physics were corrected, not loosened: a
column whose pressure falls with g = 0 is not at rest, and its settling is
now the physical drive, so every "column at rest" or "flat column" of the
element suites is now built at uniform pressure (new helper
\texttt{density\_of\_uniform\_pressure} in \texttt{src/tests/test\_columns.f90}; T6, T7d,
\texttt{element\_operator} (two rows), \texttt{steady\_species\_rows}, \texttt{ionization\_stage\_flux},
\texttt{certification} (\texttt{column\_background}, rho T = 1)), and the T1a/T1b/T9-front
columns are hydrostatic for their own composition
(\texttt{impose\_pressure\_profile}). T9 normalizes the relative settling mass to
g\_eff and types its targets from the column\textquotesingle s own mean particle mass; T7c
types G with the column\textquotesingle s g\_eff; T14 drives the helium outward with a column
whose pressure rises outward, because a closed hydrostatic column at this
inventory stops near X = 0.8 (MEASURED) and never reaches the bound. The
whole driver: 39 passed, 0 failed (old operator: 30 passed, 9 failed).

Suites that link the changed modules (all 56 \texttt{src/tests/*/run.sh}), run with
the new build and with the control build of the entry text: every suite
passes except \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_flux} (3 rows), \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} (the
stationary-evaluate rows), \texttt{run\_mode}, \texttt{steady\_completion\_flag} and
\texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual}, whose FAIL rows are identical with the
control build {[}CORRECTED 2026-10-10, section 137: the three \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_flux}
rows were NOT pre-existing; they appeared with the real g\_eff of this section,
which the test\textquotesingle s reference did not follow{]} (pre-existing; the last two need \texttt{EXHALE\_STEADY\_RUNLOGS},
\texttt{run\_mode} is the (AM) resolution-floor item). \texttt{certification},
\texttt{element\_operator}, \texttt{ionization\_stage\_flux} and \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} failed
on their flat-column fixtures before those were rebuilt at uniform pressure
(above) and pass after.

\textbf{Regression cases it reaches} (scratch copies, control build of the entry
text against the new build, both gfortran, single thread):

The cases with \texttt{He\_diffusion:\ True} (the matrix cases \texttt{mol\_diffusion} and
\texttt{lower\_profile}, the latter with \texttt{He\_metal\_diffusion} too, and the He/H
ladder \texttt{heh\_0p3}, \texttt{heh\_3}, \texttt{heh\_10}, \texttt{heh\_30}, \texttt{heh\_1\_12k}, \texttt{heh\_1\_lw\_12k})

correction alone attributes nearly all of it to item 2 (He/H +0.67\% at

beyond 1.1 R\_p g\_eff/g - 1 equals v dv/dr / g of the state to 1.5e-3

\subsection{116. The carried ionization stages are scaled by their gross channels; the molecular solve scale groups as the acceptance does; a resolution floor and an H3+ own-row criterion in the acceptance (item (AO) D1-D4, 2026-10-07)}\label{116-the-carried-ionization-stages-are-scaled-by-their-gross-channels-the-molecular-solve-scale-groups-as-the-acceptance-does-a-resolution-floor-and-an-h3-own-row-criterion-in-the-acceptance-item-ao-d1-d4-2026-10-07}

Item (AO) of \texttt{md/TO\_BE\_DONE.md}, user decisions of 2026-10-07: D1, D2 and D3
as recommended, D4 a defect fix. Four changes, each measured against the
build before it: control = the source of section 115, then D4, D4 + D1,
D4 + D1 + D2 and D4 + D1 + D2 + D3 (gfortran 16.2, one thread, scratch
copies of the regression cases with \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_CLASS\_RECORD=1}, in
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/ao/}). Every number is MEASURED unless marked
READ. No golden was refreshed.

\subsubsection{116.1 D4 (defect): the carried proton and helium stages carry their gross production and loss}\label{1161-d4-defect-the-carried-proton-and-helium-stages-carry-their-gross-production-and-loss}

\textbf{Defect.} The physical scale of a carrier row (\texttt{row\_terms\_phys},
\texttt{carrier\_residual}), which the carrier acceptance and the certification
entries "carrier balance ..." divide by, takes the row\textquotesingle s production plus loss
from \texttt{carrier\_source}. For the two ionized helium stages (molecular and
atomic gas) and the proton of an atomic gas, \texttt{carrier\_source} handed out the
positive and negative parts of the NET source, under a comment saying that
the two magnitudes "are not available". That was false: \texttt{mol\_heh\_rows} and
\texttt{heh\_tr\_rows} both return the gross rate of every row (\texttt{gross}, from the same
factors as the rows, section 112), and \texttt{he\_h\_cx\_fvec} adds each charge
exchange reaction to the gross rate of every row it moves. Where a stage is
held by fast chemistry its net is many digits below its channels: He+ in the
molecular layer of the LHS 1140 b route-B He/H 0.65 state is made by
photoionization and destroyed by He+ + H2 -\textgreater{} H+ + H + He at about 330
cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1 each at 1.20 R\_p, net 6e-3, so the scale held the transport alone
and read a state balanced to 5e-8 of its channels as 4.2e-4 against 1e-5
(\texttt{md/heplus\_carrier\_certification\_20261007.md}). The molecular proton row
carried its He \textless-\textgreater{} H charge exchange as a net on one side as well.

\textbf{Fix} (\texttt{src/modules/lower\_atmosphere/diffusive\_photochemistry.f90},
\texttt{carrier\_source}, lines 5254-5760). \texttt{mol\_heh\_rows} (molecular gas) or
\texttt{heh\_tr\_rows} (atomic gas) and \texttt{he\_h\_cx\_fvec} now fill \texttt{stage\_row\_gross}.
Every term of rows (1)-(3) is sign-definite in the densities, so a row f with
gross rate G has production (G + f)/2 and loss (G - f)/2; the metal charge
exchange (\texttt{charge\_exchange\_stage\_sources}) is added with its own gross
production and loss on its own sides. The atomic rows are in the
boundary-flow basis (row (2) the summed He I balance, row (3) the He II -\textgreater{}
He III flow, src(He II) = -f2 - f3); each reaction touches He II once and
none is in both rows, so the gross rate of He II is G2 + G3. The proton row
takes the same form in both gases. src and the Newton\textquotesingle s own scale (\texttt{dsc}) are
unchanged, and sprod - sloss = src to the rounding of the two sums. The
comments of \texttt{carrier\_source}, \texttt{carrier\_residual} and
\texttt{carrier\_row\_terms\_write} are corrected. The He 2\^{}3S row already had this
form (section 94).

\textbf{Test.} \texttt{src/tests/helium\_metastable\_transport}, new assertions (4b): the
He II density is bisected to the zero of its own net source with the other
carried states held (He III and the 2\^{}3S level lowered so that the bracket
closes in both gases); there the production of He II must hold the
photoionization of the ground singlet, the loss of He II and the production
of He III the photoionization of He II (compared on the row\textquotesingle s own P + L), and
sprod - sloss = src must hold for H II, He II and He III. Control: 6 FAIL
(the three bounds in both gases; e.g. the He II production fell short of the
singlet photoionization by 3.0e14 of its own P + L in atomic gas); D4: 40 of
40 PASS.

\subsubsection{116.2 D1: the molecular solve scale sums the kinetic rows of each group}\label{1162-d1-the-molecular-solve-scale-sums-the-kinetic-rows-of-each-group}

\textbf{Change} (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol\_metals.f90}, \texttt{set\_element\_turnover\_scales},
lines 138-201; \texttt{System\_HeH\_mol.f90}, \texttt{turnover\_or\_bound\_reciprocal}, line
656). The held solve scale marks the cell\textquotesingle s constraint rows
(\texttt{mark\_constraint\_rows\_of\_cell}) and sums each acceptance group over its
kinetic rows, as the acceptance does (section 112). A constraint row keeps
the bound, which is never applied to it (the row is written x - x\_fix after
the scaling). Before, a pinned H+ or H2 balance, the reservoir partition of a
lower ghost and the carried helium and oxygen balances entered the turnover
the other rows of their element were divided by.

solve scale (\texttt{PIN\_molecular\_solve\_scale\_keeps\_pinned\_H+\_balance},
\texttt{...\_keeps\_reservoir\_H2\_balance}) became
\texttt{PIN\_molecular\_solve\_scale\_excludes\_pinned\_H+\_balance} and
\texttt{...\_excludes\_reservoir\_H2\_balance} (1/scale(4) with H+ pinned and 1/scale(1)
with the reservoir row equal the kinetic hydrogen turnover). D4 build: FAIL
(7.16e-7 against 1.42e-6 and 1.40e-6); D1: PASS.

\textbf{What it does not do.} Section 112.5 attributed the H3+ refusals of the
transported-carrier layouts to the solve scale. It is not their cause. With
every sweep recorded (scratch build whose class record appends), the 11993
refused records of \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed} after D1 are all H3+ rows (own-row
imbalance 2e-6 to 1.0, mean 0.9 per cent) in which H3+ now carries 5 to 56
per cent of the hydrogen kinetic turnover. With the tolerance of the cell
solve lowered from sqrt(eps) = 1.5e-8 to 1e-12 (\texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_TOL=1e-12}) the
same run has no non-root at all, and needs fewer hybrd1 calls (101304
against 126816): hybrd1 stops at its relative step test, taken on the scaled
norm of all the unknowns, which the O(1) fractions dominate, before the trace
unknown x(H3+) is resolved. The same cause makes two assertions fail after
D1 (and not before): \texttt{ghost\_reservoir\_chemistry}
\texttt{ghost\_root\_inside\_every\_element\_budget} (the ghost root ends at x(He++) =
-1.6e-15, rows within 2.0e-7; with the solve tolerance 1e-12 in a scratch copy
of the test, x(He++) = +4.5e-15 and rows of 1.4e-13), and \texttt{grid\_and\_gates}
\texttt{restart\_lower\_ghost\_is\_the\_derived\_boundary} (the lower ghost rows of two
round trips 1.32e-14 apart against 1e-14; 0 with the D4 build; 4.3e-16 with
the D1 build and \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_TOL=1e-12}). Not changed here: the solve
tolerance is a path of every cell solve of every case (116.6, item 1).

\subsubsection{116.3 D2: the resolution floor of the acceptance}\label{1163-d2-the-resolution-floor-of-the-acceptance}

\textbf{Change} (\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, \texttt{normalized\_reaction\_residual},
header and lines 4803-4842, internal \texttt{rows\_as\_volume\_rates} 4879-4915;
\texttt{chemical\_row\_layout.f90}, \texttt{fraction\_resolution} line 138). A kinetic row
whose measure is above \texttt{ieq\_res\_tol} (in a group with turnover, finite) is
accepted when the remaining imbalance is below what the composition
resolves: its Newton correction along its own unknown, dx\_i = \textbar f\_i\textbar{} /
\textbar df\_i/dx\_i\textbar{} (forward difference of the same system as volume rates, step
sqrt(eps)\textbar x\_i\textbar), is at most \texttt{fraction\_resolution} = epsilon(1) = 2.2e-16, the
spacing of the floating-point numbers at the element total (every unknown is
a fraction of the nuclei of an element, and a smaller change moves none of
the element\textquotesingle s sums: the neutral 1 - sum x, the electrons; a correction below
eps \textbar x\_i\textbar, below the resolution of x\_i itself, is a special case of it), or
the species and its root both lie below \texttt{absent\_element\_density} in nuclei,
(\textbar x\_i\textbar{} + dx\_i) n\_E \textless= 1e-30 cm\^{}-3. The row\textquotesingle s measure is then 0, and the new
optional output \texttt{resolution\_floor\_out} marks it. The form "below the
resolution of x\_i itself" alone cannot accept the case the item names: on
\texttt{hydrostatic\_column} the refused H+ rows have dx/x = 1e-6 to 1e-3 (their
imbalance over their own gross rate), with x(H+) \textless= 4e-17 (MEASURED from the
class record and the state of the D1 run).

\textbf{Test.} \texttt{src/tests/acceptance\_scale}, section (vii-a): an H-only cell
without radiation at 1140 K and n\_H 4.7e13 (the column of
\texttt{hydrostatic\_column}), root x* = 2.5e-48: at x = 1e-20 the row is all
recombination and its correction is x/2; FUTURE: accepted and floored (D1
build: measure 1.0, FAIL; D2: 0, PASS). At x = 1e-3 the correction is 5e-4:
PIN, still refused. An element at n\_H = 1e-31 cm\^{}-3: FUTURE, accepted as
absent (D1: FAIL; D2: PASS). The new keyword cannot compile against the D1
source, so the red run used the same assertions without it.

\textbf{Measurement} (D1 build -\textgreater{} D2 build; \texttt{wasp\_full} and \texttt{wasp\_he23off} end
with exit status 2, not certified, in every build, as their stored runs do):

\texttt{the\_handoff\_clock\_starts\_at\_zero}) load a 10-step initialization snapshot of

\textbf{Found while measuring (not changed).} Seventy accepted cells of
\texttt{hydrostatic\_column} (142 in the control) carry a NEGATIVE H+ density,
n(H+) = -8.9e-2 cm\^{}-3 at r = 1.31 R\_p, x(H+) = -8.0e-12: the mirror root
x = -x* of the H+ row, whose electrons are its own protons (n\_e = n(H+)), so
that f(-x*) = 0 with a negative electron density. The admissibility test
\texttt{ionization\_fractions\_physical} accepts negative fractions down to -1e-10
(\texttt{ftol}), so this unphysical root passes as class 1 (measure 2.9e-7). The
pre-112 golden has none. 116.6, item 3.

\subsubsection{116.4 D3: H3+ against its own gross rate}\label{1164-d3-h3-against-its-own-gross-rate}

\textbf{Change} (\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, \texttt{normalized\_reaction\_residual},
lines 4790-4801; \texttt{chemical\_row\_layout.f90}, \texttt{h3p\_row\_balance\_tol} line 148).
In the molecular layouts the H3+ row\textquotesingle s measure is the larger of its group
measure and (\textbar f\textbar/(P + L)) ieq\_res\_tol / h3p\_row\_balance\_tol, so it passes
only when \textbar f\textbar{} \textless= 1e-6 G\_H and \textbar f\textbar{} \textless= 1e-3 (P + L) (section 112.6: the one
species the group norm masks where its cooling is material). It applies in
every cell: the materiality test of 112.6 needs a cooling share inside the
cell acceptance, which it does not form, and a cell where H3+ is too scarce
to matter has a correction below the resolution floor of 116.3 or a measure
far below 1e-3 at its root.

\textbf{Test.} \texttt{src/tests/acceptance\_scale}, section (vii-b): at the root of the
molecular cell of (iii), where H3+ carries 3.7e-8 of the hydrogen turnover, a
10 per cent move of H3+: FUTURE, refused (D2 build: measure 1.7e-9, admitted,
FAIL; D3: 4.8e-5, PASS); a 1e-4 move: PIN, admitted (5.0e-8).

\textbf{Measurement} (D2 build -\textgreater{} D3 build): \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} class 4 75 -\textgreater{} 139
(the added records at steps 241-316, cells up to 128, the transient after

outputs and classes BITWISE identical.

\subsubsection{116.6 Open}\label{1166-open}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  The tolerance of the cell solve. hybrd1 (molecular) stops at a relative
  step of sqrt(eps) on the scaled norm of all unknowns, and the atomic
  \texttt{newton\_dense} at an absolute step of 1e-11; both stop before a trace
  unknown (H3+ at 1e-6, He++ at 1e-15, H+ at 1e-14 of its element) is
  resolved, and the acceptance then refuses the state (116.2, 116.3). With
  \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_TOL=1e-12} \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed} has no non-root and the two
  assertions of 116.2 pass. A change of every cell solve\textquotesingle s path; for the
  user.
\item
  \texttt{run\_mode} rows 2: the 10-step initialization snapshot it hands to a
  physical run is not a root of the chemistry (116.3); either item 1 or a
  longer relaxation of the fixture (20 steps hand off) settles it.
\item
  Negative ionization fractions accepted down to -1e-10
  (\texttt{ionization\_fractions\_physical}, \texttt{ftol}): the mirror root x = -x* of a
  row whose electrons are its own ions passes as a root (116.3). A negative
  density is never physical; the admissible bound belongs at the resolution
  of the fraction.
\item
  D5 of (AO) (the phase5 He/H 0.65 helium-depleted outer wind) and the LHS
  1140 b reruns with the frozen binary.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{118. The element simplex of the ionization acceptance is bounded at the resolution of a fraction; the mirror root is refused, a candidate outside the simplex is projected and judged again, and the atomic sweep re-solves a cell without a root (item (AO) (iii), 2026-10-08)}\label{118-the-element-simplex-of-the-ionization-acceptance-is-bounded-at-the-resolution-of-a-fraction-the-mirror-root-is-refused-a-candidate-outside-the-simplex-is-projected-and-judged-again-and-the-atomic-sweep-re-solves-a-cell-without-a-root-item-ao-iii-2026-10-08}

Item (iii) of 116.6. Measured against the source of section 116 (control:
gfortran 16.2 build of that source, md5 \texttt{b23415a1}, the same binary as the D3
build of section 116, so its regression runs in
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/ao/reg\_d3/} are the control; measured runs in
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/neg/}; one thread, \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_CLASS\_RECORD=1}).
Every number is MEASURED unless marked READ. No golden was refreshed.

\subsubsection{118.1 The defect}\label{1181-the-defect}

\textbf{A negative density was admitted as a root.} \texttt{ionization\_fractions\_physical}
(\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}), the test that a candidate lies in the element
simplex, admitted every fraction down to -1e-10 and every element\textquotesingle s tracked
species up to 1 + 1e-10 (\texttt{ftol}). The band was introduced on 2026-08-11 as a
round-off band (READ: \texttt{md/hd209\_metal\_stagnation.md} section 5, where a band
at the solver\textquotesingle s xtol = 1.5e-8 was tried and measured worse on \texttt{examples/15},
the retried root being the better one); its value was not derived. The H+ row
of a cell whose electrons are its own protons, n\_H g (1 - x) -
n\_H\^{}2 alpha\_B x\^{}2 (+ collisional terms), has beside its root x* the root -x*
to O(x*\^{}2). On \texttt{hydrostatic\_column} (the faint field of \texttt{Log10\ of\ EUV\ luminosity:\ 10.0}) x* is about 1e-11 above 1.1 R\_p, inside the band: 70
cells of the written state carried that root, n(H+) = -8.9e-2 cm\^{}-3 at r =
1.3106 R\_p (x = -8.0e-12, the electron density of the cell negative with it),
all class 1, and \texttt{Ion\_species\_adv.txt} carried the same 70 negatives. The
golden written before section 112 has none (116.3).

\textbf{Why the mirror root was reached.} The atomic cell solve (\texttt{solve\_ieq}:
\texttt{newton\_dense}, \texttt{hybrd1} only when the Newton reports failure) stops when its
step is below 1e-11 (1 + max \textbar x\_i\textbar), an absolute step on fractions of order
one, and reports convergence: at x* \textasciitilde{} 1e-11 the attempts from the previous
state and from the uncoupled balance stop at a relative error of order one
(measure 4e-3 to 1, refused), and the attempt from all nuclei neutral reaches
the mirror root, which the measure passes (its imbalance is 1.7e-16 of its
gross rate in the fixture below) and the band admitted. The comment of
\texttt{newton\_solver.f90} and the solver paragraph of
\texttt{docs/physics\_overview/ionization.tex} said the fallback made the result
"never worse than" hybrd1\textquotesingle s; a Newton that stops on its step test is never
checked by hybrd1, so that was false (both corrected).

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{118.2 The fix (\texttt{src/modules/radiation/ionization\_equilibrium.f90})}{118.2 The fix (src/modules/radiation/ionization\_equilibrium.f90)}}\label{1182-the-fix-srcmodulesradiationionization_equilibriumf90}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{The bound} (\texttt{ionization\_fractions\_physical}, lines 4484-4615). The
  rounding of a number keeps its sign, so a fraction whose root is zero or
  positive comes out negative only through a sum or difference formed at
  the scale of the element total, whose spacing is \texttt{fraction\_resolution} =
  epsilon(1) = 2.2e-16 (\texttt{chemical\_row\_layout.f90}, the resolution of the
  floor of 116.3). A candidate is outside the simplex when a fraction is
  below -epsilon(1), or when the k fractions of one element sum above
  1 + k epsilon(1): the neutral remainder 1 - s is a fraction of the same
  element with the same resolution, and forming s from k terms, each at most
  one, adds at most (k - 1) epsilon/2 of rounding (a fraction converted into
  another element\textquotesingle s budget, HeH+ into helium, OH and H2O into hydrogen,
  counts as one term). \texttt{ftol} is gone.
\item
  \textbf{A candidate outside the simplex is projected onto it and judged again}
  (both branches of the sweep; molecular lines 3015-3036 and 3143-3147,
  atomic lines 3503-3530 and 3549-3553). The projection is
  \texttt{clamp\_fractions\_to\_element\_budget} (negative fractions raised to zero, an
  element\textquotesingle s species scaled to its nuclei) and the measure is taken again at
  the projected state; it is a root when the measure says so (class 3). It
  is taken only when no attempt gives a root without projection, and before
  the constrained continuation (molecular), the fourth solve (atomic, item 3)
  and the bounded non-root. Why: hybrd1 resolves an unknown only to its step
  test on the scaled norm of all of them, so a root on a face (a stage at a
  fraction far below that, a neutral remainder of a fully ionized metal)
  comes back a little outside it; the band admitted those as they were, the
  bound alone left them non-roots (with items 1 and 3 only,
  \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} had 11 non-root records at excursions of 3.6e-14 to
  2.1e-13 of the element, and \texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner} 87 against 85). The mirror
  root projects to x = 0, where the row\textquotesingle s imbalance is its whole production:
  no root.
\item
  \textbf{The atomic cell without a root is re-solved} (atomic branch, lines
  3353-3420). The attempt loop of the atomic branch has a fourth attempt,
  made only when attempts 1-3 gave neither a root nor a projected root: the
  first bounded non-root of 1-3 is re-solved by hybrd1, whose step test is
  relative to the scaled norm of the unknowns and resolves a trace stage of
  that size; the result faces the same judge and takes the class its rank
  gives. A cell with a root among attempts 1-3 never reaches it.
\item
  \textbf{Admitted states are written on the face} (atomic extraction, lines
  3698-3710; the metal neutral of both branches, 3717-3725). A state
  admitted with a fraction in {[}-epsilon, 0) or a remainder in
  {[}-k epsilon, 0) is on the face to the resolution of the composition; the
  stage fractions are raised to zero and the neutral remainders H I, He I
  and X I are taken as max(1 - s, 0), as the molecular branch already did
  for its species and H I, He I. No negative density leaves the sweep.
\end{enumerate}

Considered and not taken: (a) refusing the candidate and keeping the cell\textquotesingle s
entry composition (class 6), which leaves the cell on a non-root where the
physical root exists and is reachable; (b) changing the stopping test of
\texttt{newton\_dense} for every cell solve (116.6 item (i), still for the user):
items 2 and 3 change only the cells that have no root without them.

The H-only branch of the sweep (no helium) has no admissibility test and no
acceptance measure. MEASURED on an H-only variant of \texttt{hydrostatic\_column}
(He/H 0, 300 steps): no negative fraction is written, and the control and
the final source give bitwise identical outputs. Not changed (118.5).

\subsection{119. One description of the matching surface: the carrier base face open against the stated ghost partition, the ghost hydrogen census and one ghost temperature from the profile, Photochem\textquotesingle s top at p\_match, and the wind\textquotesingle s H and H2 fluxes handed over separately (item (AN) items 2-4, user decisions 2026-10-07; 2026-10-08)}\label{119-one-description-of-the-matching-surface-the-carrier-base-face-open-against-the-stated-ghost-partition-the-ghost-hydrogen-census-and-one-ghost-temperature-from-the-profile-photochems-top-at-p_match-and-the-winds-h-and-h2-fluxes-handed-over-separately-item-an-items-2-4-user-decisions-2026-10-07-2026-10-08}

Design: \texttt{md/AN\_overlap\_structural\_design\_20261008.md} (the boundary-value
problem on each side, the exchanged quantities, the closure variables; no
public interface beyond the approved decisions changed: no input.inp key, no
restart token, additive keys in EXHALE\_resolved.out and the profile header).
Diagnosis: \texttt{md/AN\_matching\_surface\_diagnosis\_20261007.md}. Every number is
MEASURED unless marked READ. No golden was refreshed.

\subsubsection{119.1 What changed}\label{1191-what-changed}

\textbf{Wind (Fortran).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}: where a handoff states the base H2
  partition (\texttt{carrier\_base\_composition\_imposed}, base.inp \texttt{q\_H2\_base} or the
  profile), face 0 of the carrier column carries the eddy, molecular and
  settling flux of H2 against the Dirichlet ghost, formed exactly as an
  interior face (\texttt{carrier\_face\_coefficients}, \texttt{jfirst\ =\ 0}; \texttt{carrier\_residual}
  forms \texttt{Jf(0)}; \texttt{solve\_carriers} adds only the cell-1 derivative \texttt{dJr(0)},
  the ghost being data). \texttt{carrier\_state} takes the ghost value of a stated
  carrier from the sweep-pinned species vector in every path. The enthalpy of
  that relative transport at face 0 is opened with it
  (\texttt{molecular\_carrier\_enthalpy\_face\_flux}). Without a stated partition the
  face stays closed (unchanged). Header sections 5 and 6 rewritten.
\item
  The certified state\textquotesingle s H2 flux through the base face is recorded
  (\texttt{carrier\_steady\_residual} optional \texttt{base\_face\_flux},
  \texttt{carrier\_record\_base\_face\_flux}, called by the certification\textquotesingle s
  \texttt{carrier\_rows\_of\_state}) and written to EXHALE\_resolved.out:
  \texttt{base\_ghost\_h2\_nuclei\_fraction}, \texttt{base\_face\_carrier\_state},
  \texttt{base\_face\_H2\_flux\_cgs} (molecules cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1, outward), its \texttt{\_adv}/\texttt{\_dif}
  halves, \texttt{base\_face\_radius\_cm} (\texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90}).
\item
  Ghost hydrogen census (\texttt{input\_read.f90} \texttt{apply\_lower\_atmosphere\_profile},
  \texttt{parameters.f90} \texttt{h\_atomic\_share\_base}, \texttt{composition.f90}
  \texttt{base\_h2\_nuclei\_fraction}): with a profile, x2 = 1 - q\_H n\_tot/n\_H, the
  nuclei per particle from the profile\textquotesingle s rho, n\_tot and X\_El (agrees with the
  sum over the q\_ columns to 2e-5 on the LHS profiles); the hydrogen of CH4,
  NH3, H2O goes onto H2. base.inp keeps 2q(1 + He/H)/(1 + q).
\item
  One ghost temperature (\texttt{base\_boundary.f90}): with a profile the two
  advective ghosts carry the profile\textquotesingle s T at their centres (the conduction
  bath\textquotesingle s temperature) and the volume-averaged pressure of the hydrostatic
  column at that T(r) (\texttt{hydrostatic\_pressure\_on\_profile}, Simpson in 1/T, 32
  panels); the well-balanced block keeps chi\_j = nhat T\_j. The stale comment
  of \texttt{base\_reservoir\_temperature\_at} (profiles "an isotherm at the clima skin
  temperature") rewritten.
\item
  The profile reader accepts p\_top\_bar = p\_match\_bar (\texttt{lower\_atmosphere\_profile.f90}).
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Photochem side (Python).}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py}: the top FACE of the chemistry grid is
  placed at p\_match (\texttt{steady\_state\_with\_top\_face\_at\_match}, moves of
  H ln(p\_face/p\_match), tolerance 1e-3 in ln p, the gas-giant stepper\textquotesingle s own
  re-pinning suspended during the passes); the table ends at p\_match with the
  face state continued from the two top cell centres (\texttt{level\_at\_top\_face});
  the chemistry runs on the climate column plus the column-balance rise up to
  the face as a persistent T(p) (\texttt{set\_chemistry\_temperature});
  \texttt{-\/-trial-flux-H2} splits the hydrogen flux as the wind does (H2 that part,
  atomic H the rest); an inflow of atoms deposits phi\_in D0/2
  (D0 = 36118.11 cm\^{}-1) in the column energy balance with the shape of the
  column\textquotesingle s own H + H (+M) rate (\texttt{recombination\_heating\_of\_inflowing\_atoms});
  \texttt{-\/-toa}/\texttt{-\/-p-top-bar} refused. New header keys \texttt{trial\_flux\_H2},
  \texttt{measured\_flux\_H2}, \texttt{recombination\_heat\_flux\_cgs} (\texttt{lower\_profile\_schema.py},
  which also accepts a table whose top is p\_match).
\item
  \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column\_balance.py}: \texttt{chemical\_heating} in \texttt{solve}; the
  overlying column is the wind\textquotesingle s, no absorber above p\_match
  (\texttt{include\_gas\_above\_match} default False; True is the former treatment, a
  diagnostic).
\item
  \texttt{element\_flux\_closure.py}: fourth variable Phi\_H2 from the wind\textquotesingle s
  \texttt{base\_face\_H2\_flux\_cgs}, damped update, residual
  \textbar F\_H2 - Phi\_H2\textbar/\textbar F\_H\textbar{} \textless= tol; \texttt{-\/-phi0-H2}; history columns
  \texttt{trial\_F\_H2\ meas\_F\_H2\ eps\_H2}; the empty overlap window (top at the match)
  read as such, the steady window supplying the flux.
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection{119.2 Tests (RED on the source of section 118, GREEN now)}\label{1192-tests-red-on-the-source-of-section-118-green-now}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist

  \texttt{carrier\_base\_face} (new): the base face against the analytic spherical
  diffusion flux of X = X\_inf - C/r (error and order on two widths), the

\item
  \texttt{matching\_surface} (new, Python): the split imposition, the top-face

\item
  \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column}: section 9 (a top-node heating equals a top flux to
  1e-9 K, the budget closes, the total is recorded, spread heating warms the
  match less than top heating) added; the transfer tests keep the gas above

All 63 suites (one thread): every exit status and every failing row as in the

pass.

Adapter on the route-B configuration (scratch, trial H2 flux 3.6e7 g/s of

match in 2-3 passes (final offset 8.5e-4 in ln p); T\_match 268.13 K against
271.16 K of the stored iteration under the same F\_cond (no absorber above the
match, plus F\_chem = 0.199 erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1); 3.9 min.

\subsubsection{119.5 The three LHS 1140 b states: the open face does not certify}\label{1195-the-three-lhs-1140-b-states-the-open-face-does-not-certify}

Closures \texttt{\textless{}case\textgreater{}\_structural\_a3663329} beside each case (seeds: route B
\texttt{xuv\_x1p3\_rerun\_bd2b93d3/k09}, He/H 2.09 \texttt{energy\_coupling\_nlte\_rerun\_bd2b93d3/k06},
He/H 0.65 the stored \texttt{phase5/HeH0.65/k02}; first trials their measured
fluxes; adapter without \texttt{-\/-toa}; \texttt{-\/-kmax\ 16}).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{No closure passed k = 0.} He/H 2.09: the stationary route refused at
  outer pass 37 (the joint distance stalled): the carrier relaxation took no
  admissible step from pass 10 on, refused on He++ at the outer edge of the
  H2 front (cell 309, r = 2.00 R\_p, \textbar res\textbar{} over its terms 0.71), carrier
  balance He+/He++ 0.62/0.89, the element He/H partition 8.6e-3 above its
  tolerance there (He/H falls from 2.09 at the base to 0.16 at the top and is
  not monotonic near r = 2). Route B: hydro info = 1 from outer pass 8,
  He+/He++ refusing at r = 1.2-1.4; stopped by hand at pass 10 (40 min a
  pass). He/H 0.65: refused at pass 60 (carrier rows refusing on He++ at
  r = 3.6 R\_p from pass 38 on, H2 front x2 = 1e-2 at 27 R\_p).
\item
  \textbf{Attribution (scratch, the k00 inputs of each closure, the same seed and
  environment):} a build of this source with face 0 closed again (everything
  else of this section in place) certifies the He/H 2.09 wind in 5 outer
  passes and route B in 11; at He/H 0.65 it had not certified after 7
  passes either (stopped; the same refusal as the bd2b93d3 rerun of section
  117, TO\_BE\_DONE (AO) D5). The open-face binary seeded from the certified
  closed-face He/H 2.09 state follows the same path toward the same stall
  (H2 front x2 = 1e-2 at 1.15 -\textgreater{} 1.54 R\_p by pass 7; stopped). The open face
  is what moves the He/H 2.09 and route-B winds into a regime the
  stationary route does not certify: with H2 supplied at the eddy rate the
  H2 layer reaches r = 2 R\_p (He/H 2.09; 1.17 R\_p with the face closed), and
  its outer edge, where H2 at 1e-2 of the hydrogen nuclei meets He++ at
  1e-5 of the helium, is where the carrier interval is refused. This appears
  to be the failure of (AO) D5 and the unresolved trace stage rows of (AP);
  that attribution is an interpretation and was not tested.
\item
  \textbf{The matching surface itself (He/H 2.09, the uncertified k00 state):}
  ghost T 218.8 / 254.2 K against the profile\textquotesingle s 218.1 / 252.6 K at the same
  pressures (before: 293.7 / 287.6 against 224.8 / 256.4); ghost x\_H
  6.0e-6 against Photochem\textquotesingle s 5.9e-6 (before 4.0e-4); cell 1 x\_H 5.4e-6, x\_H2
  0.1928 against the profile\textquotesingle s q\_H2 0.1926 (before 0.094 and 0.137, and
  0.093 / 0.137 with the face closed and the census and ghost temperature of
  this section). He/H 0.65 (uncertified k00): ghost x\_H 1.03e-5 against
  1.01e-5, cell 1 x\_H 6.8e-6 and x\_H2 0.4344 against 0.4340 (before, the
  stored state of the oxygen-defect binary: 0.58 and 0.018). On the states
  the solver returned the jump at the face is gone with the open face and
  only with it; route B kept no usable state (its last two pass states
  carry an unconverged hydrodynamic solve). Figure \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/an\_struct/fig\_matching\_surface\_structural.png},
  tables \texttt{an\_struct\_tables.txt} (script \texttt{an\_structural\_compare.py}).
\item
  Not measured, because no state certified: T\_match, F\_H, F\_He, Mdot and the
  width-matched EW of a converged closure (\texttt{route\_xuv\_scaled/measure\_case\_a3663329.sh}
  and \texttt{run\_ew\_a3663329.sh} are in place).
\end{itemize}

Open, for the user: the open base face is physically required (decision 2)
and makes the wind\textquotesingle s H2 layer reach 1.5-2 R\_p (27 R\_p at He/H 0.65 with the
unscaled spectrum); certifying those states needs the trace-stage resolution
of (AP) and the He++ carrier failure of (AO) D5 first. Items 5 of (AN) (the
p\_match sensitivity) waits on that.

\subsection{120. The cell solve stops when its Newton correction resolves every unknown relative to its own magnitude; the hydrogen-only sweep faces the same judge (item (AP), user decision 2026-10-08)}\label{120-the-cell-solve-stops-when-its-newton-correction-resolves-every-unknown-relative-to-its-own-magnitude-the-hydrogen-only-sweep-faces-the-same-judge-item-ap-user-decision-2026-10-08}

Item (AP) of \texttt{md/TO\_BE\_DONE.md} and 116.6 item 1, 118.5 (a) and (b). Every
number is MEASURED unless marked READ. Control: the gfortran 16.2 build of the
source of section 119 (md5 \texttt{52be5b2d}, the source of \texttt{EXHALE\_a3663329.x});
measured: the same source with this section\textquotesingle s change alone (md5 \texttt{b0bdc85c});
one thread, scratch copies of the regression cases with
\texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_CLASS\_RECORD=1} in \texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/ap\_d5/}
(\texttt{reg\_ctrl/}, \texttt{reg\_ap1/}). No golden was refreshed.

\subsubsection{120.1 The stopping test}\label{1201-the-stopping-test}

\textbf{What was wrong.} The cell solve reported convergence on two tests, neither
of which is a statement about each unknown. The atomic \texttt{newton\_dense} stopped
when its step was below 1e-11 (1 + max \textbar x\_i\textbar), an absolute step on fractions
of order one, or when the residual norm of the cell\textquotesingle s network was below
sqrt(eps): a trace stage whose root is of that order (H+ at 8e-12 of the
hydrogen under a faint field) was returned at a relative error of order one.
MINPACK \texttt{hybrd1} (the molecular and He 2\^{}3S layouts) stops on a relative step
of sqrt(eps) on the SCALED NORM of all the unknowns, which the O(1) fractions
dominate, so H3+ and He+ in molecular gas were left out of balance by up to a
few per cent of their own gross rates (116.2, 116.6): every H3+ refusal of
the hp\_* cases had this cause.

\textbf{The criterion} (\texttt{src/modules/nonlinear\_system\_solver/newton\_solver.f90},
module header and \texttt{correction\_resolves\_every\_unknown}). Every unknown is the
fraction of the nuclei of one element held by one species, and a trace
species is as much a part of the answer as an O(1) fraction (its density
enters its own cooling, the electrons of a shielded layer and the carried
stages of the transport solve). The solve has converged when the Newton
correction at the returned state resolves every unknown relative to its own
magnitude,

\begin{verbatim}
|dx_i| <= tau |x_i| + epsilon(1)    for every i,
\end{verbatim}

with tau the tolerance of the sweep (sqrt(eps) = 1.5e-8, or what
\texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_TOL} names) and epsilon(1) = 2.2e-16 = \texttt{fraction\_resolution}, the
resolution of a fraction at its element total, below which no change of a
fraction moves any sum of its element: the resolution floor of the acceptance
measure (116.3). The correction dx = -J\^{}\{-1\} f is the Newton estimate of the
remaining error of each unknown, so a state that meets the test is resolved
to about tau\^{}2 of each unknown, far inside the acceptance of every kinetic
row.

\textbf{The solver} (\texttt{damped\_newton\_to\_every\_unknown}, with an analytic Jacobian
for \texttt{newton\_dense} and a forward-difference one, the step rule of MINPACK
fdjac1, for \texttt{newton\_fd}; LU with partial pivoting kept for the line search).
A trial x + lam dx (lam halved from 1, at most 20 times, 60 iterations) is
taken when it lowers the residual norm (the Armijo test that stood) or when
the simplified correction at the trial point, J\^{}\{-1\} f(x + lam dx) with the
Jacobian of x and measured relative to each unknown (D\_i = 1/(\textbar x\_i\textbar{} +
epsilon(1))), falls below (1 - lam/4) of that of the step. The second test is
what lets a trace unknown be resolved once the large rows sit at their
rounding and the residual norm, which they dominate, can no longer decrease.
The residual-norm stop (fnorm \textless{} ftol) is gone: a norm over rows of different
sizes says nothing about each unknown.

\textbf{Where it acts.} \texttt{solve\_ieq} (H-only, atomic H/He, atomic H/He + metals):
\texttt{newton\_dense} with the new test; on failure \texttt{hybrd1} from the same guess and
then the Newton from hybrd1\textquotesingle s result, kept only when it meets the test. Every
\texttt{hybrd1} call of the sweep (the molecular attempts, the He 2\^{}3S systems, the
fourth attempt of the atomic branch) is now \texttt{hybrd1\_resolving\_every\_unknown}:
hybrd1, then the forward-difference Newton from its result, kept (info = 1)
only when it meets the test; otherwise hybrd1\textquotesingle s x and info are returned. The
validation switch \texttt{EXHALE\_FORCE\_HYBRD1} skips both Newtons. Not changed: the
constrained continuation (class 5; MINPACK hybrd in mode 2 on ln n) and the
\texttt{\_adv} post-processing solves (\texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90}), which are not the
cell solve of the sweep.

\subsubsection{120.2 The hydrogen-only sweep is judged like the others}\label{1202-the-hydrogen-only-sweep-is-judged-like-the-others}

118.5 (a): the H-only branch had no admissibility test and no acceptance
measure. Now (\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, H-only branch): a candidate is a
root when it lies in {[}-epsilon, 1 + epsilon{]} (\texttt{ionization\_fractions\_physical},
which already carried the H-only layout) and its one row, the H+ row, is
within \texttt{ieq\_res\_tol} of its own gross rate or below the resolution floor
(\texttt{normalized\_reaction\_residual}, whose layout already carried System\_H); two
starting points, the guess built from the previous state and the positive
root of the quadratic balance in closed form (\texttt{System\_H.f90},
\texttt{hydrogen\_ionization\_balance\_root}: A x\^{}2 + B x - g = 0 with A = n\_H (a + b),
B = g - n\_H b, each sign of B in its cancellation-free form); a candidate
outside the simplex projected and judged again (class 3); a cell without a
root takes the bounded non-root (class 4) or, above the cap, keeps its entry
composition (class 6). The ledger, the class record of each cell and the
non-root streak are those of the other branches: their reset and their
assembly were moved out of the He branch so that both sweeps fill one ledger.

MEASURED on the H-only variant of \texttt{hydrostatic\_column} (He/H 0, 300 steps):
151200 class-1 records over the run and none of any other class (the control
had no record at all), outputs within 1.0e-6 of the control (Hydro\_ioniz
column 7 at r = 1.32 R\_p, 7.4219e-28 against 7.4219e-28 in its last digits;
Ion\_species within 5.0e-7).

\subsection{121. Why the open-face LHS 1140 b winds were refused: an unbounded reconstruction of the trace ionization stages, and an outer alternation that oscillates between a helium-retaining and a helium-depleted wind (item (AO) D5 and section 119.5, 2026-10-08)}\label{121-why-the-open-face-lhs-1140-b-winds-were-refused-an-unbounded-reconstruction-of-the-trace-ionization-stages-and-an-outer-alternation-that-oscillates-between-a-helium-retaining-and-a-helium-depleted-wind-item-ao-d5-and-section-1195-2026-10-08}

number is MEASURED unless marked READ. Builds (gfortran 16.2): control
\texttt{52be5b2d} (source of section 119, that of \texttt{EXHALE\_a3663329.x}), section 120
alone \texttt{b0bdc85c}, section 120 + this section \texttt{0d817aa3}; scratch
\texttt{\textasciitilde{}/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/ap\_d5/} (\texttt{k00\_\textless{}case\textgreater{}\_\textless{}build\textgreater{}{[}\_\textless{}route\textgreater{}{]}/}: the
k00 inputs of the structural closures of 119.5, the closure\textquotesingle s environment,

\subsubsection{121.1 The refusing rows: a trace stage drained through its own outflow face}\label{1211-the-refusing-rows-a-trace-stage-drained-through-its-own-outflow-face}

The control\textquotesingle s He/H 2.09 k00 solve (\texttt{phase3/energy\_coupling\_nlte\_structural\_a3663329/k00})
kept no carrier step from outer pass 10 to its refusal at pass 37 ("NO
ADMISSIBLE ADVANCE"), every interval refused on He++ at cell 309 (r = 2.003
R\_p). Its last carrier assembly (\texttt{output/carrier\_row\_terms.txt}):

\textbar{} cell \textbar{} r {[}R\_p{]} \textbar{} x(He++) of the helium \textbar{} He++ face flux out {[}cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1{]} \textbar{} production \textbar{} loss \textbar{} \textbar res\textbar{} / terms \textbar{}
\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar{}
\textbar{} 308 \textbar{} 1.985 \textbar{} 3.37e-6 \textbar{} 6.3e-3 \textbar{} 1.3e-3 \textbar{} 1.9e-4 \textbar{} 0.31 \textbar{}
\textbar{} 309 \textbar{} 2.003 \textbar{} 6.0e-12 \textbar{} 4.76e-2 \textbar{} 2.1e-3 \textbar{} 3.3e-10 \textbar{} 0.71 \textbar{}
\textbar{} 310 \textbar{} 2.021 \textbar{} 5.6e-5 \textbar{} 2.70e-1 \textbar{} 9.0e-3 \textbar{} 3.5e-3 \textbar{} 0.67 \textbar{}

A cell holding He++ at 6e-12 of its helium (n = 9e-5 cm\^{}-3) sent 4.8e-2
cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1 of He++ through its outer face, 23 times its production. He+ had
the same pattern (cells 309-312: 4.6e-3, 2.1e-2, 6.0e-2, 2.5e-2 of the
helium) and so had the He/H 0.65 closure (He++ 5.5e-19 at cell 364, r = 3.62
R\_p, between 5.7e-12 and 6.5e-11). No implicit step of positive density
balances such a row, so every substep of the interval was refused.

\textbf{The defect.} The stage face fraction is the WENO3 reconstruction of the
stage fraction (\texttt{ionization\_stage\_face\_fractions} -\textgreater{} \texttt{species\_face\_fraction}
-\textgreater{} \texttt{Reconstruct\_scalar}, \texttt{src/modules/states/Reconstruction.f90}; the
stationary route selects WENO3 for the whole run). Its smoothness indicators
were b = dq\^{}2 + dr\^{}2: a squared jump of the scalar plus the squared cell
width in code units, which do not carry the same units. For every scalar
whose jumps are below dr (dr = 1.8e-2 at r = 2 R\_p; every trace stage) both
indicators are dr\^{}2, the weights are the linear ones, and the face value is
the unlimited three-point interpolant, which is not bounded by the two cells
of the face. At the minimum of cell 309 it is
x309 + 0.33 (x310 - x309) - 0.17 (x309 - x308) = 1.8e-5, three million times
the cell\textquotesingle s own value (\texttt{species\_face\_fraction} bounds a face to {[}0,1{]} only).
The published weights (Yamaleev and Carpenter 2009, J. Comput. Phys. 228,
3025, Eq. 65, READ from the publisher PDF in \texttt{references/}) carry the square
of the solution scale in the floor, eps = max(\textbar\textbar u0\^{}2\textbar\textbar, \textbar\textbar(u0\_xi)\^{}2\textbar\textbar) dxi\^{}2;
\texttt{docs/physics\_overview/numerics.tex} already recorded that the code\textquotesingle s floor
drops that scale.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{121.2 The fix (\texttt{Reconstruct\_scalar}, WENO3 branch)}{121.2 The fix (Reconstruct\_scalar, WENO3 branch)}}\label{1212-the-fix-reconstruct_scalar-weno3-branch}

\begin{verbatim}
eps_j = (dr_j q_s,j)^2 ,   q_s,j = max(|q_j-1|, |q_j|, |q_j+1|) ,
b0 = dq+^2 + eps_j ,       b1 = dq-^2 + eps_j ,
\end{verbatim}

with S = 1 where a stencil of zeros gives b = 0. The face values then commute
with a scaling of the scalar (q -\textgreater{} c q gives c times the faces). The scale is
the local one and not a column norm as in the published prescription,
because a stage fraction spans many decades along one column (He++ from
9e-17 to 7e-4 of the helium on the refused He/H 2.09 state) and a column
norm would set the floor by its largest value. The primitive reconstruction
of the hydrodynamic variables (\texttt{Reconstruct}) keeps eps = dr\^{}2 (not changed
here: it changes every result of the code; for the user).

\textbf{Test} (\texttt{src/tests/species\_face\_flux}, new \texttt{test\_trace\_scalar\_reconstruction}):
RED = the control\textquotesingle s \texttt{Reconstruction.f90} (scratch tree \texttt{red\_tree/}), GREEN =
this section.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{FUTURE\_trace\_scalar\_faces\_scale\_with\_the\_scalar} (a smooth O(1) profile
  and the same times 1e-8): RED 3.9e-11 relative departure, GREEN 2.2e-16.
\item
  \texttt{FUTURE\_trace\_minimum\_outflow\_face\_bounded\_by\_its\_cell} (the triple
  3.37e-6, 5.98e-12, 5.56e-5 of cells 308-310, outward mass flux): face
  fraction RED 1.78e-5, GREEN 0 (at most the cell\textquotesingle s 5.98e-12).
\item
  \texttt{PIN\_order\_one\_scalar\_faces\_on\_the\_profile}: 9.9e-6 in both.
  Suite: 18/18 (RED 16/18).
  \texttt{src/tests/physics\_probe} (\texttt{weno3\_reconstruction\_order.f90}) asserted that
  \texttt{Reconstruct} and \texttt{Reconstruct\_scalar} put the same face on one profile and
  that its local copy of the face formula is the scalar routine; with the two
  floors now different by design both rows read 2.2e-10 against the new
  source. The local copy takes the scalar floor as an option
  (\texttt{weno3\_local\_copy\_matches\_production}, 0 against \texttt{Reconstruct\_scalar}) and
  the vector row compares \texttt{Reconstruct} with the primitive form
  (\texttt{weno3\_vector\_matches\_primitive\_formula}, 0); the order tables are
  unchanged.
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection{121.3 The trajectory: the outer alternation oscillates between two winds}\label{1213-the-trajectory-the-outer-alternation-oscillates-between-two-winds}

The fix removes the drained minima and the cell-324 non-roots of section
120.5, but not the refusal: with it (build \texttt{0d817aa3}) the He/H 2.09 k00
solve fails the hydrodynamic solve at outer pass 8 (hydro info = 1), route
B at pass 5 and He/H 0.65 is refused on He++ rows whose physical terms are
1e-12 cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1 at r = 6.35 R\_p (from pass 15). The cause is the order of
the alternation itself. Each outer pass solves the wind at a fixed
composition, then relaxes the element composition to the fixed point of the
element operator AT THAT WIND, then the carriers. On the control\textquotesingle s He/H 2.09
k00 run (\texttt{stage\_state\_p\textless{}k\textgreater{}\_*.txt}, the composition each pass hands on, MEASURED):

The element fixed point of one wind empties the helium above 1.45 R\_p
(He/H 1.4 -\textgreater{} 6e-5 at 1.5 R\_p, 1.0 -\textgreater{} 1e-11 at 3 R\_p: diffusive separation in a
wind of 5 m/s at 1.4 R\_p, READ from the state); the wind of that composition
loses the helium photoionization heating (T 3600 -\textgreater{} 1300-1400 K) and,
without He+ to destroy it, H2 survives outward (the front at x2 = 1e-2 walks
1.15 -\textgreater{} 1.54 -\textgreater{} 2.06 R\_p, the 2 R\_p layer of 119.5); the element fixed point
of that cold wind puts the helium back (He/H 1.35), and so on: a period-2
to period-4 oscillation of the Picard alternation between a
helium-retaining and a helium-depleted wind, with the inner map distance of
the element step 0.13-0.95 over the first ten passes. The carrier refusals of 121.1 are
where the oscillation strands the carried stages (steep fronts of trace
stages, which the unbounded faces then drained).

\textbf{Damping.} Run with \texttt{EXHALE\_DIFF\_OMEGA=0.5} (the starting under-relaxation
of the element update; the closures pin 1.0 in their \texttt{closure.json}, the
code\textquotesingle s own start is 0.5) alone, the He/H 2.09 k00 solve still
refused at pass 10 (carrier step refused on H+ at r = 3.0 R\_p; element map
distance 0.2-0.8 at every pass). With the carrier movement bound also held
at 0.04 (\texttt{EXHALE\_CARRIER\_TRUST\_HOLD=0.04}; by default it doubles from 0.01
to 0.32 over the passes), the alternation contracts: element map distance
0.15 at pass 16, 6.6e-2 at 18, 1.4e-2 at 23, 9.6e-4 at 30, the worst gated
row 1.45e-3 at pass 30, and the state is ACCEPTED and CERTIFIED at outer
pass 44 (He/H 2.09 -\textgreater{} 0.19 from base to top, smooth; H2 front x2 = 1e-2 at
1.227 R\_p, against 1.17 R\_p with the face closed; steady Mdot median
2.256e7 g s\^{}-1 with the trial fluxes of k00). The 2 R\_p H2 layer of 119.5
was therefore the oscillation, not the open face\textquotesingle s solution.

\textbf{Attribution} (the same recipe, the same k00 inputs, scratch): the control
build (the source of section 119) and the build of section 120 alone are
ACCEPTED at outer pass 44 as well, along the same element map distances to
the printed digits for the first ten passes. The three certified states agree
within 4.8e-4 (Hydro\_ioniz, the cooling column; Ion\_species 4.4e-4), steady
Mdot medians 2.2557662e7, 2.2557662e7 and 2.2557670e7 g/s. So the outer
alternation, and not the defects of sections 120 and 121, is what kept the
He/H 2.09 open-face wind from certifying; the reconstruction defect is what
held the refused control run at no admissible advance once the oscillation
had left steep fronts of trace stages.

\subsubsection{121.4 Regression cases (section 120 -\textgreater{} this section)}\label{1214-regression-cases-section-120---this-section}

Section 120 build -\textgreater{} this section\textquotesingle s (gfortran, one thread, the seventeen
default cases). BITWISE in sixteen of them: \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_trace\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_front},
\texttt{hydrostatic\_column}, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry}, \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, \texttt{mol\_metals},
\texttt{mol\_lyman\_werner}, \texttt{mol\_diffusion}, \texttt{mol\_ir\_bands}, \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion},
\texttt{mol\_carrier}, \texttt{lower\_profile}, \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton}, \texttt{wasp\_he23off},
\texttt{wasp\_full}. CHANGED: \texttt{iontrans\_metals}, the stationary atomic case
whose ionization stages are carried (WENO3 stage faces): rho, v, p, T within
5.0e-4, He II within 7.5e-4, He III within 9.3e-4, He I within 1.0e-2 at
1.2-3 R\_p (where the helium is ionized and He I is the minor stage), every
species below 1.2 R\_p within 5.6e-5; certified in both builds.

\subsubsection{121.5 What is left, and the proposal}\label{1215-what-is-left-and-the-proposal}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Route B} (He/H 0.65, s = 1.3) is not certified by any route tried:
  the default and omega 0.5 routes fail the hydrodynamic solve at outer
  pass 3-5 (info 1, mass row 1.2-1.6; every build, the control included);
  omega 0.25 with the carrier bound held at 0.02 keeps the hydrodynamic
  solve but the element map distance stays at 0.3-0.9 through pass 16. The
  closure with the frozen binary stopped at its k00 (outer pass 4) when the
  charge-exchange guard of the constrained continuation reported a thread
  holding no cell (\texttt{cx\_add\_to\_turnover}, \texttt{error\ stop}), during the JFNK of
  the pass after the failed hydrodynamic solve. \texttt{cx\_set\_cell} stores the
  cell temperature as the tag that guard tests (\texttt{cx\_cell\_T\ \textless{}\ 0} means "no
  cell"), so a cell temperature below zero in a failed JFNK iterate would
  trip it with that message; this was NOT verified (no traceback), and the
  gfortran runs of the same route did not abort.
\item
  \textbf{He/H 0.65} (\texttt{phase5}): the element map distance stays at 3e-2 to
  5e-2 per pass and the carried He+ rows at 0.4-0.6 of their terms near
  r = 1.2 R\_p (cells 217-226) at omega 0.5 and 0.25 alike, and the
  hydrodynamic solve fails at outer pass 11 (closure, 0.5/0.04) or 17
  (0.25/0.02). With the face closed (section 117) the same case did not
  certify either: carrier refusals at r = 3.05-3.9 R\_p in a wind at
  1600-1700 K whose helium is almost all neutral (He+ 2e-7 and He++ 4e-10
  of the helium, production of He++ 6 to 13 times its loss on frozen
  carriers). The original D5 description (a helium-depleted outer wind) is
  the depleted branch of the oscillation of 121.3.
\item
  \textbf{Proposal.} The outer route alternates three solves (wind at fixed
  composition, element composition at a frozen wind to its fixed point,
  carriers at a frozen wind under a movement bound) whose coupling through
  the helium (its heating, its mean mass, its settling) is strong enough
  on these states to make the alternation oscillate. Two remedies, in
  order of effort: (a) a movement bound on the element update like the one
  the carriers have, held rather than grown, with omega 0.5 (what converged
  He/H 2.09; a route setting, not an equation, and the closures of section
  122 run with it); (b) the element and stage rows as unknowns of the
  stationary Newton (\texttt{Coupled\ carrier\ solve} extended to the carried
  stages, which it refuses today, and to the element rows), the one route
  that does not depend on the alternation contracting. Also for the user:
  the primitive reconstruction (\texttt{Reconstruct}) keeps eps = dr\^{}2 (121.2),
  and the charge-exchange guard of item 1 should not use the cell
  temperature as its sentinel.
\end{enumerate}

\texttt{docs/physics\_overview/numerics.tex} (paragraph "WENO3 of a transported
scalar", Eq. eq:weno\_scalar\_floor; the primitive floor stated with the
published Eq. 65), \texttt{md/TO\_BE\_DONE.md} (AO) D5.

top-face move (more than 85 minutes); the last certified iteration is k04.

The wind\textquotesingle s escape hardly moves with the trial (measured F\_H 1.30e7, F\_He
9.41e6 g/s from k0 to k4); the damped closure approaches it at omega = 0.5,
and k4 is within the tolerance 0.05 on H and the temperature (T\_match moved
2.9 K \textless{} 6 K) but not on He (0.072) or on the hydrogen split (eps\_H2 0.074).
The wind\textquotesingle s H2 flux through the base face is 5.62e11 molecules cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1
outward, 2.4 times the hydrogen escape in nuclei, with the atomic H flowing
down into the column (the split Phi\_H2 = 3.15e7 g/s on H2, -1.83e7 g/s on
atomic H at k5): H2 is mixed up by the eddy flux, dissociated in the wind,
and its atoms return to the column, where their recombination heat (2.24 erg
cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1 at k4) is as large as the conducted flux (2.43).

\textbf{State k04} (certified, \texttt{measure\_case\_a76da494.sh}, \texttt{run\_ew\_a76da494.sh}):
log10 Mdot = 7.350 (steady median 2.241e7 g/s), F\_H = 1.300e7 g/s, F\_He =
9.41e6 g/s at the match, T\_match = 242.4 K; H2 front x2 = 0.5 at 1.099 R\_p,
1e-2 at 1.227 R\_p; He 10830 EW 0.1465 \%A unbroadened, width-matched 0.1416
\%A (f = 22.06 km/s), red/blue 7.7 (observed 1.108 \%A). Collisional validity:
exobase 9.5 R\_p below the sonic point 22.6 R\_p (Kn up to 5.7 in the
acceleration region), the hydrodynamic result unvalidated there, as for every
LHS 1140 b state so far.

Against the closed-face closure of the same configuration (section 117,
bd2b93d3, k06): log Mdot 7.560 -\textgreater{} 7.350, F\_H 1.645e7 -\textgreater{} 1.300e7, F\_He
1.987e7 -\textgreater{} 9.41e6 g/s, T\_match 284.6 -\textgreater{} 242.4 K, width-matched EW 0.2790 -\textgreater{}
0.1416 \%A. The open face halves the helium escape and the line.

\textbf{The matching surface} (\texttt{an\_struct/fig\_matching\_surface\_structural\_a76da494.\{png,pdf\}},
\texttt{an\_struct\_tables\_a76da494.txt}, script \texttt{an\_structural\_compare\_a76da494.py}):
ghost T 204.7 / 225.5 K against the profile\textquotesingle s 204.4 / 224.8 K at the same
pressures; ghost x\_H 4.74e-5 against the profile\textquotesingle s q\_H 4.20e-5 / 4.56e-5;
cell 1 x\_H 4.70e-5, x\_H2 0.1928 against q\_H2 0.1926. Before (bd2b93d3, the
closed face): cell 1 at 323 K, x\_H 0.094, x\_H2 0.137 against 284.6 K, 6.8e-6

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{122.2 Route B (He/H 0.65, s = 1.3) and He/H 0.65 (\texttt{phase5})}{122.2 Route B (He/H 0.65, s = 1.3) and He/H 0.65 (phase5)}}\label{1222-route-b-heh-065-s--13-and-heh-065-phase5}

of these two are not measured; section 121.5 states what is left.
