\subsection{123. Energy continuity at the open matching surface measured; the WENO3 floor of the primitive variables tested (2026-10-08)}\label{123-energy-continuity-at-the-open-matching-surface-measured-the-weno3-floor-of-the-primitive-variables-tested-2026-10-08}

Two measurements ordered by the review of 2026-10-08 (user decisions 2 and 3 of
\texttt{md/session\_handoff\_20261008\_rev1.md}), no source change.

\textbf{Total energy flux through the matching face}
(\texttt{md/AN\_energy\_continuity\_20261008.md}, scripts \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/an\_energy/}).
One sign (outward positive), one reference (sensible enthalpy from T = 0 with the wind\textquotesingle s
caloric EOS; chemical zero at H2 v = 0 J = 0, He, CH4, NH3, H2O and the free electron,
so atomic H carries D0/2), erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1 at R0, He/H 2.09 open face, closure
iteration k04: wind side -4.6786, column side -4.6746, difference -0.0041. The
difference splits into closure residuals that vanish at convergence (conduction
+0.143, atomic-H chemical energy -0.156) and structural omissions of +0.0092. What
the column\textquotesingle s energy equation does not book is a net heating of +0.018: the relative
H2/H sensible enthalpy at the face (-0.0279 on the wind side, 5.20e11 cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1 of
diffusive H2 flux, the code\textquotesingle s own formula), the change with the 57 K warming of
the advective and interdiffusion terms (-0.0046, -0.0030) and the C/N atomization
(-0.0020). That is 0.80\% of the conducted heat (2.288) and 0.28\% of the wind\textquotesingle s XUV
heating (6.59); its effect on T\_match is at most 0.44 K (0.67 K for the H2/H term
alone) against the closure tolerance 6 K and a last-step change of 2.9 K. The
recombination heat is counted once, in the column (F\_chem); the wind\textquotesingle s energy row
has no formation-energy density. On the closed face (bd2b93d3, k06) the difference is
+0.130. This is an energy-conservation error, not a stated approximation.
User decision (2026-10-08): it is NOT implemented now. The bound stands as the
documented limitation of the interface: net +0.018 erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1, 0.8\% of F\_cond,
T\_match shift below 0.7 K; it scales with the H2 face flux and Mdot and is to be
re-measured on every other state. The corrections (a new field
\texttt{base\_face\_carrier\_enthalpy\_flux\_cgs} carried by the closure and deposited by the
adapter, or two perturbation terms built from F\_H and F\_He in
\texttt{conducted\_heat\_column\_balance.py}) are specified in the note and will be put in once
the closure converges. Two defects found by the measurement, to be fixed after the
diagnoses of the day: F\_H (wind component 1) includes the C, N and O mass (0.44\% on
k04) and is converted with the hydrogen mass in the closure and in
\texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py} (lines 286, 301), so the H flux handed to Photochem
is 0.44\% high (F\_chem 0.3\%); and the column\textquotesingle s conduction is formed from the full T
instead of T - T0 (the climate gradient at the 0.85 bar kink injects 0.10 into the
bottom flux; not at the match).

\textbf{WENO3 floor of the primitive variables} (\texttt{md/weno3\_primitive\_floor\_tests\_20261008.md},
suite \texttt{src/tests/weno3\_primitive\_floor/}, figure \texttt{docs/figures/weno3\_primitive\_floor\_20261008.png}).
The test carries a copy of the face formula and equals \texttt{Reconstruct} bit for bit with the
present floor eps = dr\^{}2. Against the scale-aware floor eps = (dr q\_s)\^{}2 (the one
of the transported scalars, section 121): rescaling invariance fails (2.3e-4 to
6.0e-3 on smooth and stored profiles, 1.7e3 on a step) against 4e-16; at outer-layer
amplitude (1e-12) a step overshoots by 0.169 of the jump with a negative face from
ratio 1e2, repaired only by the positivity limiter; the smooth order (3.00) and the
stored k04/k06 profiles (0 faces outside the neighbours, 0 negative) do not
discriminate; the face values would change by at most 3.4e-7 (rho), 4.8e-9 (p), 1.4e-6
(v; 1.9e-3 on two base faces of k06). With \texttt{Well\ balanced:\ True} the pressure faces are
second order for every floor (1.99 against 3.00), as in the cited Kaeppeli \& Mishra
(2016) design (READ: abstract and section 2.1.3). User decision (2026-10-08): change
the primitive floor to the scale-aware form, together with the Fortran work of the
oscillation/coupling decision, measured on the regression matrix and on restarts of
k04 and k06 before any golden refresh.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{124. The Photochem adapter runs on its stated step budget and retries a stalled steady-state pass once; the He/H 2.09 open-face closure resumed at k = 5 and converged there (user decisions 2026-10-08; \texttt{md/k05\_chemistry\_failure\_20261008.md})}{124. The Photochem adapter runs on its stated step budget and retries a stalled steady-state pass once; the He/H 2.09 open-face closure resumed at k = 5 and converged there (user decisions 2026-10-08; md/k05\_chemistry\_failure\_20261008.md)}}\label{124-the-photochem-adapter-runs-on-its-stated-step-budget-and-retries-a-stalled-steady-state-pass-once-the-heh-209-open-face-closure-resumed-at-k--5-and-converged-there-user-decisions-2026-10-08-mdk05_chemistry_failure_20261008md}

The k = 5 chemistry step of the He/H 2.09 open-face closure
(\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/phase3/energy\_coupling\_nlte\_structural\_a76da494}) was
refused after the top-face pass that followed the warmed-column solve: the BDF
integrator locked into a period-2 limit cycle on the condensation switch of the
water condensate at level 27 (0.70 bar), and Photochem\textquotesingle s own step cap ended the pass
(diagnosis in the note above). User decisions (2026-10-08): fix the adapter\textquotesingle s two
defects and resume the closure at k = 5 with unchanged inputs; do not swap inputs to
avoid the stall.

\textbf{Adapter} (\texttt{src/utils/photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py}; Python only, no Fortran
touched, binary a76da494 unchanged).

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  The step budget. Every steady-state pass now runs through
  \texttt{robust\_steps\_to\_steady\_state}, which sets \texttt{pc.gdat.max\_total\_step\ =\ 100*blocks}
  before \texttt{initialize\_robust\_stepper}. Before, the adapter counted \texttt{-\/-blocks} x 100
  calls of \texttt{robust\_step} but never set Photochem\textquotesingle s cap (\texttt{gasgiants.py},
  default 100000), so any \texttt{-\/-blocks} above 1000 had no effect and the run record
  stated a budget that was not used. Setting the cap was chosen over refusing
  \texttt{-\/-blocks\ \textgreater{}\ 1000} because \texttt{-\/-blocks} is documented as the budget (the module
  docstring, \texttt{closure.json} with \texttt{-\/-blocks\ 1600}) and a refusal would have changed
  the closure\textquotesingle s settings.
\item
  The single stall retry (\texttt{steady\_state\_with\_stall\_retry}). A pass that ends
  without a steady state (budget spent, or Photochem\textquotesingle s \texttt{give\_up}) is integrated
  once more from the state the stalled integration holds, at relative tolerance
  \texttt{STALL\_RETRY\_RTOL\ =\ 1e-4}; the stated rtol is restored afterwards. It is accepted
  only under the unchanged criteria: Photochem\textquotesingle s own steady state, then the top-face
  tolerance, the measured-flux and the elemental-conservation checks. A second stall
  refuses. It applies to the first solve, the new-temperature (warmed-column) solve
  and every pass of \texttt{steady\_state\_with\_top\_face\_at\_match}. A retry is logged as
  \texttt{steady\_state\_retry\ rtol=1e-04\ at\ pass\ \textless{}label\textgreater{}} (labels such as \texttt{top\ face\ 1\ after\ new-temperature\ solve\ 1}) and written to the profile header as the additive key
  \texttt{\#\ steady\_state\_retry\ ...} (\texttt{lower\_profile\_schema.write\_profile}, written only
  when a retry happened); \texttt{reached\_steady\_state} keeps its meaning. The Fortran
  reader ignores the key (\texttt{lower\_atmosphere\_profile.f90}, \texttt{case\ default}) and the
  Python readers skip \texttt{\#} lines. The refusal messages now say that the retry also
  stalled.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Tests} (\texttt{src/tests/matching\_surface/}, class \texttt{StallRetryTests}, six tests; a
stand-in model that follows Photochem\textquotesingle s \texttt{robust\_step} contract, including the give-up
at \texttt{total\_step\_counter\ \textgreater{}\ max\_total\_step}). RED on the adapter before the change
(3 failures, 3 errors: the stall-then-steady pass refused, \texttt{max\_total\_step} 100000
instead of 160000 at \texttt{-\/-blocks\ 1600}, no header line); GREEN after: 16/16 in the
suite. \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column} PASSED, \texttt{element\_flux\_closure} 10/10 OK,
\texttt{physics\_probe/jupiter\_radius\_python\_tools.py} passed.

\textbf{The k05 stall, reproduced with the fixed adapter} (scratch
\texttt{/home/kiseon/.claude/jobs/f24ea156/tmp/k05\_fix/}, lart4, the k05 command of
\texttt{k05.failed.20261008/adapter.log} with its in-memory trial values, the closure\textquotesingle s

\subsection{125. Trial admissibility of the stationary residual, recovery of the outer alternation to the last converged wind, and the WENO3 primitive floor at the domain scale (plan rev2 step 2, user approval 2026-10-09)}\label{125-trial-admissibility-of-the-stationary-residual-recovery-of-the-outer-alternation-to-the-last-converged-wind-and-the-weno3-primitive-floor-at-the-domain-scale-plan-rev2-step-2-user-approval-2026-10-09}

Plan: \texttt{md/PLAN\_20261008\_corrected\_model\_rev2.md} step 2 and section 3 (checkpoint). Background: \texttt{md/osc\_diagnosis\_20261008.md} sections 3-5, \texttt{md/weno3\_primitive\_floor\_tests\_20261008.md}. Source before: the frozen \texttt{EXHALE\_a76da494.x} (its copy in \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/build\_cellsolve\_ifx/src/}). Frozen binary after: \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_a68121e6.x} (manifest \texttt{BINARY\_MANIFEST\_a68121e60694.txt}); it supersedes \texttt{EXHALE\_7fe0b827.x}, which carried the local WENO3 floor refused in 125.3 and was used only for the reproductions of 125.5 marked as such. Every number below is MEASURED on lart4 unless marked READ.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{125.1 Trial admissibility (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90}, \texttt{UW\_conversions.f90}, \texttt{charge\_exchange.f90}, \texttt{constrained\_chemical\_equilibrium.f90})}{125.1 Trial admissibility (steady\_newton.f90, UW\_conversions.f90, charge\_exchange.f90, constrained\_chemical\_equilibrium.f90)}}\label{1251-trial-admissibility-steady_newtonf90-uw_conversionsf90-charge_exchangef90-constrained_chemical_equilibriumf90}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{eval\_residual} screens every cell of the unpacked conserved state with \texttt{admissible\_conserved\_cell} right after \texttt{unpack\_U}, before any equation of state, boundary or rate: a new named refusal \texttt{refuse\_conserved\_state\_inadmissible} (code 8), with its counter in \texttt{n\_eval\_refusal(0:8)} and its text in \texttt{write\_last\_evaluation\_refusal}. No clipping of T. The early return writes every output (Fvec a quiet NaN, heat = cool = 0, n\_part = 0, f\_sp = the seed, the judged measures sqrt(huge) so that a division by a tolerance stays finite, cells\_judged = the refused cell) and puts back the products a discarded evaluation saves. An evaluation that does not ask for \texttt{admissible} is an evaluation of the iterate: meeting a state that is not a gas there stops the run with the cell named (\texttt{error\ stop}).
\item
  Both solves screen their entry state (\texttt{conserved\_state\_of\_the\_entry\_is\_a\_gas}) and return info = 1 with the cell named, so the iterate is admissible by construction.
\item
  The merit of a refused trial is not formed: the line searches of PTC and JFNK, the Levenberg-Marquardt curve and escapes, and the trust-region ray probes set the merit from the flag (the ray probes take the iterate\textquotesingle s merit, which makes no slope, and \texttt{ray\_slope\_verdict} reads the flag first).
\item
  The finite-difference banded Jacobian (\texttt{build\_banded\_jac\_full}) samples a color on its backward side, {[}F(Y) - F(Y - h e){]}/h, when every forward length (sqrt(eps) halved six times) is refused, as \texttt{jv\_product} already did; counted as \texttt{n\_jac\_backward\_color} and reported. A forward column the forward probe resolves cannot move.
\item
  The refusal census is printed for every solve (\texttt{(JFNK)\ residual\ samples:\ admitted\ ...,\ refused\ for\ a\ conserved\ state\ that\ is\ not\ a\ gas\ ...}), also on the route without species rows, where it was not printed before.
\item
  \texttt{admissible\_conserved\_cell} also requires the internal energy formed exactly as \texttt{U\_to\_W\_comp} forms it, E - 0.5 m m/rho, to be positive where it cannot overflow: a cell with E set to its kinetic energy passed the scaled test while the conversion gave rho e = 0 and the sweep was handed T = 0 (MEASURED, kind \texttt{rho\_e\_zero} of the new suite).
\item
  Charge exchange: the "thread holds a cell" state is its own threadprivate flag \texttt{cx\_cell\_loaded}, set with \texttt{cx\_cell\_T} in \texttt{cx\_set\_cell}; \texttt{cx\_require\_cell} checks the flag first; \texttt{cx\_add\_to\_turnover} requires T (the caller in \texttt{set\_turnover\_scales\_at\_field} passes \texttt{ieq\_cell\%T\_K}); \texttt{cx\_set\_cell} stops on a temperature that is not finite and positive (a rate coefficient is a Maxwellian average).
\end{itemize}

Tests (RED: a76da494 source plus the test hooks only, \texttt{ctl\_hooks}; GREEN: this source). New suite \texttt{src/tests/trial\_admissibility/run.sh} on the iontrans\_metals state (metals on), 16 rows: five kinds of trial (rho e \textless{} 0 in cell 2, rho e = 0, rho = 0, NaN energy, infinite momentum), each as an adoptable trial and as a probe: GREEN admissible = F with no radiative pair, no particle count and no finite row (10/10); RED admissible = F but 1499 finite rows from a sweep at T = -2.4e-3 K for the first two kinds, and an abort in charge exchange "at T = NaN" at rho = 0. Iterate after the refused trials: 0 of 1500 entries differ (GREEN). Jacobian at a cell whose rho e is 1e-12 of its kinetic energy: forward probe at the shortest length refused, 0 unresolved colors, the momentum column equal to the backward difference of that column alone to 2.9e-4 of its largest entry. An iterate that is not a gas stops the run named (RED: evaluated, "REACHED"). A solve entered at a state that is not a gas returns info = 1 at once, named (RED: a full JFNK run). \texttt{src/tests/charge\_exchange\_rows/run.sh\ cell\_tag}: a worker thread that never loaded a cell stops with "holds no cell" (RED: "last filled at T = -1 K"), a cell at -1.4356e-5 K stops in \texttt{cx\_set\_cell} (RED: given rate coefficients, "REACHED").

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{125.2 Failed-wind recovery (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion})}{125.2 Failed-wind recovery (EXHALE\_main.f90, steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion)}}\label{1252-failed-wind-recovery-exhale_mainf90-steady_wind_with_element_diffusion}

Definition (written at the declaration of \texttt{u\_wind\_conv}): the last converged wind is the conserved state u (ghosts included) and the species fractions f\_sp of the most recent pass whose solve returned 0, taken where its joint test takes them (after \texttt{sources\_and\_ghosts\_of\_the\_held\_composition}), with the carrier module state and the acceptance record of that solve (\texttt{gate\_rnorm\_accepted}, \texttt{gate\_fspread\_accepted}) beside them; every other quantity the loop reads (primitive state, T, excited hydrogen, heat, cool, eta, the sweep ledger, the ghosts) is rebuilt from the pair at held composition. The wind is a solution for its composition, so the two are restored together. The boundary inputs do not change inside one call, and the record does not outlive it.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  After a failed solve (info 1, 2 or 3) the pair is restored, the pass keeps its flag, and the update is retried from the restored wind with a shorter step: omega halves (floor 0.125) when an element update was consumed; the carrier bound halves (floor 1e-3) whether or not the update ended on it, unless held. Where nothing can be shortened the alternation ends with the new named ending \texttt{outer\_failed\_update\_unshortenable} (the next pass would repeat the failed one; the state handed back is the restored wind, not accepted).
\item
  First pass (no converged wind): the solve is repeated once from its own iterate at the same composition (nothing is relabeled; no update has been consumed); if that fails too the alternation ends, \texttt{outer\_no\_converged\_wind}, with the iterate and its flag.
\item
  The acceptance record is restored with the wind: before, the written restored state was compared with the flux spread of the discarded iterate and the run printed "WARNING: the face-flux spread of the written state differs from the value the solve accepted" (route B 8.06e-2 accepted against 2.17e-12 written); after, 1.785e-12 against 1.893e-12, no warning.
\end{itemize}

Tests: new suite \texttt{src/tests/failed\_wind\_recovery/run.sh} (iontrans\_metals, hook \texttt{EXHALE\_TEST\_FAILED\_SOLVE\_PASS} reports the listed passes failed and raises their returned internal energy by 1e-3), 8 rows GREEN: the retried update is taken from the converged wind (distance 0) and not from the failed iterate (1.43e-2); the bound shortened 1e-2 -\textgreater{} 5e-3; the failed pass keeps info 1; the restored state stands at the joint distance the converged pass recorded (1.94072e3 both); the retry converges; a second failure returns to the same wind; a first-pass failure is solved again once and then ends with no update. RED (\texttt{ctl\_hooks}): the update is taken from the failed iterate (distance to the converged wind 1.45e-2, to the iterate 0), a second failure enters from the next iterate (2.1e-2), and a first-pass failure continues. \texttt{grid\_and\_gates/output\_state\_consistency.sh}: its stagnation fixture now ends on \texttt{outer\_failed\_update\_unshortenable} (pass 12 of the fixture ends info = 2 under the new floor, the bound is held, nothing can be shortened); the row accepts that ending, which hands back the certified state as the stagnation ending does (the next row, \texttt{outer\_iteration\_ending\_hands\_back\_one\_state}, 7.4e-16, PASS); reason written at the row.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{125.3 The WENO3 primitive floor at the domain scale (\texttt{Reconstruction.f90}, \texttt{hydrodynamic\_rows\_body.inc})}{125.3 The WENO3 primitive floor at the domain scale (Reconstruction.f90, hydrodynamic\_rows\_body.inc)}}\label{1253-the-weno3-primitive-floor-at-the-domain-scale-reconstructionf90-hydrodynamic_rows_bodyinc}

The approved floor eps\_k = (dr\_j q\_s,k)\^{}2, q\_s,k the largest \textbar W\_k\textbar{} of cells j-1, j, j+1, was installed first and REFUSED by measurement: on the regression matrix the marching WENO3 stage of \texttt{wasp\_full} and \texttt{wasp\_he23off} no longer reached their du \textless{} 1e-3 stop (wasp\_full: smallest du in each 2000 steps 3.8e-3, 3.7e-3, 3.6e-3, 2.7e-3, 3.2e-3, 3.7e-3, 3.4e-3, 3.6e-3, 3.7e-3, 3.7e-3, 4.0e-3, 4.4e-3, 4.1e-3 up to step 26 803; wasp\_he23off du 3.77e-3 at step 34 451; both killed by PID, outputs not compared), while the control stops at step 12 627 (du 9.989e-4, log10 Mdot 13.32). The cause, by test: the new binary with the dr\_j\^{}2 floor (\texttt{diag/floorB}) is bitwise the control through the stop at step 12 627 (outputs identical), so changes 125.1-125.2 do not touch the marching path; one component at a time (smallest du in steps 2500-3000; control 1.24e-3, local floor on every component 3.81e-3): local floor on rho alone 3.38e-3, on p alone 1.26e-3, local p and v with the sound speed in the v scale 3.81e-3, local p and v with the domain scale on rho 1.51e-3, the domain scale on every component 1.48e-3. The density component of the local floor is what keeps the marching state unsteady; the Newton-finished state is the same under either (wasp\_full\_newton log10 Mdot 13.29). Adopted: eps\_k = (dr\_j W\_s,k)\^{}2 with W\_s,k the largest \textbar W\_k\textbar{} over the physical cells, the solution-scaled floor of Yamaleev and Carpenter (2009, Eq. 65) with its norm over the domain as that equation writes it; S = 1 where b = 0. It is invariant under W -\textgreater{} cW (suite \texttt{weno3\_primitive\_floor}: domain\_scale rescaling rows 2.2e-16 to 4.3e-16 on smooth, step and stored states, PASS, where the retired dr\_j\^{}2 floor FAILs at 2.2e-4 to 1.7e3), third order on smooth data without the well-balanced option (3.00-3.01), and identical to the state-scale rows on every test of the suite (the suite scales whole profiles). Its cost, stated at the code: where a component is far below its domain maximum (the outer layer) the weights are linear and a jump there overshoots by up to 1/6 of the jump, which the positivity limiter bounds. With \texttt{Well\ balanced:\ True} the pressure face values are second order (1.99-2.06) for every floor: the second-order construction of Kaeppeli and Mishra (2016); now stated in \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/numerics.tex} and at the code. NEEDS CONFIRMATION by the user: this replaces the approved local form by the published domain form. The suite driver keeps the retired, state-scale and data-scale floors beside the production one (\texttt{KIND\_DOMAIN}), and its CHECK rows compare production with the domain copy (8/8 PASS, difference 0). \texttt{weno3\_vector\_matches\_primitive\_formula} (physics\_probe) uses the domain scale: 0 (PASS).

\subsubsection{125.5 The route-B and He/H 0.65 k00 entries (closure environment, omega 0.5, bound 0.04 held, 9-12 threads)}\label{1255-the-route-b-and-heh-065-k00-entries-closure-environment-omega-05-bound-004-held-9-12-threads}

Inputs: \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/\{route\_xuv\_scaled/HeH0.65/xuv\_x1p3,phase5/HeH0.65\}\_structural\_a76da494/k00}, copied to scratch. The charge-exchange abort does not occur; no trial was refused for a conserved state that is not a gas on any pass (census "not a gas 0" on every solve). What limits them now is the wind-carrier half: the hydrodynamic solve after the second (route B) and eighth (He/H 0.65) composition update fails (info 1, \textasciitilde8500 samples admitted, none refused), the converged wind is restored, and the retries fail too. Both endings are the restored wind, not accepted (route B: no progress in 3 passes at pass 5; He/H 0.65: no step can be shortened at pass 11, bound held and omega at 0.125). \subsubsection{125.6 Also corrected}\label{1256-also-corrected}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  The base conductive flux formula in \texttt{viscous\_conduction.f90} (comment of \texttt{heat\_conducted\_into\_lower\_atmosphere}) and in the header line \texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90} prints into every \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} read q = -kappa (T\_1 - T\_bath)/(r\_1 - r\_0) for a value labelled positive into the lower atmosphere; the value is kappa (T\_1 - T\_bath)/(r\_1 - r\_0) (READ: \texttt{lower\_atmosphere\_heat\_flux\_cgs\ =\ -q\_into\_wind}, q\_into\_wind = blo(1)(T\_b - T\_1)... \textgreater{} 0 into the wind). Corrected there and in \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/hydrodynamics.tex}, \texttt{molecules\_metals.tex}, \texttt{postprocessing.tex}, \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_user\_manual.tex} and \texttt{md/input\_schema.md}. The record \texttt{md/interface\_definitions\_step3\_20261009.md} 7.2 that found it is left as written.
\item
  \texttt{backup/regression/iontrans\_metals/input.inp}: \texttt{Restart\ option\ change:\ iontrans,\ wellbal,\ stage\_enth}. Without \texttt{stage\_enth} the case refused to start ("metadata field options differs ... stage\_enth: F -\textgreater{} T") with both binaries, so the matrix could not run it.
\end{itemize}

Not changed, reported: a stationary restart whose solve returns info = 1 is written with "the state written is a relaxation snapshot: the run made no stationary claim ... exits 0" (\texttt{certify\_the\_state\_to\_be\_written(info\_jfnk\ .eq.\ 0,\ ...)} in \texttt{stationary\_state\_of\_the\_loaded\_restart}, READ; the same in a76da494). The pair is consistent with the code\textquotesingle s rule (a claim is made only by a converged solve; status 2 belongs to a claimed and refused state), but the wording "relaxation snapshot" is wrong for a stationary restart whose solve failed, and the exit status 0 lets a script read a failed stationary solve as a run without a claim; changing the status is an interface change of the closure and is left for decision.

Docs: \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/steady\_state.tex} (trial refusal; failed solve and recovery; progress control), \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/numerics.tex} (the floor, the well-balanced order), \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_user\_manual.tex} (endings, recovery, refusal census), \texttt{md/TO\_BE\_DONE.md} (AR) 1 and (AQ).

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{126. An open base face starts from a seed that agrees with its ghost: the base cells take the ghost hydrogen partition, and the carriers are relaxed at the entry wind before the first solve (change 4 of step 2, user approval 2026-10-09; \texttt{md/cold\_base\_cell\_diagnosis\_20261009.md})}{126. An open base face starts from a seed that agrees with its ghost: the base cells take the ghost hydrogen partition, and the carriers are relaxed at the entry wind before the first solve (change 4 of step 2, user approval 2026-10-09; md/cold\_base\_cell\_diagnosis\_20261009.md)}}\label{126-an-open-base-face-starts-from-a-seed-that-agrees-with-its-ghost-the-base-cells-take-the-ghost-hydrogen-partition-and-the-carriers-are-relaxed-at-the-entry-wind-before-the-first-solve-change-4-of-step-2-user-approval-2026-10-09-mdcold_base_cell_diagnosis_20261009md}

Measured separately from section 125, after its checkpoint. Frozen binary \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_9ac38309.x} (manifest \texttt{BINARY\_MANIFEST\_9ac3830993b9.txt}; source = that of \texttt{EXHALE\_a68121e6.x} plus the two blocks below, in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}). Every number MEASURED on lart4 unless marked READ.

\subsubsection{126.1 What was implemented}\label{1261-what-was-implemented}

(a) \texttt{stationary\_state\_of\_the\_loaded\_restart}, step (2b): on an open face (\texttt{base\_h2\_composition\_imposed}) the cells of the uniform base region, 1..\texttt{N\_low\_cells}, take the n(H I)/n(H2) of ghost 0 as the boundary sweep of step 2 leaves it, each cell keeping its hydrogen nuclei and its mass (f(H I) + 2 f(H2) held; f is a number per unit mass). On the stationary restart route only: a marching run evolves the seed in time. The diagnosis\textquotesingle s control \texttt{match\_base\_partition.py} did the same on the IC file (READ).
(b) \texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion}, before pass 1: on an open face with transported carriers alternated, the carrier rows are relaxed at the entry wind (\texttt{relax\_photochemical\_composition} at the largest bound \texttt{carrier\_trust\_max} = 0.5, repeated up to 20 times while it ends on the bound and keeps steps), and the dependent state is rebuilt, so the energy row is not converged against an unbalanced carrier composition. The rule is stated at the code.

\subsubsection{126.2 Tests}\label{1262-tests}

New suite \texttt{src/tests/open\_face\_seed/run.sh} (route B k00, one pass): GREEN (this source) base partition of cells 1..50 at the hydrodynamic entry equal to the ghost\textquotesingle s to 7.6e-5 (tolerance 1e-3: the entry is read after the composition sweep that re-solves the eliminated H I), hydrogen nuclei as loaded to 3.9e-10 (tolerance 1e-9: the build without (a) departs by 3.7e-10 over the same load-to-entry path), Sidf of cell 1 -9.0e-4 (19.67 before), the entry relaxation attempted; RED (the source of section 125): partition 1.3e4 from the ghost\textquotesingle s, Sidf 19.67, no relaxation; the nuclei row passes in both.

Cells 1-60 in full: \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/cold\_cell/change4\_cells\_1\_60\_\{routeB,HeH209k00\}.txt}. (a) removes the cold cell 1 (route B 57.2 K -\textgreater{} 334.8 K, Sidf 19.67 -\textgreater{} -9.0e-4; He/H 2.09 57.4 K -\textgreater{} 354.5 K, 16.89 -\textgreater{} -1.6e-3), and the partition jump moves to the top of the base region, where the same cold cell reappears on pass 1 (cell 51: 54.9 K, Sidf 16.95 against -17.07 in cell 50; He/H 2.09 cell 51 60.6 K), as the diagnosis\textquotesingle s control RB\_ghost50 had (READ: 54.9 K). (b) does not act on these entries: the relaxation is refused at its first trial, "the shortest admissible trial was not covered", because the carrier substep acceptance refuses the H2 row of cell 2 (route B; relative residual 5.6 at full length falling to 4.7e-3 at 2\^{}-10 of it, the solve itself stopping at the round-off of its row terms, \texttt{carrier\_solve\_unreachable\_at\_substep}; diagnosed with an instrumented scratch build, not in the tree), so the composition enters pass 1 unchanged. Why the carrier substep cannot close that row at the entry state is not identified.

\subsubsection{126.4 Full alternations from k00 (closure environment, omega 0.5, bound 0.04 held, 9 threads, budget 60 passes)}\label{1264-full-alternations-from-k00-closure-environment-omega-05-bound-004-held-9-threads-budget-60-passes}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Route B: every hydrodynamic solve converges (49 passes, info 0 on each, against failures from pass 3 on without change 4); the base stays smooth (cells 1-3 at 270/302/331 K from pass 6 on; the cold cell 51 of pass 1 is gone by the end: cells 48-53 932-969 K at pass 49); the helium of the wind leaves (He/H at 2 R\_p 0.146 -\textgreater{} 8.3e-4), and the alternation ends at pass 49 with no progress in 3 passes: the He+ and He++ carrier balances of cell 354 stand at 0.96 and 1.00 of their tolerance scale (1e-5), He 2\^{}3S 0.13, the elemental He/H row 8.8e-2. Not certified.
\item
  He/H 0.65: 60 passes, all but passes 33-34 (info 2, restored and retried) converged; cells 1-3 at 420-435 K; He/H at 2 R\_p 0.38 -\textgreater{} 0.17-0.20 oscillating with a period of 6-7 passes; budget ended at pass 60 with the He+ balance of cell 217 at 0.88 (H+ 0.23, H2 0.16). Not certified. A shallow cool region remains at the old jump (cells 49-53 at 173-195 K against 245 K at cell 48, pass 60).
\end{itemize}

Neither k00 certifies with change 4. The wind-carrier failure of section 125.5 is gone on both; what refuses them now are the ionization-stage carrier balances in the wind (route B: at the helium separation front of a wind that loses its helium, the regime of \texttt{md/osc\_diagnosis\_20261008.md} section 1; He/H 0.65: the He+ row at 1.2 R\_p of section 4 there).

\subsubsection{126.5 Matrix}\label{1265-matrix}

No case of the regression matrix runs a stationary restart on an open face or alternates carriers on one (the only stationary case, iontrans\_metals, is atomic; the molecular cases are marching snapshots), so (a) and (b) do not reach it (code path, READ); the matrix was not rerun for this change.

Docs: \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/steady\_state.tex} (paragraph "An open base face starts from a seed that agrees with its ghost").

\subsection{127. The marching stops on the face mass flux and the state change, not on the cell-centered du; the local WENO3 floor is restored; secondary ionization is on from step 0 (2026-10-09/10, user decisions)}\label{127-the-marching-stops-on-the-face-mass-flux-and-the-state-change-not-on-the-cell-centered-du-the-local-weno3-floor-is-restored-secondary-ionization-is-on-from-step-0-2026-10-0910-user-decisions}

\textbf{Why.} An independent diagnosis (\texttt{md/weno3\_local\_floor\_marching\_stall\_diagnosis\_20261009.md})
showed that the local WENO3 primitive floor eps\_k = (dr\_j q\_s,k)\^{}2 (approved
2026-10-08, replaced in section 125 by a domain-scale floor because wasp\_full
never reached du \textless{} 1e-3 with it) is not defective. Its certified stationary
wasp\_full state (500 cells, secondary ionization off) has a face mass-flux
spread of 5.3e-13 but a cell-centered du = (max - min)/mean of rho\_j v\_j r\_j\^{}2
of 3.57e-3 (1.67e-3 on 1000 cells), set by cells 499/500 next to the outer
ghost; the domain-floor stationary state has du 1.76e-3. du is a
resolution-dependent property of the cell averages, not a measure of
stationarity. The secondary-ionization switch was gated on the same du: in the
local-floor march it never switched on. User decisions (2026-10-09): keep the
local floor; stop the marching on the numerical face mass flux and the state
change, du kept as a diagnostic; decouple the secondary ionization from du;
review the outer ghost condition (design note only, below). Threshold of the
face flux for the marching stop: 4e-4 (user decision 2026-10-10, from the
measurement in 127.2).

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{127.1 Changes (working tree; frozen binary \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_5ed2b073.x}, manifest \texttt{BINARY\_MANIFEST\_5ed2b073943e.txt}, ifx; gfortran tree build md5 7a1ff33f)}{127.1 Changes (working tree; frozen binary LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_5ed2b073.x, manifest BINARY\_MANIFEST\_5ed2b073943e.txt, ifx; gfortran tree build md5 7a1ff33f)}}\label{1271-changes-working-tree-frozen-binary-lhs1140bmodelsp2exhale_5ed2b073x-manifest-binary_manifest_5ed2b073943etxt-ifx-gfortran-tree-build-md5-7a1ff33f}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{Reconstruction.f90}, \texttt{hydrodynamic\_rows\_body.inc}: eps\_k = (dr\_j q\_s,k)\^{}2,
  q\_s,k = max \textbar W\_k\textbar{} over cells j-1, j, j+1 (the form of \texttt{Reconstruct\_scalar});
  comments no longer state that the local floor was refused.
\item
  \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{parameters.f90}: the marching stop is
  fspread\_gate \textless{} \texttt{march\_flux\_spread\_th} (4e-4; the face spread of
  r\_\{j+1/2\}\^{}2 F\_rho over r \textgreater= r\_flux, the steady solvers\textquotesingle{} gate quantity) AND
  chg\_win \textless{} \texttt{march\_change\_th} (1e-4), chg\_win = max over cells of
  \textbar rho/rho\_win - 1\textbar, \textbar E/E\_win - 1\textbar, \textbar(rho v) - (rho v)\_win\textbar/sqrt(rho E)\_win
  over one monitor window of N\_resid = 500 steps. A \texttt{{[}stop{]}} line prints both
  every window. The cell-centered du is computed and printed as a diagnostic
  and still drives the PLM -\textgreater{} WENO3 switch and the JFNK hand-off (both
  unchanged). The stall net (\texttt{relaxation\_stalled}, formerly \texttt{du\_plateaued})
  counts settled windows only while the face spread has stopped falling
  (less than 1\% drop since the previous window, \texttt{flux\_still\_falling}; added
  by the advisor after 127.2). Overrides for measurement:
  \texttt{EXHALE\_MARCH\_FLUX\_TH}, \texttt{EXHALE\_MARCH\_CHANGE\_TH}, \texttt{EXHALE\_MONITOR\_STEPS}.
\item
  \texttt{input\_read.f90}, \texttt{parameters.f90}, \texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90}:
  \texttt{Secondary\_ionization:\ True} (the default) now applies the coupling from
  step 0; \texttt{Staged} keeps the earlier behavior, its switch now made when
  chg\_win \textless{} march\_change\_th (out of the PLM stage), not on a du stop flag.
  The cold-start runaway that staging was made for did not occur
  (MEASURED: wasp\_full from its cold IC, coupling on from step 0, 20 000
  steps, no NaN; wasp\_full\_newton from its cold IC, Newton finish at log10
  Mdot 13.29, as with staging; \texttt{tmp/step2/diag/imm\_*}).
\item
  No consumer parses the old stop text (searched \texttt{src/utils}, \texttt{LHS1140b},
  \texttt{backup/regression}, \texttt{examples}, \texttt{src/tests}); \texttt{LHS1140b/models/run\_case.sh}
  reads only the \texttt{-\textgreater{}\ stopped:} prefix.
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection{127.2 Measurements behind the thresholds (wasp\_full, local floor unless stated, secondary ionization off, continued from a loaded state)}\label{1272-measurements-behind-the-thresholds-wasp_full-local-floor-unless-stated-secondary-ionization-off-continued-from-a-loaded-state}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Both floors give the same face-spread history (\texttt{tmp/step2/diag/cont\_loc},
  \texttt{cont\_dr2}): 7.5e-3 at step 5500 to 4.0e-4 at step 31 500, falling 4-5\%
  per 500-step window. The first implementation of the stall net ended both
  runs there as "stall, target not met" (chg\_win 8.5e-5 \textless{} 1e-4 for 2000
  steps) while they were still converging: a defect, fixed by item 2\textquotesingle s
  \texttt{flux\_still\_falling}.
\item
  Continued with the fix (\texttt{cont2\_loc}, 8 threads): the face spread fell
  smoothly (e-folding about 9000 steps) to 1.94e-5 at a further 27 500 steps,
  chg\_win 4.0e-6, where the stop at the steady gate 2e-5 was reached.
\item
  Between the states at face spread 4.0e-4 and 1.9e-5: rho changes by at most
  1.45e-3 (r = 1.415 R\_p), v by 1.24e-3, T by 3.3e-4 (medians 2e-4 to 5e-4);
  log10 Mdot 13.45 in both. Hence the marching threshold 4e-4 (user decision):
  the remaining change is below the proposed profile budget of
  \texttt{md/acceptance\_by\_observable\_precision\_20261009.md} (1\%).
\end{itemize}

mol\_base\_handoff, mol\_diffusion & 10.57 -\textgreater{} 10.51 & 0.30, 1.2 (v near the base), 0.19 \\
mol\_metals, mol\_ir\_bands & 10.58 -\textgreater{} 10.52 & 0.34, 1.1, 0.22 \\
mol\_lyman\_werner & 10.54 -\textgreater{} 10.49 & 0.24, 0.92, 0.15 \\

lower\_profile & 9.56 -\textgreater{} 9.57 & 0.41, 1.5, 0.35 \\
oxygen\_chemistry & 9.40 -\textgreater{} 9.40 & 0.022, 1.7 (v near zero), 0.18 \\
hp\_zero\_seed, hp\_trace\_seed & 10.56 -\textgreater{} 10.56 & 7.8e-5, 0.43 (base v), 4.1e-3 \\
hp\_front & 10.56 -\textgreater{} 10.56 & 6.4e-5, 0.31, 3.1e-3 \\

\end{longtable}
}
}

wasp\_full and wasp\_he23off (cold start, coupling on from step 0, the L1 outer
face at Mach 0.96) were stopped on 2026-10-10 when the user decided to move
the WASP outer face past the sonic point (section 127.6): at that time the face
spread stood at 1.7e-2 (wasp\_full, step 28 500, one thread), 1.3e-2
(wasp\_he23off, step 35 000) and 4.3e-3 (wasp\_full on 8 threads, step 46 500),
still above 4e-4. Every capped case still ends on its step cap (the new stop
does not fire earlier). The moved cases are the relaxation snapshots in which the secondary
ionization was previously never switched on (staged, never settled within the
cap); the molecular snapshots move toward \texttt{mol\_sec\_ion}\textquotesingle s Mdot 10.51. No
golden refreshed (end of series).

\subsubsection{127.4 Outer ghost condition: design note only}\label{1274-outer-ghost-condition-design-note-only}

\texttt{md/outer\_boundary\_subsonic\_review\_20261009.md}: the hydrostatic ghost
extension with copied velocity (\texttt{Apply\_BC.f90:458-472}) is applied at a
subsonic outer face (Mach 0.85 on wasp\_full) where v dv/dr is 5-9 times the
extension\textquotesingle s gravity, outside the validity its own comment states. Options
from the published sources (Thompson 1987, 1990; Poinsot \& Lele 1992;
Murray-Clay et al. 2009; Salz et al. 2016; Caldiroli et al. 2021): an outer
face beyond the sonic point for transonic winds, a relaxed characteristic
condition (whose Mdot is then conditional on an exterior pressure) for edges
that must stay subsonic. Not implemented; awaits the user. DECIDED 2026-10-12
(section 144): no subsonic-edge treatment; the Mdot of such a run is labeled
not quantitative.

\subsubsection{127.5 Exit status of a failed stationary solve; change 4 (b) diagnosed (2026-10-10, user decisions)}\label{1275-exit-status-of-a-failed-stationary-solve-change-4-b-diagnosed-2026-10-10-user-decisions}

\textbf{Exit status.} \texttt{certification.f90} (\texttt{stationary\_solve\_failed},
\texttt{certification\_note\_stationary\_solve\_failed}), called by the stationary restart
and the direct steady route of \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} with info /= 0: the state is
written with the reason \texttt{stationary\_solve\_failed} and the run exits 3 (it was
written as a "relaxation snapshot", exit 0). Exit 2 (converged, not certified)
is unchanged in the binary and is now a flag for its callers:
\texttt{src/utils/element\_flux\_closure.py} (\texttt{WRITTEN\_STATE\_STATUSES\ =\ (0,\ 2,\ 3)}; a
failed solve still stops the closure; exit 2 without the certification\textquotesingle s closing
line is a gfortran runtime error and stops it), \texttt{LHS1140b/models/run\_case.sh}
(accepts 3 beside 0 and 2 before classifying the ending),
\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P1/fiducial\_conduction/ew\_of\_state.sh} (evaluate exit 2 makes
the products, labeled uncertified; exit 2 without the closing line is fatal).
TEST (gfortran 106de97f): \texttt{iontrans\_metals} as a stationary restart with
\texttt{EXHALE\_JFNK\_MAXIT=1\ EXHALE\_OUTER\_PASSES=1} returns info 1, writes
\texttt{certified=F\ cert\_reason=stationary\_solve\_failed} and exits 3. A converged route
is untouched (code path).

\textbf{Change 4 (b): the refusal of the entry relaxation (diagnosis corrected the same day).}
A first reading (a scratch build printing the worst row when a substep ended
"unreachable at substep") found the H2 row of base cells 4-11 at 1e-8 to 4e-8 of
its physical terms, with the time term 2e8-1e9 times those terms at substeps of
1.6e-3 to 6.2e-3 s; a step floor built on it (step 2e-1 s) changed nothing, and a
second scratch build printing EVERY rejected substep showed why: those substeps
are the END of the halving chain (step fraction 4e-3 of a 50 s interval), and
such round-off-limited rows are already accepted with a flag by
\texttt{carrier\_returned\_state\_verdict}. The refusals that decide the outcome are
earlier and different (MEASURED, \texttt{tmp/c4b\_run2/run.log}, route B k00, first
entry relaxation, grow 1 to 1/16): at every substep length from the full
interval down, the Newton itself ends converged on its own scale (raw status 0)
or stagnated (3), yet the RETURNED state has 6-8 H2 rows at 0.2 to 5.6 of
their physical terms, in cells 2-9 at the base and 48-51 at the top of the
seeded region (cells 1-50 take the ghost partition under change 4 (a)). The
step floor was therefore removed again (the tree is back to the binary of the
exit-status change, md5 116c26a8). What moves the returned state off the Newton\textquotesingle s
root (MEASURED, a third scratch build printing the hydrogen budget of every
cell before the element limiter, \texttt{tmp/c4b\_run5/run.log}): in the seeded cells
1-50 the H2 of the entry state already holds ALL the hydrogen the cell has
(2 n(H2) = nH\_free to 1e-5: the ghost partition x(H I) \textasciitilde{} 1e-5 that change 4 (a)
writes), while above the seed (cell 51 on) 2 n(H2) \textasciitilde{} 0.86 nH\_free. The carrier
relaxation moves the carriers at FROZEN element densities, so any net gain of
H2 in a saturated cell asks for hydrogen the cell does not have: at the full
interval cell 1 asks for 2 n(H2) = 1.31 nH\_free (inflow through the open base
face), cells 2-12 for 1.13 down to 1.000002, and cells 41-50, at the top edge of
the seed, for up to 1.08 (H2 brought up from below while the seed edge cannot
pass it on). \texttt{limit\_to\_element\_budget} scales the H carriers back into the
budget, and the scaled state no longer solves its rows (0.2 to 5.6 of the
physical terms). Cause: change 4 (b) relaxes carriers at frozen elements on a
seed that saturates the hydrogen budget, so a stationary carrier profile there
needs a redistribution of the hydrogen ELEMENT that only the element
relaxation can make. Measured beside it (section 126.4): with (a) alone, (b)
refused at every entry, route B converged 49 solves in a row and the cold cell
left the grid, so (b) has contributed nothing. REMOVED (user decision
2026-10-10): the entry relaxation block and its variables in
\texttt{steady\_wind\_with\_element\_diffusion} (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, a comment states why),
the row \texttt{entry\_carrier\_relaxation\_attempted} of \texttt{src/tests/open\_face\_seed}, and
the (b) sentence of \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/steady\_state.tex}; change 4 (a) is
kept. TO\_BE\_DONE (AX) 1 closed.

\subsubsection{127.6 The WASP regression cases move their outer face past the sonic point (user decision 2026-10-10)}\label{1276-the-wasp-regression-cases-move-their-outer-face-past-the-sonic-point-user-decision-2026-10-10}

Measured on wasp\_full\_newton (\texttt{md/outer\_boundary\_subsonic\_review\_20261009.md}
section 5, figure \texttt{docs/figures/outer\_radius\_wasp\_full\_newton\_20261010.png}):
with the default Roche L1 outer face (1.574 R\_p, Mach 0.95, sonic point near
1.6 R\_p) rho and v differ from the 2 R\_p solution by up to 15-17\% near 1.31 R\_p
and log10 Mdot by 0.01, five times the grid effect seen between the 2 and 3 R\_p
domains (both supersonic). Decision: the WASP cases whose L1 face is subsonic
(wasp\_full, wasp\_he23off, wasp\_full\_newton; iontrans\_metals is a stationary
restart of a stored L1 state and needs a new stored state) take
\texttt{Outer\ radius\ {[}R\_p{]}:\ 2.0}. Test runs at 2 R\_p from the cold IC: \texttt{tmp/r2cases/}
(running).

\subsection{128. The column conduction as an exact excess over the climate column; the latent and eddy heat of the column measured (2026-10-10; TO\_BE\_DONE (AR) 3(ii), (AU))}\label{128-the-column-conduction-as-an-exact-excess-over-the-climate-column-the-latent-and-eddy-heat-of-the-column-measured-2026-10-10-to_be_done-ar-3ii-au}

Record: \texttt{md/column\_conduction\_and\_latent\_heat\_20261010.md}.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column\_balance.py} solves the total equation (option T of \texttt{md/interface\_definitions\_step3\_20261009.md}) in the excess unknown dT = T - T0 with the exact discrete perturbation flux (both terms) and the reference source S0 explicit (\texttt{balance\_terms}); the zero-input shortcut is removed (it returned a non-solution where T0 has a gradient), the depth test is on the response\textquotesingle s bottom flux, the Newton scale is the largest term of the balance. New suite \texttt{src/tests/conducted\_heat\_column/reference\_conduction\_tests.py} (RED on the entry text, GREEN now).
\item
  k05 (scratch rerun): T\_match unchanged, 240.982642 K with either text; S0 moves T by at most 3.1e-5 K (0.85 bar); the held geometry costs -6.4e-3 K on T\_match (MEASURED).
\item
  (AU): the change of the latent deposit after the coupling moves T\_match by -1.5e-12 K (not booked). The eddy heat flux of Photochem\textquotesingle s K\_zz = 1e9, booked as a change over T0, would lower T\_match by 55.4 K; not implemented, user decision on K\_zz near the match needed (note section 5).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{129. One stellar geometry at the matching face, set by the wind\textquotesingle s input (user decision 2026-10-10)}\label{129-one-stellar-geometry-at-the-matching-face-set-by-the-winds-input-user-decision-2026-10-10}

\texttt{md/matching\_face\_radiation\_python\_20261010.md} found three conventions for the
same stellar beam: the wind takes xi\_w F at normal incidence (xi\_w =
\texttt{dayside\_dilution()} of the input\textquotesingle s \texttt{2D\ approximate\ method}: 1, 1/2 or 1/4),
Photochem 0.5 F at 48 deg (0.335 F on the horizontal), the climate column F/4.
Decision: every model at the face takes the energy the wind\textquotesingle s input states.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py}: \texttt{-\/-face-geometry\ wind} is the default
  (\texttt{per\_model} reproduces earlier runs); it needs the wind\textquotesingle s xi\_w, from
  \texttt{-\/-wind-dilution} or the face file.
\item
  \texttt{element\_flux\_closure.py}: the chemistry call adds \texttt{-\/-face-geometry\ wind\ -\/-wind-dilution\ xi\_w} (xi\_w from the template\textquotesingle s \texttt{2D\ approximate\ method}, the
  rule of \texttt{dayside\_dilution}) unless the configuration names a geometry, and
  every wind run\textquotesingle s \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} value is compared with it
  (\texttt{check\_wind\_dilution}, refusal on a mismatch).
\item
  \texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90}: \texttt{dayside\_dilution} written to \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out}.
  What it changes for LHS 1140 b: its runs use \texttt{2D\ approximate\ method:\ Mdot}
  (xi\_w = 1), so the column now takes the substellar beam; on k05 (worker\textquotesingle s
  scratch run) the climate deep boundary moves from 561 to 878 K, the tropopause
  from 1.004 bar / 185 K to 0.48 bar / 220 K, the cold-trap water from 1.35e-7 to
  5.85e-5 and T\_match from 240.98 to 248.99 K (with the face file). The
  \texttt{2D\ approximate\ method} of the LHS inputs is a physical choice of the model
  and is reported with every result.
  Tests: \texttt{matching\_face\_radiation} 16, \texttt{matching\_surface} 16,
  \texttt{element\_flux\_closure} 10 (one row updated: a status 3 is now refused as
  "stationary solve failed", and a status 2 without the certification\textquotesingle s
  closing line as a runtime error, both before the post-processing pass; that
  suite had not been run after the exit-status change of section 127.5 and its
  old row failed until updated), all pass. Fortran: tree build md5 7cc33e0b.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{130. Eddy heat transport in the reference climate and the column; with K\_zz = 1e9 no reference climate exists (user decision 2026-10-10, option (b))}\label{130-eddy-heat-transport-in-the-reference-climate-and-the-column-with-k_zz--1e9-no-reference-climate-exists-user-decision-2026-10-10-option-b}

Record: \texttt{md/eddy\_heat\_transport\_column\_20261010.md}.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Physics: q\_e = rho c\_p K (dT/dz + g/c\_p) (Johnstone et al. 2018, Eq. 44, read;
  the potential-temperature form), with the chemistry\textquotesingle s own K\_zz
  (\texttt{lower\_profile\_schema.eddy\_diffusion\_coefficient}, one definition).
  Hunten (1974), on the dissipation heating that may balance the eddy
  cooling, was read in its ADS abstract only (paywalled; listed for the
  user).
\item
  \texttt{radiative\_convective\_column.py}: \texttt{stratosphere\_energy\_balance} replaces
  clima\textquotesingle s isothermal stratosphere by the solution of radiation (clima\textquotesingle s own
  \texttt{TOA\_fluxes\_dry}), molecular and eddy conduction on the column\textquotesingle s own nodes
  (dlnp 0.01 anchored at the match, no non-radiative flux through it),
  troposphere held; Newton with an analytic conduction and a finite-difference
  radiative Jacobian; clima\textquotesingle s altitude rtol tightened to 1e-12 (at 1e-9 its
  fluxes are a step function of T, MEASURED); superadiabatic or sub-50 K
  solutions refused.
\item
  \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column\_balance.py}: eddy face coefficient and the offset
  rho K g in the exact excess form, the reference\textquotesingle s held radiative heating in
  S0 (S0 is now the reference\textquotesingle s own imbalance), JANAF heat capacities, flux
  parts and imbalance records.
\item
  \texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py}: \texttt{-\/-eddy-heat-transport\ \{on,off\}}, default
  on; \texttt{off} reproduces the earlier runs bit for bit (k05 command: T\_match
  240.98267612 K, identical data rows, profile and solution\_id); the
  reference nodes are added to the column\textquotesingle s levels.
\item
  k05 (MEASURED): clima\textquotesingle s isotherm carries a net radiative heating of 1.78e4
  erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1 above the tropopause (7.51e4 with the wind geometry), so it
  is not a steady state; the eddy-consistent reference exists only up to
  K\_zz = 1.78e5 (per\_model) and 1.42e6 (wind) cm\^{}2 s\^{}-1, where it reaches
  the 50 K end of the opacity tables; K\_zz = 1e9 is refused. With the
  K\_zz = 1e5 chemistry (composition held) T\_match goes from 408.87 K (off) to
  411.93 K (on): +7.18 K from the new T0, -4.13 K from the eddy conduction,
  0.10 K from the reference imbalance. Photochem\textquotesingle s equilibrium initializer
  fails below about 180-184 K, so the eddy-on adapter cannot run the
  chemistry on such references.
\item
  User decision needed: every LHS 1140 b closure with \texttt{-\/-kzz-const\ 1.0e9}
  stops in its first chemistry call under the default; add
  \texttt{"-\/-eddy-heat-transport",\ "off"} to the closure\textquotesingle s \texttt{adapter\_args} to run as
  before, or decide on the dissipation heating (papers needed) or a K\_zz for
  heat (note section 6).
  Tests: \texttt{eddy\_heat\_tests.py} (16) and \texttt{stratosphere\_balance\_tests.py} (9), new,
  RED on the entry texts and GREEN; \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column} run.sh 126 PASS 0
  FAIL, \texttt{matching\_face\_radiation} 16 and \texttt{matching\_surface} 16 pass. No Fortran.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{130, addendum (user decision 2026-10-10, after the measurement above).} The
default of \texttt{-\/-eddy-heat-transport} is OFF: the chemistry\textquotesingle s K\_zz is used as an
effective tracer-mixing coefficient that carries no heat, since as a heat
diffusivity no reference climate exists above K\_zz \textasciitilde{} 1.4e6 cm\^{}2 s\^{}-1
(xi\_w = 1) and the LHS 1140 b closures use 1e9. The approximation and its size
are stated at the option in \texttt{photochem\_to\_lower\_profile.py}. \texttt{on} stays for
the review of turbulent dissipation heating (Hunten 1974, JGR 79, 2533;
Osborn 1980, J. Phys. Oceanogr. 10, 83, both supplied by the user) and for
heat-diffusivity sensitivities.

\subsection{131. The JFNK hand-off reads the face mass-flux spread (user decision 2026-10-10)}\label{131-the-jfnk-hand-off-reads-the-face-mass-flux-spread-user-decision-2026-10-10}

\textbf{Why.} The hand-off of a \texttt{Solver:\ Newton} run was keyed on the cell-centered
du \textless{} 1e-2. With the outer face of wasp\_full\_newton at 2 R\_p (section 127.6) du
never falls below 1.04e-2 (its resolution floor next to the outer ghost,
section 127), and the hand-off came only through its stall clause, after
45 118 marching steps.

\textbf{Change} (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}, \texttt{parameters.f90}, \texttt{input\_read.f90},
\texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90}; gfortran tree build a8ad24c4): the hand-off is armed
the first time a monitor step sees the face mass-flux spread (\texttt{fspread\_gate})
at or above \texttt{newton\_flux\_switch}, and fired at a monitor step below it; a state
whose spread has settled (the marching stall net) within 5 times the switch is
handed over too. \texttt{Solver:\ Newton\ {[}value{]}} now sets this FACE-SPREAD threshold
(it set a du threshold before); the variable is \texttt{newton\_flux\_switch}
(\texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} key of the same name), default 0.1. du stays a printed
diagnostic and still drives the PLM -\textgreater{} WENO3 switch.

\textbf{Measured} (wasp\_full\_newton, 8 threads, \texttt{tmp/handoff/}):

\toprule\noalign{}
outer face & switch & hand-off at step & JFNK & log10 Mdot \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
2 R\_p & 0.2 & 11 500 & info 0 (5 non-monotone accepts) & 13.28 \\
2 R\_p & 0.1 & 16 500 & info 0 (2) & 13.28 \\
2 R\_p & 0.03 & 21 500 & info 0 (1) & 13.28 \\
2 R\_p & du \textless{} 1e-2 (before) & 45 118 (stall clause) & info 0 & 13.28 \\

}

Default 0.1: converged at both outer faces with a margin (0.2 also converged
but needed more non-monotone accepts), a third of the steps of the du switch.

\texttt{iontrans\_metals} keeps its L1 domain: it is a stationary restart of a stored
L1 state whose purpose is the transported ionization stages, not the outer
boundary; a new stored state on a 2 R\_p domain would change what it guards
without guarding anything new (rev4 plan A2). Its outer face is subsonic
(Mach 0.95), which its output now reports.

\subsection{132. The H3+ LTE cooling function is MiZATeP (Mizus et al. 2017); the Miller et al. 2013 fit is removed (plan rev4 step B)}\label{132-the-h3-lte-cooling-function-is-mizatep-mizus-et-al-2017-the-miller-et-al-2013-fit-is-removed-plan-rev4-step-b}

\textbf{Why} (\texttt{md/h3p\_cooling\_cause\_20261010.md}): the Miller et al. 2013 LTE fit
cannot be reproduced from the line list and partition function its authors
state; the NMT and MiZATeP line lists agree with each other to 1\%, and the fit
lies above both by 1.14 (1000 K), 1.48 (1500 K), 1.89 (2000 K), 2.11 (3000 K)
(MEASURED). A correction of existing physics against its published source.

\textbf{Change} (worker diff \texttt{tmp/h3p\_impl/h3p\_mizatep.diff}, applied unchanged;
note \texttt{md/h3p\_cooling\_mizatep\_implementation\_20261010.md}): new module
\texttt{h3p\_mizatep\_cooling\_table.f90} (4981 nodes, 20-5000 K, verbatim from the
ExoMol file \texttt{1H3\_p\_\_MiZATeP.cf}, kept as
\texttt{references/ExoMol\_1H3\_p\_\_MiZATeP\_20170330.cf}; generator
\texttt{src/utils/h3p\_mizatep\_cooling\_table.py}); \texttt{h3p\_lte\_emission\_mizatep} replaces
\texttt{h3p\_emission\_lte} for the emission and for the W(T\_rad) absorption term, ln W
linear in 1/T between nodes; Miller\textquotesingle s non-LTE factor s kept (a ratio of its own
vibrational sums); renames in \texttt{certification.f90}; comments in \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}
and \texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}. Units unchanged (W molecule\^{}-1 sr\^{}-1).

\textbf{Verified by the advisor}: the built module returns 2.92879e-20,
1.29793e-19, 3.19812e-19, 9.54546e-19 W sr\^{}-1 at 1000/1500/2000/3000 K, the
ExoMol values; suites physics\_probe 2519 PASS / 0 FAIL (51 new H3+ assertions,
31 of them RED on the old module) and certification 145 / 0. gfortran tree
build f69f7371. Measured by the worker on 12 000-step molecular snapshots:
log10 Mdot unchanged (10.51, 10.52), total cooling -19\% and -7\% at 1.02 R\_p, T
within 0.5 K. Findings recorded in the note: the ExoMol \texttt{.cf} is wrong below
\textasciitilde16 K (rounded partition function; the table starts at 20 K), and Table 7 lies
0.5-4\% above the \texttt{.cf} at 3000-5000 K (the code follows the \texttt{.cf}, which a full
line sum reproduces). Living documents that still name the Miller fit are
updated in step D. Every H3+-bearing golden moves; refreshed at the end.

\subsection{133. Turbulent dissipation heating does not make the chemistry\textquotesingle s K\_zz = 1e9 a heat diffusivity; recorded, not implemented (review, user directive 2026-10-10)}\label{133-turbulent-dissipation-heating-does-not-make-the-chemistrys-k_zz--1e9-a-heat-diffusivity-recorded-not-implemented-review-user-directive-2026-10-10}

Record: \texttt{md/eddy\_dissipation\_heating\_review\_20261010.md}. No file in \texttt{src/}
changed (user directive: physics that published models do not treat is
recorded, not forced into the code).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Papers read in full (both supplied by the user): Hunten (1974, JGR 79,
  2533) and Osborn (1980, J. Phys. Oceanogr. 10, 83; a scan, quotations
  checked on the page images).

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Turbulence that mixes a stable gas with K draws the mechanical power
    K N\^{}2/R\_f per unit mass, and dissipates eps = K N\^{}2/Gamma of it into heat.
    Here Gamma = R\_f/(1 - R\_f) \textless= 0.2 (Osborn Eqs. 7-10); Hunten\textquotesingle s
    Eqs. 7-9 have Ri\_c \textasciitilde{} 0.2 in its place.
  \item
    Hunten: eddy mixing "probably heats the atmosphere and almost certainly
    does not cool it"; "It is better to leave out two compensating effects
    than to include only one of them."
  \item
    Derived here: the mixing of theta also adds the work against buoyancy,
    rho K N\^{}2, to the enthalpy of a hydrostatic column. The eddy flux of
    section 130 (Johnstone et al. 2018, Eq. 44) has neither term.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  Measured on k05 (xi\_w = 1, scratch copy of
  \texttt{stratosphere\_energy\_balance} with the heating, clima re-solved before
  every solve):

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    The eddy flux alone ends between 1.0e6 and 1.38e6 cm\^{}2 s\^{}-1 (below
    50 K); with the work against buoyancy alone it ends before 2.79e6.
  \item
    With dissipation a steady T(p) exists to 1e9: T at the match is 345 K
    (dissipation) or 383 K (both terms), and the stratosphere reaches
    650-720 K.
  \item
    At 1e9 the turbulent heating is 4.2e8-5.3e8 erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1: 790-1000
    times the absorbed stellar flux (5.36e5). It is conducted into the held
    troposphere, and OLR - ASR = 3.5e6-6.4e6.
  \item
    The heating passes the absorbed stellar flux at K \textasciitilde{} 1.2e6.
  \item
    Verdict: K\_zz = 1e9 cannot be a local stably stratified turbulence (no
    energy source of that size). It is an effective tracer coefficient, and
    \texttt{-\/-eddy-heat-transport\ off} stays.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  Published models (READ):

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Johnstone et al. 2018 carry eddy conduction (free K\_E \textless= 6e8) without
    the dissipation heating.
  \item
    Koskinen et al. 2013a/2022, Huang et al. 2023 and Yelle 2004 carry no
    eddy term in the energy equation; in CETIMB K\_zz enters the species
    diffusion only.
  \item
    Zhang \& Showman 2018 (ApJ 866, 1): K\_zz is a tracer-dependent effective
    coefficient.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  Proposal, not merged: if a heat diffusivity is ever switched on, the work
  against buoyancy and the dissipation go with it (scratch
  \texttt{turbulent\_heating\_functions}, off by default, 7 scratch tests PASS).
\item
  Defect found, reported, not fixed: clima\textquotesingle s \texttt{TOA\_fluxes\_dry} leaves its
  last profile in \texttt{c.T}, so a second \texttt{stratosphere\_energy\_balance} call on
  one clima object holds a drifted troposphere (bottom node 234.5 K or
  289.4 K instead of 222.66 K).

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    The adapter (one call per fresh object) is not affected.
  \item
    The K\_zz continuation tables of section 130 are affected beyond their
    first entry.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  Not measured: the column T\_match on the heated references (the analysis
  stopped at the verdict).
\item
  Figure: \texttt{docs/figures/eddy\_dissipation\_reference\_k05\_20261010.png}.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{134. The chemistry is handed the hydrogen-NUCLEUS flux, not the component-1 mass flux (plan rev4 step C)}\label{134-the-chemistry-is-handed-the-hydrogen-nucleus-flux-not-the-component-1-mass-flux-plan-rev4-step-c}

\textbf{Defect.} The elemental flux windows of \texttt{write\_element\_flux\_profile}
(\texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90}) give F\_H = 4 pi r\^{}2 (F\_rho (1 - Y\_He) - J),
the mass flux of component 1 of the binary split: the hydrogen carriers
TOGETHER with the trace metals and the C, O bound in molecules
(\texttt{mixture\_mass\_split}). The closure handed it to the chemistry as
\texttt{-\/-trial-flux-H}, which the adapter divides by m\_H as a flux of hydrogen
nuclei, so the metal mass was counted as hydrogen (0.44\% on LHS 1140 b).

\textbf{Fix.} The windows also reduce F\_Hnuc = s\_H F\_H, s\_H = m\_H n\_H / m\_1 the
hydrogen-nucleus share of component 1\textquotesingle s mass in the upwind cell (the metals of
component 1 share its advective and diffusive flux in this operator), written
as \texttt{lower\_profile\_F\_Hnuc\_\{median,spread\}} and \texttt{steady\_F\_Hnuc\_\{median,spread\}}
in \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} (\texttt{lower\_atmosphere\_profile.f90},
\texttt{write\_setup\_report.f90}). \texttt{element\_flux\_closure.py} reads F\_Hnuc
(\texttt{hydrogen\_nucleus\_flux}), falling back to F\_H with a stated reason for an
older EXHALE. F\_H and F\_He keep their meaning (F\_H + F\_He = Mdot). A resumed
closure whose history holds component-1 fluxes no longer matches its saved
measurement and is refused, as intended for a changed meaning.
MEASURED (\texttt{lower\_profile}, HD 209458 b with solar metals, 8 threads, tree
build d10b3634): F\_Hnuc/F\_H = 0.98820 in both windows (3.3144e12 / 3.3539e12
g/s overlap; 6.7128e9 / 6.7926e9 steady); F\_H, F\_He, Mdot unchanged.
Tests: \texttt{element\_flux\_closure} 10 and \texttt{matching\_surface} 16 pass.

\textbf{Also fixed (found by the dissipation-heating review, section 133).} clima\textquotesingle s
\texttt{TOA\_fluxes\_dry} leaves its last trial profile in \texttt{c.T}; the stratosphere
solve (eddy heat transport \texttt{on} only) now restores the climate object\textquotesingle s
solution on exit (\texttt{stratosphere\_energy\_balance}, a wrapper around
\texttt{stratosphere\_energy\_balance\_of\_held\_troposphere}); the module docstrings no
longer say the eddy option is on by default.

\subsection{138. A Roche domain extended past the potential ridge: the cold IC, the outer ghost and the absorbing columns assumed a potential rising outward (plan rev4 step E, 2026-10-11)}\label{138-a-roche-domain-extended-past-the-potential-ridge-the-cold-ic-the-outer-ghost-and-the-absorbing-columns-assumed-a-potential-rising-outward-plan-rev4-step-e-2026-10-11}

\textbf{Found by} the step-E benchmarks (Salz et al. 2016 HD 209458 b / HD 189733 b, Huang et al. 2023 WASP-121 b Cases B and D, Yan et al. 2022 WASP-52 b), every one a Roche domain with \texttt{Outer\ radius\ {[}R\_p{]}:} well past L1. Diagnosis note: \texttt{md/roche\_outer\_domain\_diagnosis\_20261011.md}. Frozen binary f8a38866 unless stated; MEASURED.

\textbf{Defects.} Past the ridge of the Roche potential (L1) phi falls outward, toward the star; three parts of the code assumed it rises.

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Cold hydrostatic IC (\texttt{set\_IC.f90}, rho = rho\_bc exp(-(phi - phi(1)))): the density rises without bound past the ridge (HD 209458 b, 15 R\_p: 5.5e10 cm\^{}-3 at 10.5 R\_p, 5.1e43 at 15 R\_p). Both Salz cold starts failed at step 0.
\item
  Outer ghost (\texttt{Apply\_BC.f90}, hydrostatic continuation exp(-(phi\_g - phi\_N)/c\^{}2)): past the ridge the factor exceeds one. Huang B at 12 R\_p, step 0: cell N 1.7e6, ghosts 3.4e34 and 4.9e63 cm\^{}-3; Yan WASP-52 b at step 6780: 3.3e3 against 3.6e22 and 8.1e41. Live runs at 15 / 8.85 / 8.2 R\_p carried ghost/cell-N ratios of 1e5 to 1e14.
\item
  The absorbing columns started at N+Ng, so the ghosts were gas on the line of sight (\texttt{calc\_column\_dens} and its two variants in \texttt{utilities.f90}, the sweep in \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}, the Ly-alpha line-center depth in \texttt{lya\_rt.f90}, the fine-structure escape column in \texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}). An exploding ghost shielded the whole domain: zero heating in every cell of the Yan atomic runs, T to 13 K, energy update failure (exit 2). In a domain with sane ghosts it added a two-cell column of no physical meaning.
  The regression case \texttt{wasp\_full} (outer face 2.0 R\_p, ridge 1.539 R\_p) carried defect 1: in 200 steps from the cold IC the density rose 2000-fold toward the outer face with inflow at 1.96 R\_p.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Fix} (physics stated at the code):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  IC: stratified on the running maximum of phi from the base outward; bitwise the old IC inside the ridge, held at the ridge value (in practice the 1e-8 floor) past it.
\item
  Outer ghost of a supersonic cell N (v\_N above the adiabatic sound speed): the steady isothermal wind through cell N, v\_g from the supersonic Bernoulli root, rho\_g from rho v r\^{}2 constant, p\_g/rho\_g = p\_N/rho\_N; valid in any potential. The subsonic edge keeps the hydrostatic continuation (open user decision, sections 127.4 and 127.6). The Bernoulli root solver has one definition, \texttt{isothermal\_wind\_speed} in \texttt{grav\_func}, shared with the transonic IC.
\item
  Every stellar-beam and escape column starts at the outer face r\_edg(N); the outer ghosts carry no depth.
\item
  Setup report: a Roche domain with \texttt{Outer\ radius} reads "extended to the user outer radius X R\_p; Hill radius Y R\_p" (it said "truncated at the Hill/L1 radius").
\item
  Test drivers \texttt{xuv\_cell\_mean\_attenuation.f90}, \texttt{lya\_beam\_cell\_mean.f90} (new assertion \texttt{lya\_outer\_ghosts\_carry\_no\_depth}), \texttt{lya\_escape\_probability.f90} on the outer-face convention.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Tests} (gfortran scratch build of this source): the five failing setups (Salz HD 209458 b and HD 189733 b cold IC at 15 R\_p, Huang B at 14.11 R0, Huang D at 13.28 R0, Yan atomic 92/8 at 10 R\_p) ran 3000-8000 steps with exit 0 and no energy-update failure; outer cells supersonic (Mach 9.4, 12.9, 181, 53, 50), ghosts at or below cell N, rho v r\^{}2 constant through the ghosts (Salz HD 209458 b: 3.49e14 in cell 502 and ghost 504). \texttt{wasp\_full} 200 steps: unchanged below 1.56 R\_p (median 2e-5), the cold-IC rise removed, outer Mach 0.067 -\textgreater{} 0.589. \texttt{mol\_base\_handoff} 200 steps (Hill domain): outermost cell only, at most 3.1e-3. Suites grid\_and\_gates free\_outflow\_boundary and hydrostatic\_residual 82 PASS; physics\_probe no failing assertion. Not done: the column change alone on wasp\_full. Every golden moves (column change); refreshed at step G.

\subsection{139. The internal energy of cold, highly supersonic expanding cells from the advected adiabat (dual-energy method) (plan rev4 step E, 2026-10-11)}\label{139-the-internal-energy-of-cold-highly-supersonic-expanding-cells-from-the-advected-adiabat-dual-energy-method-plan-rev4-step-e-2026-10-11}

\textbf{Found by} the Yan et al. 2022 WASP-52 b atomic runs after section 138 (Roche, 10 R\_p): h98he2\_f1, h92he8\_f1, h98he2\_f0p5 failed at steps 7290, 8795 and \textasciitilde8000, exit 2, "incoming temperature already below the floor" (T\_floor = max(0.01, 1 K/T0) = 13.04 K, \texttt{energy\_semi\_implicit.f90}). Note: \texttt{md/cold\_supersonic\_internal\_energy\_20261011.md}.

\textbf{Cause} (MEASURED, last accepted state of h98he2\_f1): the scheme recovers rho e = E - rho v\^{}2/2; at 9-10 R\_p rho e/(rho v\^{}2/2) = 5.7e-4 to 8.2e-4 (M \textasciitilde{} 50), and the difference carries the truncation error of the kinetic-energy flux. Between 9.03 and 9.14 R\_p the adiabat p/rho\^{}(5/3) drops 7.2-fold (1.00e-15 to 1.38e-16 cgs) where the net source is positive (heating 1e-16, cooling 2e-19 erg cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1), which an adiabatic flow with heating cannot do. Not round-off, not the outer ghost (the collapse starts \textasciitilde15 cells inside the face). Yan Fig. 23a (READ, digitized) gives 377 K at 9.15 R\_p and 334 K at 9.73 R\_p.

\textbf{Fix} (new module \texttt{adiabatic\_entropy\_transport.f90}; Ryu et al. 1993, ApJ 414, 1; Bryan et al. 1995, CoPhC 89, 149; full texts not read, their threshold values not compared): the adiabat K = p/rho\^{}gamma is carried with the face mass fluxes in every SSP-RK3 stage (donor-cell face value), taken fresh from the state at the start of each step; after an accepted step, rho e is taken from K where rho e \textless{} 0.01 E (M \textgreater\textasciitilde{} 13), div v \textgreater= 0 (no shock) and the gas has the constant gamma (atomic). Heating, cooling, conduction and viscosity are applied after it as before (operator split). Total energy is not conserved in the reset cells by less than 1\% of their E; the run log prints the number of reset cell-steps.

\textbf{Tests}: h98he2\_f1 8000 steps exit 0 (256 080 cell-steps reset; outer T 820-990 K, adiabat 1.05e-15 to 1.12e-15); h92he8\_f1 9000 steps exit 0 (294 429; T 427-527 K at 9-10 R\_p, outer Mach 22); \texttt{wasp\_full} 200 steps bitwise identical to section 138 (0 cells reset). Reached by: every Yan run, Huang B and D (75 and 78 cells, 2.8-13.8 R\_p; Huang B showed the same adiabat fall at its outer face), the Salz production states (22 and 261 cells). Open: in the 98/2 test the base cells flowed at -290 cm/s at step 8000 against +675 cm/s in the control at step 7271 (base breathing or this change, not separated).

\textbf{Benchmark binary}: \texttt{EXHALE\_dualE\_f9047247} (gfortran 16.2, source of sections 136 + 138 + 139, manifest beside it); all step-E runs were restarted on it from their last states (\texttt{Restart\ intent:\ relaxation}) on 2026-10-11 10:15 KST.

\subsection{140. Step-E inputs moved into the tree; documents brought to sections 138-139 (plan rev4 step E, 2026-10-11)}\label{140-step-e-inputs-moved-into-the-tree-documents-brought-to-sections-138-139-plan-rev4-step-e-2026-10-11}

\textbf{SEDs.} \texttt{inputdata/sed/hd209458b\_solar\_salz2016.txt} and its builder \texttt{build/build\_hd209458b\_solar\_salz.py} moved in from the session scratch; the builder run in the tree reproduces the file byte for byte (MEASURED, \texttt{cmp}). The two Huang Lyman-continuum files were already in the tree; \texttt{build/build\_wasp121b\_composite\_lyc\_huang.py} with and without \texttt{-\/-xuv-multiplier\ 0.74} reproduces both byte for byte (MEASURED). \texttt{build/sed\_coverage\_and\_order\_check.py} now checks the three files (file rules, band fluxes) and the three Huang configurations, and asserts the normalizations: Salz F(5-100 A) = 4.0041e1 and F(100-912 A) = 1.1028e3 against log L\_X = 26.40 and log L\_EUV = 27.84 at 0.04723 au; Huang F(1-912 A) = 2.69e5 and 0.74 x 2.69e5 (all PASS, exit 0). \texttt{inputdata/sed/README.md}: bin convention, inventory, band-flux table and build commands for the three files.

\textbf{A finding stated in the inventory.} The WASP-121 b files normalized to F\_XUV = 1.6e6 (\texttt{wasp121b\_solar\_huang2023.txt}, \texttt{\_wasp17\_}, \texttt{\_composite\_}) carry the number Huang et al. 2023 quote, not the flux their model was driven by (2.69e5; benchmark README of \texttt{benchmarks/huang2023\_wasp121b/}): about 6x brighter below 912 A. \texttt{WASP-121b/} and \texttt{benchmarks/wasp121/} still use the 1.6e6 composite; whether they switch is the user\textquotesingle s decision.

\textbf{Case D checked against the paper.} Section 6.1 (READ): "we reduce the stellar flux at wavelengths shorter than 1700 A to 74\% of the reference spectrum ... and weaken the stellar Lya radiation intensity to 35\% of the canonical value", the second being "the Lya flux used in the Lya radiative transfer calculation". The case-D SED (everything below 1700 A x 0.74, Lya line included) with \texttt{Stellar\ Lya\ flux:\ 3.5e4} follows that; no change.

\textbf{Comparison script.} \texttt{benchmarks/benchmark\_profile\_comparison.py} (from the scratch \texttt{compare/}), paths now relative to its own directory: runs under \texttt{benchmarks/\textless{}benchmark\textgreater{}/}, output under \texttt{benchmarks/comparison/}. The scratch copy keeps the scratch layout until the runs move.

\textbf{Documents} (sections 138-139 were in the code only):

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/radiation.tex}: the column starts at r\_edg(N), sum over j \textless{} j\textquotesingle{} \textless= N (it said r(N+N\_g)); the ghosts carry no depth.
\item
  \texttt{physics\_overview/hydrodynamics.tex}: either potential can be carried past L1 (it said only the spherical one could).
\item
  \texttt{physics\_overview/boundaries\_initial.tex}: the steady-wind ghost of a supersonic cell N; the cold IC on the running maximum of phi; the columns do not read the ghosts.
\item
  \texttt{physics\_overview/numerics.tex}: the dual-energy method in the operator-split step; Ryu et al. 1993, Bryan et al. 1995 and Yan et al. 2022 added to the bibliography (bibcodes and author lists confirmed on ADS).
\item
  \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_BC\_and\_IC.tex}: four stale statements fixed (no incoming characteristic at any outflowing outer face; the outermost ghost given its own column; Roche always truncated at the Hill radius; cold-IC line numbers), plus the steady-wind ghost and the running-maximum IC.
\item
  \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_user\_manual.tex}: Roche past L1 paragraph (IC, ghosts, columns), a paragraph on supersonic outer cells and the reset count of the run log; the \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} outer-face numbers marked as measured before section 138.
\item
  \texttt{README.md} (features), \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} (the same outer-face numbers marked).
\item
  \texttt{src/modules/states/Apply\_BC.f90}: the comment of \texttt{free\_outflow\_ghost} listed the column integral among the readers of the ghost; fixed (comment only).
  All three PDFs built with no undefined reference.

\subsection{141. Stale folders deleted (user decision, 2026-10-11)}\label{141-stale-folders-deleted-user-decision-2026-10-11}

A read-only survey of \texttt{EXHALE\_v1.00/} (three workers, references re-checked by the advisor) sorted every leftover into delete / ask / keep; the user approved the delete list and added \texttt{build/tests/}. 233 paths (list and log: the session scratch \texttt{delete\_list.txt}, \texttt{delete\_stale\_20261011.log}), about 10.6 GB, none tracked by git, none in use by a process on lart2/3/4:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P1/}: 64 audit, diagnostic and test directories of plan rev2 (binaries before sections 112-139). Kept: \texttt{fiducial\_conduction}, \texttt{v5\_test}, \texttt{audit\_F1} (live), \texttt{atomic97}, \texttt{s2\_continuation}, \texttt{newphys}, \texttt{ghost\_trial\_refusal}, \texttt{publish\_rows}, \texttt{cx\_round4}, \texttt{s1\_remeasure}, \texttt{connectivity} (cited by living documents or open items), and \texttt{iontr\_diag}, \texttt{progress\_control}, \texttt{omp\_scaling\_lart2} (cited as the measurement behind constants in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} and \texttt{init.f90}).
\item
