\subsection{142. The regression matrix measured across sections 138-139 (2026-10-11/12)}\label{142-the-regression-matrix-measured-across-sections-138-139-2026-10-1112}

Both binaries gfortran 16.2, \texttt{-O3\ -fopenmp}, the 17 default cases single-threaded: the tree build (md5 41af91c7, sections 136-139) against the same source before section 138 (\texttt{T/rochefix/ctrl/EXHALE.x}, md5 72ccde1b; its source differs from the tree only in the files of the 138 and 139 diffs, checked with \texttt{diff\ -rq}). A first control with the frozen ifx binary f8a38866 gave the same numbers to the digits below (compiler effect negligible), except that it refused to load \texttt{iontrans\_metals}: r\_max of the L1 domain differs in the last bit between ifx and gfortran and the case\textquotesingle s \texttt{IC/} (2026-09-27) was written by gfortran.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Every case ran to completion; \texttt{iontrans\_metals} CERTIFIED in both. The printed log Mdot is the same in every case (13.24, 13.24, 13.28, 10.49-10.52, 9.57, 8.27, 9.40, 10.56).
\item
  The dual-energy reset of section 139 acted in no case (0 cell-steps), so every difference is section 138 (columns from the outer face, steady-wind ghost of a supersonic outer cell, IC on the running maximum of phi).
\item
  Largest relative differences in the physical cells, MEASURED (\texttt{T/cmp\_phys.py}; cells where both values are below 1e-6 of the column maximum skipped): \texttt{wasp\_*} rho 1.1e-2, heating 2.0e-2, \textbar dv\textbar/c\_s 4.5e-3, all at 1.98 R\_p next to the 2.0 R\_p outer face, median rho 1.3e-4; molecular cases T 3e-3, heating 1.8e-2, \textbar dv\textbar/c\_s 8e-3 at 4.62 R\_p next to the outer face, median rho 7e-7; \texttt{lower\_profile}, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry}, \texttt{hp\_*} T and heating below 2e-3; \texttt{hydrostatic\_column} round-off (1e-13); \texttt{iontrans\_metals} rho 1.1e-2 at 1.32 R\_p, median 6.4e-4. Relative cooling differences up to 0.4 occur only where the cooling is 1e-4 of the local heating and changes sign (\texttt{mol\_base\_handoff}, 1.74-2.36 R\_p).
\item
  Verdict: the change acts where it should (the outermost cells, where the two ghost cells used to add column and where the ghost rule changed), and moves no Mdot at the printed precision. Every golden moves (the column change touches every cell above round-off), so the refresh belongs to plan rev4 step G as planned. Outputs: \texttt{T/reg\_new\_41af91c7/}, \texttt{T/reg\_ctl\_72ccde1b/}, \texttt{T/reg\_ctl\_f8a38866/}.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{143. WASP-121 b run of record: the spectrum Huang et al. computed with (user decision, 2026-10-12)}\label{143-wasp-121-b-run-of-record-the-spectrum-huang-et-al-computed-with-user-decision-2026-10-12}

\texttt{WASP-121b/input.inp} and \texttt{benchmarks/wasp121/input.inp} now load \texttt{inputdata/sed/wasp121b\_composite\_lyc\_huang2023.txt} (F(1-912 A) = 2.69e5 at the orbit, the Lyman-continuum flux Huang et al. 2023 section 2.2 states for their spectrum) in place of \texttt{wasp121b\_composite\_huang2023.txt} (normalized to the F\_XUV = 1.6e6 the same section quotes from Salz et al. 2019; about 6 x brighter below 912 A; the evidence is in section 140 and the benchmark README). These were the only inputs using a 1.6e6 file. Checked: \texttt{sed\_coverage\_and\_order\_check.py} exit 0, no FAIL (both configurations name the new file); a one-step run of the \texttt{WASP-121b} input (scratch copy, tree build 41af91c7) reads it and prints \texttt{Log\ XUV\ flux\ at\ the\ planet\ distance:\ 5.430}. No stored output was regenerated; both READMEs carry a 2026-10-12 section saying a run with this input is needed.

Found and fixed with it: both READMEs named the authors "Huang, Koskinen, Lavvas and Ben-Jaffel" (ADS: Huang, Koskinen, Lavvas, Fossati) and said Table 1 supplies \texttt{R\_p\ =\ 2.2075\ R\_J} and \texttt{R\_star\ =\ 1.458\ R\_sun}; Table 1 (READ, Delrez et al. 2016 values) gives the optical \texttt{R\_p\ =\ 1.766\ R\_J} and \texttt{R\_star\ =\ 1.4572\ R\_sun}. The input\textquotesingle s \texttt{Planet\ radius\ 2.2075\ R\_J} (1.25 x 1.766) has no identified source; it is a model input and is left for the user (Huang\textquotesingle s Case A base, from log g = 2.84 at 1 microbar, is 2.058 R\_J, the value of \texttt{benchmarks/huang2023\_wasp121b/caseA}).

Follow-up the same day (user decision): \texttt{Planet\ radius\ {[}R\_J{]}} of both inputs 2.2075 -\textgreater{} 2.058 (Huang case A, 1 microbar level from Table 3 log g = 2.84). Only the radius changed: these inputs keep a fixed base density (log n = 12.49) and \texttt{Equilibrium\ temperature\ 2358\ K}, whereas \texttt{benchmarks/huang2023\_wasp121b/caseA} sets the base by \texttt{Base\ BC:\ pressure\ 1.0} (1 microbar) and 3960 K (the first point of Huang\textquotesingle s published T profile); the two base conditions differ and are reported to the user.

\subsection{144. A subsonic outer face: no further treatment; its Mdot labeled not quantitative (user decision, 2026-10-12)}\label{144-a-subsonic-outer-face-no-further-treatment-its-mdot-labeled-not-quantitative-user-decision-2026-10-12}

Decision on the open item of section 127.4 (\texttt{md/outer\_boundary\_subsonic\_review\_20261009.md} section 6): transonic winds keep the outer face past the sonic point; no relaxed characteristic condition is implemented for a subsonic face, because the escape models the code is compared with (Salz et al. 2016, Murray-Clay et al. 2009, Caldiroli et al. 2021) put the face past the sonic point and state no incoming condition, and that condition would make Mdot depend on an exterior pressure none of them states (project rule on physics the published models do not treat).

Code (\texttt{steady\_mass\_loss\_rate}, \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}): below Mach 1.1 at the outermost cell the run log prints "NOT A QUANTITATIVE MDOT" with the reason, and \texttt{EXHALE\_setup.out} now carries the same line (it carried only the Mach number). Output text only. Also fixed: the comment of the beam ledger in \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} said the column N(N+Ng) is the outermost cell\textquotesingle s own content; since section 138 the columns start at r\_edg(N) and are zero above cell N. TEST (gfortran, md5 e6f7fcb7): \texttt{hydrostatic\_column} 300 steps in a scratch copy, outer Mach 0.79: both lines written; data rows of \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt} and \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} bitwise identical to the 41af91c7 build (only the provenance time stamp differs). Documents: user manual (new paragraph), physics document (upper boundary), README, README\_HOWTO.

Stored states of record ending subsonic at the default L1 face (estimate from the stored profile, gamma 5/3): \texttt{WASP-121b/} 1.02, \texttt{examples/18\_oxygen\_chemistry} 0.96, regression \texttt{iontrans\_metals} 0.95; \texttt{benchmarks/wasp121} 1.14 just above. Their inputs are unchanged (an input change is the user\textquotesingle s).

Second follow-up the same day (user decision): both WASP-121 b inputs now take the base and outer face of Huang\textquotesingle s case A: \texttt{Equilibrium\ temperature\ 3960\ K}, \texttt{Base\ BC:\ pressure\ 1.0} (1 microbar; the density key removed), \texttt{Outer\ radius\ {[}R\_p{]}:\ 16.403} (Roche potential kept past L1). In \texttt{benchmarks/wasp121/} the VULCAN handoff \texttt{base.inp} (T\_base 2135.9 K, r\_base 1.98187 R\_J, HeH\_base 0.0909, which override T0, R0 and He/H) was renamed \texttt{base\_vulcan\_photochemistry.inp}, so it is no longer read. One-step runs of both inputs (binary e6f7fcb7, scratch copies): p\_base 1 microbar, n0 1.8275e12 / 1.8290e12 cm\^{}-3 (case A benchmark: 1.83e12), planet radius 2.058 R\_J, upper boundary 16.40 R\_p, log F\_XUV 5.430. Stored outputs are not regenerated; both READMEs say so.

\subsection{145. Diagnosis: the base-cell mass row refusal is the known split between the solver merit and the certification scale (2026-10-12)}\label{145-diagnosis-the-base-cell-mass-row-refusal-is-the-known-split-between-the-solver-merit-and-the-certification-scale-2026-10-12}

Note: \texttt{md/base\_mass\_row\_refusal\_diagnosis\_20261012.md}. The Yan WASP-52 b molecular benchmark (cold IC) ended with the wind stationary (face mass-flux spread 1.9e-4, JFNK \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} 4.4e-10) and was refused by the mass row of base cell 1, 9.3e-3 against 2.2e-11. A stationary evaluation with the boundary trace and the mass-floor scan (lart4) shows face mass fluxes of cells 1-12 differing by 1.4 percent of their mean, 9-10 decades above rounding. Cause, as stated at \texttt{residual\_row\_scale}: the certification divides the mass row by its flux, the Newton merit by the state scale, on which the row reads its fractional flux error times the local Mach number (1.2e-4 here), so JFNK stops at a base layer it cannot see; the marching stop reads only r \textgreater= r\_flux. Not a rounding-anchor problem, and not the Salz HD 209458 b JFNK stall (merit stuck at 1.9). Under the acceptance rule of 2026-10-09 the benchmark Mdot (log 11.76) stands, the base-layer flux differences bounding the base supply\textquotesingle s remaining effect. No code changed: a solver merit on the flux scale is a solver item for the user to decide.

\subsection{146. Section 145 corrected after the Codex review: the refused Yan state is the marching fallback (2026-10-12)}\label{146-section-145-corrected-after-the-codex-review-the-refused-yan-state-is-the-marching-fallback-2026-10-12}

Review: \texttt{md/REVIEW\_20261012\_base\_layer\_mass\_flux\_solver.md} (Codex). Its seven corrections were checked against the code and the publisher PDFs and all hold (table in \texttt{md/PLAN\_20261012\_base\_layer\_mass\_flux\_solver.md} section 0.1): the Newton mass column scale is rho (\texttt{state\_scales\_of\_cell}), the printed \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} is the gate measure \texttt{rnorm} over the whole column and \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Fs\textbar{}\textbar{}2} the merit; Koskinen et al. 2013a sect. 2.1.1 set only the lower-boundary velocity from the flux constant (READ); Murray-Clay et al. 2009\textquotesingle s six dependent variables do not include Mdot (READ); the 1.4 percent spread is not an Mdot bound. Re-reading the raw log as the review asked: the Yan molecular stationary route converged its hydrodynamic rows (pass 20: base mass row 3.5e-11 against 1.7e-11, \texttt{rnorm} 4.4e-10) and failed on the carrier transport rows (H2 0.41, H+ 0.45, He+ 0.80 against 1e-5); the run fell back to marching and the 9.7e-3 refusal of section 145 is the verdict on that marching state. Section 145\textquotesingle s cause ("the merit cannot see the base mass row") is withdrawn; its benchmark statement for Yan (log Mdot 11.76 accepted by observable precision) stands, without the "1.4 percent bound". Huang Case B and Salz HD 209458 b stalled with interior rows near the measure\textquotesingle s maximum (1.9), far from a root. The review\textquotesingle s redesign (a common-flow constraint) is not started; the plan is rescoped to F1 (carrier rows in the partitioned outer iteration), F2 (the marching stop does not read the base layer), F3 (JFNK stalls far from a root).

\subsection{147. Second Codex review: the F1-F3 plan corrected (rev2) (2026-10-12)}\label{147-second-codex-review-the-f1-f3-plan-corrected-rev2-2026-10-12}

Review: \texttt{md/REVIEW\_20261012\_stationary\_failures\_F1\_F3.md}. Each finding was checked against the logs and the source before it was taken in; all held. Plan rev2: \texttt{md/PLAN\_20261012\_stationary\_failures\_F1\_F3.md} (its section 6 lists every correction with its evidence). The corrections that change what section 146 said:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  The Yan written state is a marching state that fails mass (9.68e-3), momentum (5.65e-4), energy (0.202) and the H2 and H+ carrier rows, not only the base mass row; the marching stop\textquotesingle s state-change part reads all cells (\texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90:4116-4133}).
\item
  A stationary evaluation of the same byte-identical restart files does not reproduce the run\textquotesingle s own residual (cell-1 energy flux divergence 4.89e-2 against 3.41e-2; energy row 0.444 against 0.202): reproducing evaluations is now prerequisite P0. The "1.4 percent" base-layer flux difference came from that reload\textquotesingle s trace of raw F without r\^{}2 and is withdrawn.
\item
  F1: the carrier relaxation does close its own block (2.39e-10 at pass 18 against \texttt{carrier\_accept\_tol} 1e-8); the alternation with the hydrodynamic solve is what fails. The worst gated cell at r = 1.2002 R\_p is the first cell of the certification gate, which is not a transport boundary; the H2 front is at 1.057 / 1.106 R\_p. The coupled block is refused with ionization transport (\texttt{input\_read.f90:2454-2520}). Pass 20 is not retained (p18, p19 only).
\item
  F3: the energy row is multi-term and not bounded by 2 (Salz HD 209458 b starts its JFNK at 2.206); the statement of section 146 that rows near 1.9 mean "terms of opposite sign" is withdrawn. Huang B\textquotesingle s energy row is at r = 5.51 R0 (divergence -1.02e-4 and net heating +9.81e-5 add), its mass row at 1.64 R0.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{148. Error-testing material deleted from the source and the test set (2026-10-16)}\label{148-error-testing-material-deleted-from-the-source-and-the-test-set-2026-10-16}

User decision 2026-10-16 (\texttt{EXHALE\_v1.10/md/environment\_variable\_options\_survey\_20261016.md}, D1 and D2): every piece of material created for error testing is deleted (fault injection, forced or expected failures, error reproduction, invalid-input and error-path tests, and the routines, variables, drivers, scripts, fixtures and environment controls only they use). Production failure detection, retry, rollback and recovery stay. No replacement error test was written.

\textbf{Deleted from the production source.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Environment controls and their injected behavior (19 names): \texttt{EXHALE\_REJECT\_STEP}, \texttt{EXHALE\_ELEMENT\_REFUSE\_STEPS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_TEST\_FAILED\_SOLVE\_PASS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_REJECT\_AFTER\_OP}, \texttt{EXHALE\_REJECT\_AT\_STEP}, \texttt{EXHALE\_REJECT\_ATTEMPTS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_REJECT\_IN\_PASS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_INJECT\_SOURCE\_ENERGY}, \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_ERR\_INJECT}, \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_ERR\_INJECT\_COUNT}, \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_FORCE\_UNRESOLVED}, \texttt{EXHALE\_CONDUCTION\_REFUSE\_DT\_ABOVE}, \texttt{EXHALE\_ENERGY\_FAIL\_AT\_STEP}, \texttt{EXHALE\_ENERGY\_FAIL\_CALLS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_ADV\_TEST\_REJECT}, \texttt{EXHALE\_TRIAL\_ADMISSIBILITY\_TEST} (the whole trial-admissibility block of \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}), \texttt{EXHALE\_PASS\_STATE\_STOP\_AT}, and the two found by the source search, \texttt{EXHALE\_CSM\_GEOM\_REFUSE} and \texttt{EXHALE\_CSM\_EXTRAP\_REFUSE} (the coupled source step refused every error estimate or every extrapolation candidate).
\item
  Routines and variables only these used: \texttt{attempted\_step\_injected\_refusal}, the reason code \texttt{as\_reject\_injected} (11 is now unassigned), \texttt{err\_pass\_index}, \texttt{inject\_source\_energy\_offset}, the error-estimate multiplier and the forced unresolved verdict, \texttt{attempted\_step\_perturb\_checkpointed\_state\_for\_test} and its \texttt{bump\_*} helpers, \texttt{element\_refuse\_leading\_steps\_for\_test}, \texttt{element\_refuse\_after\_accepted\_for\_test}, \texttt{conduction\_refuse\_dt\_above\_s} with \texttt{CONDUCTION\_INJECTED}/\texttt{CONDUCTION\_REASON\_INJECTED}, \texttt{energy\_update\_read\_environment}, \texttt{species\_face\_identity\_injection}, \texttt{stop\_if\_pass\_state\_step}, \texttt{carrier\_perturb\_checkpointed\_state\_for\_test} and its \texttt{bump\_*} helpers, \texttt{carrier\_reject\_leading\_attempts\_for\_test} with the reason \texttt{carrier\_reject\_forced\_for\_test}, \texttt{carrier\_history\_reset\_for\_test}, the test-only counter \texttt{carrier\_transport\_stops\_suppressed}, \texttt{lin\_test\_dense\_preconditioner\_fails}, and the public export of \texttt{build\_banded\_jac\_full}.
\item
  D2: \texttt{put\_back\_2d\_logical}, \texttt{same\_2d\_logical}; the always-true flag \texttt{resid\_at\_own\_composition} (the sweep to the fixed point now runs unconditionally; the comment in \texttt{parameters.f90} states it as fact, with its measurements); the never-set stop \texttt{step\_change\_converged} with its loop term, its report branch and the parameter \texttt{dtu\_th} (\texttt{dtu} stays a printed diagnostic). The \texttt{if\ (.false.)} line of \texttt{element\_census.f90} went with the dead snapshot argument it silenced, and the commented-out Norcross block of \texttt{cross\_sec.f90} was removed.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Kept at the boundaries, renamed for what they carry.} The negative \texttt{as\_inject\_op} that carried the real exhaustion of the nested positivity retry is now the logical \texttt{hydro\_bisections\_exhausted} (named \texttt{hydro\_stage\_retry\_exhausted} until 2026-10-17) (verdict \texttt{as\_reject\_hydro\_stage}, operation \texttt{as\_op\_hydro}). \texttt{read\_test\_reject\_reason} is now \texttt{read\_column\_energy\_solve\_controls}, still reading \texttt{EXHALE\_ADV\_COLUMN\_FLOOR\_IT} and \texttt{EXHALE\_ADV\_COLUMN\_EXCLUDE\_UNROOTED} once, with the logical \texttt{adv\_column\_controls\_read} in place of the \texttt{-1} marker. \texttt{chem\_cycles\_cap\_for\_test} stays as a measurement control (the single closure increments of \texttt{closure\_returned\_state.f90}); \texttt{carrier\_initial\_substep\_for\_test} stays for the step-doubling rows; \texttt{EXHALE\_AS\_FIXED\_DT}, \texttt{EXHALE\_ERR\_EVERY}, \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_SC\_MAX} and \texttt{n\_eq\_sweeps\_fixed} stay.

\textbf{Deleted from the test set} (\texttt{src/tests/}, not tracked by git). Whole suites: \texttt{failed\_wind\_recovery}, \texttt{trial\_admissibility}, \texttt{conduction\_retry}, \texttt{restart\_radius\_guard}; files \texttt{certification/certification\_capacity.f90}, \texttt{charge\_exchange\_rows/\{stale\_cell\_rates\_refused,stale\_cell\_stage\_sources\_refused,stale\_cell\_turnover\_refused,cell\_tag\_and\_temperature\}.f90}, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates/\{restart\_grid\_guard,sed\_coverage\_stop\}.sh}. Rows in 27 further suites: the injected refusals and exhaustions of \texttt{attempted\_step} (including \texttt{order\_by\_class}, the temporal-order measurement that needed the error injection), the checkpoint perturbation rows of \texttt{attempted\_step} and \texttt{carrier\_retry}, the forced refusals of \texttt{carrier\_retry} (sections 2, 4, 8, 12, 13) and of \texttt{closure\_returned\_state} (section 4), the face-identity injection of \texttt{species\_face\_flux}, the injected rejection reasons and the no-record transit refusal of \texttt{adv\_static\_limit}, the refused-key, missing-clock and inadmissible-handoff rows of \texttt{run\_mode}, the conflicting-name rows of \texttt{executable\_identity}, the NaN/Inf/negative-input rows of the unit drivers, the expected-failure controls of \texttt{steady\_species\_rows}, and the refused-input rows of \texttt{grid\_and\_gates}, \texttt{state\_mapper}, \texttt{transit\_state}, \texttt{molecular\_seed}, \texttt{spectrum\_type}, \texttt{energy\_update}, \texttt{element\_flux\_closure}, \texttt{matching\_face\_radiation}, \texttt{matching\_surface}, \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column}, \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg}, \texttt{coupled\_block\_handover}, \texttt{certification} and \texttt{physics\_probe}. The \texttt{executable\_identity} SUITES list, \texttt{README.md}, \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} (suite list, 61 suites) and \texttt{docs/EXHALE\_user\_manual.tex} (PDF rebuilt) were updated; the Makefile discovers suites by directory and needed no change.

\textbf{Verification} (all after the edits, once).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{make} (gfortran 16.2): no warnings. \texttt{-Wunused-variable\ -Wunused-parameter} on the 13 edited production sources against the same flags on the pristine source: no new warning (the six \texttt{as\_op\_*} imports and \texttt{build\_banded\_jac\_full} it found were removed). Binaries: control (pristine source) md5 51092371..., measured md5 bdd9fe4d....
\item
  Regression, 17 default cases single-threaded, control binary in \texttt{backup/regression/\textless{}case\textgreater{}} against the measured binary in sibling copies of the same case directories: the data lines of \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt}, \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} and both \texttt{\_adv} files are bitwise identical in all 17 cases (\texttt{iontrans\_metals} writes no \texttt{\_adv} pair), and the stop and final lines of the 17 logs are identical. \texttt{golden/} was not written.
\item
  \texttt{make\ test}, control on a pristine copy of the source (\texttt{git\ archive} plus the untracked \texttt{src/tests} and links to the untracked fixtures) and measured on the tree: no row fails in the measured tree that passes in the control. Measured: 60 PASS, 4 FAIL entries (\texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 4 rows of stationary evaluation and outer-iteration ending, \texttt{residual\_determinism} seed family, \texttt{steady\_completion\_flag}, \texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual}), each failing identically in the control. The two \texttt{residual\_determinism} branch runs (\texttt{atomic\_elem\_newton}) were still marching after 5.5 h in both trees and were stopped by hand. Control-only failures: \texttt{low\_energy\_electron\_degradation} (the copy cannot reach \texttt{../references/}) and the deleted \texttt{run\_mode} rows.
\item
  Whole-binary rows of \texttt{attempted\_step} (run separately, they need \texttt{EXHALE\_ATTEMPTED\_STEP\_EXE}): control 164 rows (2 FAIL, the deleted temporal-order rows), measured 64 rows, all PASS, each with the verdict and value it has in the control. \texttt{carrier\_retry}: control 156 rows, measured 84, all PASS and present in the control. \texttt{run\_closure\_returned\_state.sh}: PASSED.
\item
  \texttt{grep} of every deleted name in \texttt{src/}, \texttt{Makefile}, \texttt{README*.md} and the user manual: 0.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Coverage given up.} The recovery paths are no longer exercised on demand: a refusal after each operation of the trial, retry exhaustion in both run modes, the energy-update, conduction and element-step failures, the failed-solve recovery of the outer alternation, the checkpoint completeness (perturbation) rows, the interrupted publication, and the invalid-input refusals. The temporal order of each row class (RK3 p+1 = 4, species split p+1 = 2), from which \texttt{err\_order\_p} was set, has no direct measurement in the suite any more. Production detection, retry and rollback are unchanged (regression above).

\textbf{Files.} Three \texttt{\_\_pycache\_\_} directories under \texttt{src/} (765 377 bytes) and nine \texttt{.ipynb\_checkpoints} copies byte-identical to their originals (745 623 bytes; in \texttt{docs/}, \texttt{docs/figures/k22\_model\_a/}, \texttt{LHS1140b/}, \texttt{cooling\_data/}; none tracked) were deleted. The other byte-identical groups found by \texttt{md5sum} (audit run states and blocks under \texttt{docs/audit\_20260905/}, the Cloudy decks of \texttt{src/utils/h2\_shielding\_*}, \texttt{inputdata/windae\_grid/}, \texttt{benchmarks/koskinen2022\_model\_a/} restarts, the \texttt{docs/version\_compare/} binaries) are run records or inputs whose identity is itself a recorded result, and were kept. The public verdict variables \texttt{conduction\_last\_nfail}, \texttt{conduction\_last\_T\_floor} and \texttt{conduction\_last\_energy\_deficit} of \texttt{viscous\_conduction.f90}, written and never read once the conduction tests were gone, were deleted on 2026-10-17 (below).

\textbf{Follow-up, 2026-10-17: the two trees made to agree, the write-only conduction verdicts removed, the residual\_determinism branch bounded.} The deletion was made separately in v1.00 and v1.10 (\texttt{EXHALE\_v1.10/md/environment\_variable\_options\_survey\_20261016.md}, "Executed 2026-10-16"), and the two judgments differed. Every suite present in both trees was compared row by row (Fortran \texttt{check*}/\texttt{call\ test\_*}, shell \texttt{PASS\textbar{}FAIL\ \textless{}name\textgreater{}} lines, Python test methods) against the pre-deletion copies of both test sets, and both trees now carry the same rows under one rule: a row that constructs a failing input or state and asserts the failure report, or reproduces a past defect ("red" rows), is deleted; a row that asserts normal behavior stands, even where it was first written as the control of an error row. The test files of the suites present in both trees are now byte-identical, apart from \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_flux}, \texttt{interdiffusion\_enthalpy\_flux} and \texttt{steady\_species\_rows}, which differed before the deletion (v1.10 physics) and carry the same rows. Rows deleted in v1.10 to match v1.00: the expected-failure rows of \texttt{energy\_update} (\texttt{test\_no\_bracket}, \texttt{test\_temperature\_floor}, \texttt{conduction\_floor\_tests.f90} \texttt{test\_floor}, with \texttt{cool\_constant} of \texttt{cooling\_models.f90}) and its three defect reproductions (\texttt{falling\_branch\_two\_iteration\_residual\_red}, \texttt{floor\_clamp\_would\_have\_moved\_the\_state\_red}, \texttt{h2\_conductivity\_8p57K\_exp\_form\_nan\_red}); \texttt{inadmissible\_probe\_is\_refused\_at\_any\_ratio} (\texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg}); the defect rows \texttt{live\_token\_on\_the\_stalling\_pass\_is\_not\_the\_bound} and \texttt{old\_conjunction\_cannot\_hold} (\texttt{coupled\_block\_handover}); \texttt{nh3\_x300\_exchange\_beyond\_its\_bound\_is\_refused} (\texttt{conducted\_heat\_column}). Rows deleted in v1.00 to match v1.10: \texttt{kzz\_1e9\_refused\_out\_of\_the\_table\_range} (\texttt{conducted\_heat\_column}), \texttt{test\_nonzero\_trial\_against\_zero\_measured\_is\_refused} (\texttt{element\_flux\_closure}), the \texttt{hydrogen\_split\_residual(None,\ ...)} assertion and the \texttt{SystemExit} expectation of \texttt{test\_blocks\_is\_the\_step\_budget} (\texttt{matching\_surface}; the step budget is now asserted on a stall followed by a steady retry), and \texttt{reader\_reports\_incomplete\_evidence\_on\_truncated\_final\_solve} with its fixture (\texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual}). Rows restored in v1.00 from v1.10: \texttt{a\_zero\_residual\_norm\_is\_admissible}, \texttt{a\_finite\_positive\_residual\_norm\_is\_admissible}, \texttt{the\_largest\_finite\_residual\_norm\_is\_admissible} (\texttt{carrier\_retry}); \texttt{an\_unread\_healthy\_step\_is\_accepted}, \texttt{an\_unread\_healthy\_step\_moves\_the\_composition} (\texttt{element\_operator}); \texttt{the\_block\_was\_still\_entered\_once} (\texttt{coupled\_block\_handover}); \texttt{the\_increment\_test\_is\_the\_fallback} on the run with no estimate (\texttt{coupled\_source\_step}); and the two covered-SED runs of \texttt{sed\_coverage} (\texttt{sed\_coverage\_covered\_floor\_runs}/\texttt{\_output}, \texttt{sed\_coverage\_balmer\_table\_runs}/\texttt{\_output}, new file \texttt{grid\_and\_gates/sed\_coverage\_covered\_runs.sh}, README section 8). One name each: the \texttt{restart\_continuation\_factor} row is \texttt{factor\_zero\_is\_a\_factor} (its "not T" half was a refusal and is gone), and the \texttt{charge\_exchange\_rows} selector is \texttt{transport\_with\_metals}. Comments, dead imports and unused variables were made the same in both trees; the reference-run row of \texttt{attempted\_step/run.sh}, which in the v1.10 copy printed PASS before its check, now prints one verdict.

Production. The public variables \texttt{conduction\_last\_nfail}, \texttt{conduction\_last\_T\_floor} and \texttt{conduction\_last\_energy\_deficit} of \texttt{viscous\_conduction.f90} were deleted (declaration, export, assignment); \texttt{nfail}, \texttt{T\_flr} and \texttt{deficit} are still computed because \texttt{conduction\_report} prints them in the failure report. \texttt{conduction\_last\_step} is likewise written and never read, and \texttt{conduction\_last\_T\_solution} is read only inside the module; both were left (reported, not deleted). The two names the two deletions gave to one object are now one: the logical carrying the exhaustion of the nested positivity retry is \texttt{hydro\_bisections\_exhausted} (what is exhausted is the bisection of the hydro dt), and the reader of the two column measurement keys is \texttt{read\_column\_energy\_solve\_controls}; \texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90} is now identical in the two trees.

\texttt{residual\_determinism}. The branch runs of \texttt{atomic\_elem\_newton} never ended because \texttt{run\_residual\_determinism.sh} loaded the fixture under the default relaxation intent: the run marched until the face mass-flux spread, read every 500 steps, fell below the hand-off switch 0.1, and the fixture (mapped onto the face-level grid on 2026-09-27) enters at a face spread of 1.82 (measured at step 500; still marching at step 662 after 300 s). The branch run now appends \texttt{Restart\ intent:\ stationary}, which measures the loaded state and enters the stationary solve with no CFL step, which is what the family is about; the report then takes 6 s. Both runs of the script carry \texttt{EXHALE\_MAXSTEPS=1} (no measurement takes a marching step, so 1 is the smallest cap under which both are measured) and \texttt{timeout\ 600}; a run that ends on either limit prints \texttt{==\textgreater{}\ LIMIT\ step\_cap} or \texttt{==\textgreater{}\ LIMIT\ wall\_clock}, and \texttt{run.sh} fails the row with \texttt{measured=step\_cap} or \texttt{measured=wall\_clock} (the step-cap path was exercised on a copy of the script without the intent: it ends in 4 s with that row). Measured 2026-10-17: the suite takes 20 s (v1.00) and 28 s (v1.10), all nine rows PASS in both, the seed family finding one root with a closure spread of 0.1635 of the row tolerance.

Verification (once, after all edits). \texttt{make} in both trees: no warnings, \texttt{make\ -q} clean; binaries v1.00 md5 \texttt{6d7e381a...}, v1.10 \texttt{07d6cef1...}. \texttt{backup/regression/hp\_zero\_seed} (\texttt{EXHALE\_MAXSTEPS=100\ OMP\_NUM\_THREADS=1}), binary of each tree before and after these edits: the data lines of \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt}, \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} and both \texttt{\_adv} files are identical in both trees. The touched suites (those above plus \texttt{adv\_static\_limit}, \texttt{attempted\_step} with its whole-binary rows, \texttt{carrier\_retry} with \texttt{run\_closure\_returned\_state.sh}, \texttt{certification}, \texttt{charge\_exchange\_rows}, \texttt{executable\_identity}, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates}, \texttt{molecular\_seed}, \texttt{physics\_probe}, \texttt{run\_mode}, \texttt{species\_face\_flux}, \texttt{spectrum\_type}, \texttt{state\_mapper}) were run in both trees: every row that existed before keeps its verdict, and every restored row passes. No row went from PASS to FAIL. The failures are the ones that stood before: in \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} the three \texttt{stationary\_evaluate\_*} rows (certification unchanged, exit status, rebuilt within the sweep budget) in both trees and \texttt{outer\_iteration\_ending\_is\_the\_stagnation\_one} in v1.00; \texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual} without a run log (its fixture rows pass in both trees when it is given one); and in v1.10 the rows whose inputs are not in that tree (\texttt{examples/14\_diffusion}, \texttt{examples/15\_molecular}, \texttt{examples/lya\_insitu\_emissivity.py}, the LHS 1140 b model states and SED, the WASP-52b SED: rows of \texttt{adv\_static\_limit}, \texttt{charge\_exchange\_rows}, \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column}, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} and \texttt{physics\_probe}) and \texttt{carrier\_retry} \texttt{the\_full\_term\_scale\_accepts\_the\_imbalance\_at\_a\_short\_step}, which fails identically in the v1.10 control. Wall time of the slowest suites: \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 26 min (v1.00) and 50 min (v1.10), \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column} 20 min; \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} with its whole-binary rows (\texttt{EXHALE\_EXE} set) 5.5 min, 44 rows PASS in both trees, measuring 5.05 passes per coupled step against 6.03 for the run with no estimate in both.

\subsection{149. Uncalled routines, unbuilt sources and work-item option names removed from the source (2026-10-17)}\label{149-uncalled-routines-unbuilt-sources-and-work-item-option-names-removed-from-the-source-2026-10-17}

User decision 2026-10-17: what \texttt{md/leftover\_test\_code\_survey\_20261016.md} (sections 2.3 and 5) and the two environment-variable surveys (item-named category) found unneeded is removed, in both trees (\texttt{EXHALE\_v1.00}, \texttt{EXHALE\_v1.10}; in v1.10 only \texttt{src/}, \texttt{Makefile} and \texttt{docs/}, never \texttt{workflows/}, \texttt{runs/} or \texttt{provenance/}). Each routine was checked first with \texttt{grep\ -rniw} over \texttt{src/} (tests, Python, Makefile included) of both trees: no caller outside the files deleted with it.

\textbf{Routines deleted (23).} \texttt{h2\_vibrational\_critical\_density} (\texttt{h2\_vibrational\_relaxation.f90}), \texttt{hep\_h2\_three\_body\_domain} (\texttt{mol\_rates.f90}), \texttt{molecular\_infrared\_ready} (\texttt{molecular\_infrared\_cooling.f90}), \texttt{formation\_energy\_report} (\texttt{molecular\_reaction\_heat.f90}), the internal \texttt{gp} of \texttt{published\_rate\_coefficient} (\texttt{charge\_exchange.f90}), \texttt{free\_opacity\_tables} (\texttt{opacity\_models.f90}), \texttt{reset\_coupled\_block\_entry\_count} (\texttt{coupled\_block\_handover.f90}), \texttt{wae\_get\_ion\_pot} (\texttt{wae\_soe.f90}); the ten Wind-AE input readers reached only from the unbuilt comparison programs, \texttt{wae\_load\_all\_params}, \texttt{val\_after\_colon}, \texttt{read\_n\_reals}, \texttt{read\_n\_ints}, \texttt{read\_planet}, \texttt{read\_phys}, \texttt{read\_bcs}, \texttt{read\_term}, \texttt{read\_tech} (\texttt{wae\_params.f90}) and \texttt{wae\_load\_params} (\texttt{wae\_spectrum.f90}); and the five \texttt{parse\_csv} / \texttt{parse\_csv\_after\_colon} copies inside those programs. The survey counted eleven readers: \texttt{wae\_load\_spectrum}, \texttt{after\_colon} and \texttt{next\_csv} of \texttt{wae\_spectrum.f90} are called by \texttt{wind\_ae\_ic.f90} and \texttt{wae\_exhale\_bridge.f90} and stay, so the readers are ten and the total is 23.

\textbf{What became unused with them, also deleted.} The run counters \texttt{n\_hep\_h2\_evaluations}, \texttt{n\_hep\_h2\_three\_body} and \texttt{hep\_h2\_worst\_ratio} of \texttt{mol\_rates.f90}, read only by \texttt{hep\_h2\_three\_body\_domain} (the one-time warning of the He+ + H2 three-body domain guard stays); the module \texttt{wae\_ion\_pots.f90} (its table was read only by \texttt{wae\_get\_ion\_pot}) with its \texttt{use} and \texttt{ion\_pots\_init} call in \texttt{wind\_ae\_ic.f90} and \texttt{wae\_exhale\_bridge.f90}; the \texttt{wae\_config} import of \texttt{wae\_params.f90}; and in \texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90} the rate functions \texttt{rk\_O8\_O\_H\_assoc}, \texttt{rk\_O9\_H\_OH\_assoc}, \texttt{rk\_O10\_OH\_OH\_water}, \texttt{rk\_O12\_O1D\_H2O\_hydroxyl} and \texttt{lindemann\_rate}, whose only caller was the hand-compiled \texttt{a2\_m1\_rate\_check.f90}. Their published values, ranges, sources and verdicts stay in the comments of \texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90} (O8-O12 are not in the minimal set: 7.8e-9, 9.7e-8, 5.5e-7 and 2.8e-5 of O1 at the HD 189733 b base), so the audit still records a value and a verdict; the header and the THIRD BODY M note now say that no code evaluates them. \textbf{Reverted the same day (user decision, 2026-10-17):} unused reaction formulas are a physics decision, not cleanup, so \texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90} was restored in both trees to its state before this section (functions, exports and comments); the rate functions are uncalled again, the built model is unchanged.

\textbf{Unbuilt sources deleted (15) and what referred to them.} \texttt{src/modules/wind\_ae/\{base\_bcs\_test,eqn\_trace\_f,glq\_trace\_f,ic\_writer\_test,ncol\_test,ramp\_test,ramp2\_test,relax\_ae\_f,soe\_trace\_f,wind\_ae\_full\}.f90}; \texttt{src/tests/a2\_m1/a2\_m1\_rate\_check.f90}, \texttt{src/tests/a2\_m2/\{a2\_m2\_g3\_run\_check,a2\_m2\_kinetics\_check\}.f90}, \texttt{src/tests/e1\_h2/e1\_h2\_channel\_check.f90} with their saved \texttt{.out}; \texttt{src/tests/grid\_and\_gates/base\_closure\_candidates\_probe.f90} (named by no script). \texttt{backup/regression/windae\_oracle/relax\_gate.py} (v1.00 only) was read: it neither builds nor runs \texttt{relax\_ae\_f}, it only compares a CSV that program wrote, so it went with it. \texttt{a2\_m1\_thermo\_xcheck.py} and its \texttt{.out} went with the rate check because the script reads the check\textquotesingle s saved output; the three directories \texttt{a2\_m1}, \texttt{a2\_m2}, \texttt{e1\_h2} are therefore gone (none held a \texttt{run.sh}). Comments that named the deleted files were corrected: \texttt{element\_census\_tests.f90} now points to \texttt{physics\_probe/h2\_channel\_detector\_ratio.f90}, which gates the H2 channel sum and stoichiometry; \texttt{wae\_params.f90} and \texttt{wae\_spectrum.f90} describe what they now hold. No Makefile, README or \texttt{docs/*.tex} named any of the 15.

\textbf{Options and identifiers renamed for what they compute.} Each of the eleven item-named environment variables was read in the code. Ten have a current user and were kept: the v1.10 workflows set \texttt{EXHALE\_DIAG\_BASE}, \texttt{EXHALE\_P54\_TS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_L15\_TRACE} and \texttt{EXHALE\_L15\_DUMP\_AT}; \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} documents the four \texttt{L22B} keys; the two mask keys change a result when set and were kept with the default (unset) unchanged. \texttt{EXHALE\_L15\_REPEAT} had no user in \texttt{src/tests}, a README, the Makefile or the workflows and was deleted with the repeat check it switched on (\texttt{l15\_repeat\_check}, \texttt{l15\_first\_difference}, the \texttt{l15\_repeat\_at} switch). The v1.10 workflow scripts that set the old names run snapshot executables built before the change and were left as they are; a run of the current source needs the new names.

Log lines that named the items (\texttt{(L15)}, \texttt{(L22\ interval\ mask)}, \texttt{(L22b)}, \texttt{\#\ L22\ step\ 2b}, \texttt{\#\ L22\ step\ 3,\ increment\ I1}) and the comments that cited \texttt{P54/(AD)} and \texttt{P158} now say what they report. The tests \texttt{carrier\_base\_face}, \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_flux} and \texttt{carrier\_outer\_boundary} call the renamed \texttt{carrier\_jacobian\_options\_setup} and \texttt{carrier\_advective\_band\_derivative}; \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md}, \texttt{src/tests/coupled\_block\_jacobian/\{README.md,run.sh\}} and the user manual (\texttt{item\ L22\ increment\ I3}) were updated and the manual PDF rebuilt in both trees. The two environment-variable surveys were recounted from the source (v1.00 187 names, v1.10 196) and their item-named category is empty.

\textbf{Verification} (once, after all edits).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist

  \texttt{make} in both trees: no warnings, \texttt{make\ -q} clean; \texttt{make\ wind\_ae\_ic} builds without warnings in both. Binaries v1.00 md5 \texttt{737270bf...}, v1.10 \texttt{e17134e1...}. \texttt{-Wall\ -Wextra} on the 16 edited production sources: no warning names a deleted or renamed entity or an entity left unused by the deletions.

  Default behavior, binary of each tree before (\texttt{pre18\_v100.x}, \texttt{pre18\_v110.x}) and after, on scratch copies of \texttt{backup/regression} cases, \texttt{OMP\_NUM\_THREADS=1}, data lines (non-\texttt{\#}) of \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt}, \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} and both \texttt{\_adv} files compared with \texttt{cmp}: identical in every file for \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed} (v1.00 at its \texttt{maxsteps} 100; v1.10 at \texttt{EXHALE\_MAXSTEPS=100}), \texttt{lower\_profile} and \texttt{mol\_carrier} (v1.00, at their \texttt{maxsteps} 12000), 504 data lines each, with identical \texttt{final:} lines.
\item
  Renamed diagnostics switched on once (post binaries): \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed} with \texttt{EXHALE\_FACE\_MASS\_FLUX\_SERIES=5\ EXHALE\_BASE\_CELL\_STARTUP=1\ EXHALE\_CARRIER\_JACOBIAN\_ACTION=jac\_action.txt\ EXHALE\_CARRIER\_JACOBIAN\_ACTION\_CELLS=1,20} writes \texttt{output/face\_mass\_flux\_series.txt}, \texttt{output/base\_cell\_startup.txt} and \texttt{jac\_action.txt} in both trees; a stationary restart of the \texttt{wasp\_full} snapshot with \texttt{EXHALE\_NEWTON\_STATE\_HASH\_TRACE=hash\_trace.txt\ EXHALE\_NEWTON\_STATE\_DUMP\_AT=1\ EXHALE\_JFNK\_MAXIT=2} writes \texttt{hash\_trace.txt} (header \texttt{\#\ Newton\ state\ hash\ trace\ \ dump\_at\ 1}, iteration and Krylov records) and \texttt{hash\_trace.txt.dump1} in both trees; the \texttt{carrier\_retry} driver (it calls \texttt{relax\_photochemical\_composition}) with \texttt{EXHALE\_CARRIER\_INTERVAL\_MASK} (cells 2, 3), \texttt{...\_MODE=veto} and \texttt{EXHALE\_CARRIER\_STATE\_DISPLACEMENT=1} prints the \texttt{(carrier\ interval\ mask)} and \texttt{(carrier\ state)} reports and keeps its 86 PASS rows, as without them.
\item
  Suites that use a touched module directly (35 in v1.00 counting \texttt{element\_census\_tests}, \texttt{closure\_returned\_state} and \texttt{grid\_and\_gates}; 38 in v1.10): every v1.00 suite passes. The rows of the suites run earlier the same day (\texttt{physics\_probe}, \texttt{carrier\_retry}, \texttt{charge\_exchange\_rows}, \texttt{certification}, \texttt{krylov\_and\_dogleg}, \texttt{coupled\_block\_handover}, \texttt{coupled\_source\_step}, \texttt{element\_operator}, \texttt{attempted\_step}, \texttt{spectrum\_type}, \texttt{closure\_returned\_state}) have the same verdicts in both trees. v1.10 failures, each present before these edits: \texttt{carrier\_retry} \texttt{the\_full\_term\_scale\_accepts\_the\_imbalance\_at\_a\_short\_step} and the missing-input rows of \texttt{charge\_exchange\_rows}, \texttt{coupled\_block\_jacobian} and \texttt{physics\_probe} (section 148); \texttt{carrier\_helium\_inventory} (2 rows), \texttt{carrier\_returned\_state\_acceptance} (1), \texttt{helium\_metastable\_transport} (2) and \texttt{steady\_species\_rows} (9), which give the same verdicts on a copy of the v1.10 source with these edits reversed. \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} (run separately: 28 min in v1.00, 52 min in v1.10) gives the same verdicts as the run made earlier the same day, 384 rows in v1.00 and 349 in v1.10, with the failures recorded in section 148 (the three \texttt{stationary\_evaluate\_*} rows in both trees, \texttt{outer\_iteration\_ending\_is\_the\_stagnation\_one} in v1.00, and the v1.10 rows whose inputs are not in that tree).
\item
  \texttt{grep} of the old names (\texttt{EXHALE\_L15\_*}, \texttt{EXHALE\_L22*}, \texttt{EXHALE\_P54\_TS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_DIAG\_BASE}, \texttt{l15\_}, \texttt{l22\_}, \texttt{l22b\_}, \texttt{p54\_}, \texttt{upmap\_}, \texttt{p54\_flux\_ts}, \texttt{base\_diag.txt}) and of every deleted routine and file in \texttt{src/}, \texttt{Makefile}, \texttt{README*} and \texttt{docs/*.tex}: none, apart from the typeset update logs \texttt{docs/Update\_EXHALE\_stage\{1,2,3\}.tex} (records of their time) and a file name \texttt{lhs1140b\_stationary\_L22\_step3\_design\_20260916.md} cited by \texttt{coupled\_block\_jacobian}.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Found, not fixed.} \texttt{make\ wind\_ae\_ic} writes \texttt{build/wind\_ae\_ic.o}, a main program, next to the production objects; 18 suite scripts take \texttt{build/*.o} less \texttt{(EXHALE\_main\textbar{}\_tests\textbar{}\_probe)\textbackslash{}.o} and then fail to link (\texttt{multiple\ definition\ of\ main}). Measured here: after \texttt{make\ wind\_ae\_ic}, the suites with that filter that were run failed to build until the object was removed (it was removed in both trees before the verification above; \texttt{wind\_ae\_ic.x} stays). The filter is in \texttt{grid\_and\_gates/run.sh} among others, which this change did not touch; the one-rule fix (exclude \texttt{wind\_ae\_ic.o} in all 18, or build the IC generator\textquotesingle s main object elsewhere) is left for the next change to \texttt{src/tests}.

\subsection{150. Error corrections of v1.10 applied to v1.00 (2026-10-17)}\label{150-error-corrections-of-v110-applied-to-v100-2026-10-17}

User decision 2026-10-17: of the v1.00 to v1.10 changes (\texttt{EXHALE\_v1.10/md/changes\_v100\_to\_v110\_20261017.md}), only the error corrections of its section 1 (E1-E21) go into v1.00; the physics and algorithm changes P1-P9 do not. For each item the v1.10 hunk was read, the same defect was confirmed in the v1.00 code, and the correction was made in the v1.00 structure. The v1.10 record carries the short table (its section 7).

\textbf{Applied.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{E1} \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}: carrier transport pins H2/OH/H2O only in cells \texttt{j\ \textgreater{}=\ 1}; the lower ghosts are no longer held at the previous sweep or the restart seed. The H2+/H3+/HeH+ pins of the same v1.10 hunk are P3 and were not ported.
\item
  \textbf{E2} the reservoir\textquotesingle s H2 row is \texttt{x(4)\ =\ x2\ (1\ -\ x\_ion)\ -\ x\_O} with \texttt{x\_O\ =\ (n(OH)\ +\ 2\ n(H2O))/n\_H}: \texttt{x2} is one minus the atomic share of the non-ionized hydrogen (\texttt{base\_h2\_nuclei\_fraction}), so it already holds the OH/H2O hydrogen. \texttt{h2\_fraction\_of\_reservoir} takes the optional \texttt{x\_oxygen}; new \texttt{hydrogen\_nuclei\_in\_oxygen\_carriers} (\texttt{ion\_cell\_state.f90}); the four callers (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol\_metals.f90}, \texttt{constrained\_chemical\_equilibrium.f90} \texttt{network\_balance\_rows}, the ghost closure test and \texttt{impose\_transported\_fractions} in \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}, where \texttt{x(4)} is now set after the oxygen carriers). The \texttt{ion\_rates} comment that defined \texttt{x2\ =\ 2\ n(H2)/(2\ n(H2)\ +\ n(H\ I))} was corrected.
\item
  \textbf{E3} \texttt{load\_IC.f90} \texttt{base\_reservoir\_composition\_row}, kept in \texttt{load\_IC} (v1.10 moved it to a new module; nothing else in v1.00 calls it): \texttt{x2} without a handoff is taken relative to the non-ionized hydrogen of the first cell and includes its OH/H2O hydrogen; OH and H2O take their hydrogen from \texttt{x2} of the non-ionized budget (excess oxygen returns to O I); new \texttt{verify\_reservoir\_nuclei} stops the run when the row misses the reservoir nuclei or the unit mass by more than 256 eps (the case where H I or a neutral stage is clipped at zero).
\item
  \textbf{E4} \texttt{input\_read.f90}: oxygen chemistry with \texttt{eos\_metals\ 0} is refused (\texttt{error\ stop\ 1}).
\item
  \textbf{E5} \texttt{steady\_newton.f90} \texttt{eval\_residual}: the first composition pass, which has no previous heat/cool field, is unmeasured (\texttt{df\_eq\ =\ huge}), also under a one-pass cap; \texttt{measure\_the\_closure\_map\_at\_the\_base} forms an amplification only from a measured closure (\texttt{closure\_error\ \textless{}\ huge}).
\item
  \textbf{E6} PTC line search: the products of the accepted state are held before each trial and put back when the trial is rejected.
\item
  \textbf{E7} \texttt{residual\_evaluation\_products} now also holds the rate state of the sweep (\texttt{ieq\_rate\_state}, which gains the oxygen FUV photolysis rates \texttt{j\_h2o\_fuv}, \texttt{j\_oh\_fuv}), the conserved-row scales (\texttt{conservation\_row\_record}, \texttt{hold/restore\_conservation\_rows} in \texttt{steady\_residual.f90}) and the caloric mixture. The fourth v1.10 product, the boundary record, belongs to P1 and does not exist in v1.00. With the rate state now restored, the four evaluations of \texttt{measure\_the\_closure\_map\_at\_the\_base} keep their products (\texttt{state\_is\_discarded=.false.}, the enclosing hold restores them), or the first one could not settle the background as its header says.
\item
  \textbf{E8} \texttt{certification.f90}: \texttt{get\_species\_densities} (which installs the caloric mixture) before \texttt{U\_to\_W}.
\item
  \textbf{E9, v1.00 form.} P1\textquotesingle s boundary record does not exist, so \texttt{eval\_residual} records the conserved state with ghosts and the composition it assembles the residual on (\texttt{evaluated\_state\_conserved}, \texttt{evaluated\_state\_composition}, kept in the products), and \texttt{conserved\_ghosts\_of\_evaluated\_state} puts those ghosts back when the physical U and the whole composition are bitwise those of that evaluation. It is called at the same places as in v1.10: \texttt{certify\_returned\_state}, \texttt{certified\_rows\_of\_the\_state} (their \texttt{u} becomes \texttt{intent(inout)}), the PTC start, accepted step and return, the JFNK diagnostics and return, \texttt{row\_cancellation\_and\_the\_rounding\_floor}, and the outer-ghost rule of the coupled Jacobian diagnostic (whose reference evaluation now keeps its products).
\item
  \textbf{E10} element-constraint step limit: \texttt{room\ \textless{}=\ 0} no longer \texttt{cycle}s (an outward direction on an active face has zero length).
\item
  \textbf{E11} \texttt{element\_inventory.f90} \texttt{budgets\_and\_breach}: fixed nuclei above the element (\texttt{n\_reserved\ \textgreater{}\ free}) are a violation; helium has no carrier budget in v1.00 and is skipped.
\item
  \textbf{E12} \texttt{eval\_residual} forms \texttt{certified\_row\_measures} when any of its four outputs is asked for (local \texttt{evaluated\_rows}); without the P6 carrier mask.
\item
  \textbf{E13} \texttt{squared\_residual\_directional\_derivative}: the trust-region ray slope is a central or one-sided quotient over admitted probes only.
\item
  \textbf{E14} \texttt{measure\_molecular\_decay\_rates}: \texttt{have} reset at entry; \texttt{stable\_chemical\_decay\_spectrum} gives \texttt{-Re\ lambda} only when every \texttt{Re\ lambda\ \textless{}\ 0}, else no rates. The v1.10 exclusion of transported rows from the spectrum was not ported: the v1.00 header states that imposed rows are lifted on purpose, and the exclusion came with the P3 rows.
\item
  \textbf{E15} mass projection: \texttt{finite\_number} on the inputs and the factor; an unusable input refuses the projection; the refusal and report messages no longer call the factor an absolute density or a certain cell-solve failure.
\item
  \textbf{E16} \texttt{n\_judged\_excursions} counted on the adopted state.
\item
  \textbf{E17} frozen-Jacobian difference step on the state scale, halved until every gas cell is admissible.
\item
  \textbf{E18} GUI: \texttt{load\_input} reads \texttt{input.inp} without renaming it; \texttt{start\_func} appends the labeled options the form does not show (\texttt{exhale\_input\_options.py}, copied unchanged from v1.10). Comment lines of the old file are not kept.
\item
  \textbf{E19} \texttt{run\_EXHALE.sh} returns the exit status of \texttt{EXHALE.x}; \texttt{make\ check} fails (exit 1) without \texttt{backup/regression/run\_check.sh}; the build stamp says \texttt{tree=unknown} outside git.
\item
  \textbf{E20} \texttt{{[}lin{]}} formats \texttt{*(ES11.3E3)}.
\item
  \textbf{E21} \texttt{R\ =\ -du/dt} (\texttt{steady\_residual.f90}), \texttt{2\^{}44} (\texttt{utilities.f90}), the mass ceiling is unresolved and not passing (\texttt{certification.f90}), \texttt{f\_sp} a number per unit mass and \texttt{\textbar{}s-1\textbar{}} a relative change (\texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}), "exactly banded" and "merit descent is gate descent" (\texttt{steady\_newton.f90}). Also corrected in v1.00 for the same reasons: the carrier "mass fraction written onto {[}0,1{]}" of the write-back (it is a number density bounded by rho), the frozen residual\textquotesingle s users (the self-test and \texttt{what\_the\_band\_omits\_of\_the\_jacobian}), and the diag-7 label of the saved carrier measure (largest over all carriers).
\end{itemize}

Not ported, not in section 1 of the change list: the \texttt{write\_back\_element\_deficit\_last} diagnostic that makes the refusal value of a write-back deficit the last composition\textquotesingle s instead of the solve\textquotesingle s worst (v1.00 still reports the solve-wide worst there), and the new certification report lines.

\textbf{Verification} (once, after all edits).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{make}: no warnings, \texttt{make\ -q} clean. Binaries: before \texttt{pre\_backport\_v100.x} (md5 \texttt{e243f2cb...}, rebuilt from HEAD \texttt{6da7d3f1}), after \texttt{post\_backport\_v100.x} (\texttt{c8ad2fee...}); the comment edit made during the runs changed only source line numbers in the code (disassembly compared); the final build with \texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90} restored is \texttt{95a1372f...}.
\item
  Regression: the 17 default cases of \texttt{backup/regression}, copied to scratch, run as \texttt{run\_check.sh} runs them (\texttt{OMP\_NUM\_THREADS=1}, \texttt{maxsteps}, \texttt{outer\_passes}, \texttt{IC/}), with the binary before and after; data lines of \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz.txt}, \texttt{Ion\_species.txt} and both \texttt{\_adv} files compared. \texttt{golden/} was not touched.

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Identical in every data line (14 cases): \texttt{wasp\_full}, \texttt{wasp\_he23off}, the seven \texttt{mol\_*} cases, \texttt{lower\_profile}, \texttt{hydrostatic\_column}, \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_trace\_seed}, \texttt{hp\_front}. \texttt{mol\_carrier} is identical too: its ghosts take the handoff partition, so E1 does not reach it.
  \item
    \texttt{iontrans\_metals} (stationary restart, JFNK): largest relative difference 1.8e-10 (Ion\_species, N I at r = 1.32), 8.3e-11 in \texttt{Hydro\_ioniz}; face mass flux \texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2} unchanged to seven digits (7.282537e15), certified before and after; wall time 29.5 min before, 31.5 after. Cause E5, by bisection: the binary with only E5 reverted reproduces the old output bitwise, while reverting E7 or E9 alone does not. The first composition pass no longer exits on an unmeasured closure, so the JFNK path differs from its first iteration (\texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 8.221e-2 before, 8.195e-2 after) and reaches the same root.
  \item
    \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton} (march, then JFNK): marching identical to step 16500; after the hand-off the largest relative difference is 8.6e-11 (\texttt{Hydro\_ioniz}, \texttt{Ion\_species}) and 4.5e-10 in a near-zero \texttt{\_adv} residual column; \texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2} unchanged (7.244059e15); certified before and after; the JFNK solves end at \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} 1.26e-10 and 1.85e-11 after against 3.78e-10 and 4.65e-11 before, in 29 against 33 iterations. Same signature as \texttt{iontrans\_metals} (E5); not bisected separately.
  \item
    \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} (restart, 1000-step relaxation snapshot far from steady: face spread 6.0, state change 1.2e3): differs at the level of the transient, median relative change 0.2-0.3 percent in rho, v, p and the species, up to 21 percent in rho at r = 1.029 and 59 percent in v at r = 1.12; median \texttt{rho\ v\ r\^{}2} above 1.1 R\_p -0.32 percent, at the outer face -0.016 percent. Cause E1, by bisection: with E1 reverted the output differs from the old one by at most 7e-9 (heat 4.9e-5 at cell 71), and with E1 and E3 reverted it is bitwise the old output, so E3 accounts for that remainder. The lower ghosts are no longer held at the restart file\textquotesingle s carrier values (see below).
  \item
    The final build (\texttt{oxygen\_rates.f90} restored, \texttt{95a1372f...}) reproduces the outputs of \texttt{post\_backport\_v100.x} bitwise on \texttt{hp\_zero\_seed}, \texttt{hydrostatic\_column}, \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} and \texttt{iontrans\_metals}.
  \item
    No change is in a physically wrong direction: the stationary changes are at the solver\textquotesingle s tolerance and the solves converge tighter; the \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} change is the transient response to ghosts that are no longer pinned to stale values. The goldens were not refreshed (user decision pending); \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} and the two JFNK cases will no longer match them bitwise, the last two within \texttt{REGRESSION\_REL\_TOL}.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  Suites: every executable \texttt{src/tests/*/run.sh} except \texttt{conducted\_heat\_column} (no touched module), plus \texttt{element\_census\_tests}, \texttt{diffusion\_tests} and \texttt{closure\_returned\_state}, run on the tree after the edits and on a scratch copy of the tree before them. No row changed from PASS to FAIL and no expected value had to change. The failures are those standing before (\texttt{grid\_and\_gates}: the three \texttt{stationary\_evaluate\_*} rows and \texttt{outer\_iteration\_ending\_is\_the\_stagnation\_one}, the same measured values; \texttt{steady\_completion\_flag} and \texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual} without a run log); the scratch copy also lacked the Python tools and references some suites read, which removed rows there, not here.
\item
  E18: \texttt{import\ EXHALE\_interface\_functions,\ exhale\_input\_options}; on \texttt{wasp\_full\_newton/input.inp} the form keys plus the retained options reproduce the 34 option lines of the file. E19: \texttt{run\_EXHALE.sh} with a stub executable exiting 0 and 3 returns 0 and 3; \texttt{make\ check} without the harness exits nonzero with the message; the stamp outside git reads \texttt{unknown}.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Found, not fixed (needs a decision).} With E1 the lower ghosts of \texttt{oxygen\_chemistry} (restart, no handoff partition) are solved locally, and their cell solve fails (\texttt{info\ 4}, residual 1.0) at every sweep: 138 relaxation-amnesty non-roots at cells 0 and -1 in 1000 steps, none before. v1.10 shows the same (85 in its run of the case, \texttt{review\_20261015\_evidence}). The pinning that hid it was the defect; a ghost composition that has no root is a separate open problem of the lower boundary without a stated reservoir partition.

\textbf{\texttt{make\ test}.} v1.00: 64 entries, all PASS (66.6 min). v1.10 (before the \texttt{open\_face\_seed} copy): 66 entries, 63 PASS, 3 FAIL:

\begin{itemize}

So v1.10 stands at 64 PASS, 2 FAIL.
\item
  \texttt{coupled\_block\_jacobian} \texttt{the\_distance\_to\_the\_central\_difference\_scales\_as\_the\_first\_power\_of\_the\_arc\_energy}: with its fixture now present (\texttt{molecular\_scalar\_gj1132\_wellmixed/HeH0.55}, a v1.00 state), the energy direction at arc multiplier 10 (probe scalar 1.23) has a central sample the v1.10 binary does not admit (\texttt{all\ central\ samples\ admitted:\ F}), so its error is unread and the power is not formed; the other seven arcs read (energy 2.2e-4, 2.2e-3, 2.2e-2 at 0.01-1). OPEN, not diagnosed: whether the fixture (solved under v1.00 physics) or the v1.10 admissibility sets it.
\item
  \texttt{boundary\_state} \texttt{face\_state\_gap\_of\_the\_entry\_composition\_boundary}: not an input row. The control run (\texttt{EXHALE\_TRACE\_EXPERIMENT=boundary\_from\_the\_entry\_composition}) must leave the boundary of the entry composition standing; in v1.10 the evaluate route builds the ghosts inside \texttt{stationary\_ghost\_state} (P1) from the held composition, so suppressing the later \texttt{Apply\_BC} changes nothing and the gap is zero in both runs. The invariant holds; the control can no longer be produced on this route. OPEN, needs a decision: the hook exists only for this control row (section 148\textquotesingle s rule would delete both), or a new control is built.
\end{itemize}

Not checked: the whole-binary rows of \texttt{attempted\_step} (\texttt{EXHALE\_ATTEMPTED\_STEP\_EXE}) and \texttt{coupled\_source\_step} (\texttt{EXHALE\_EXE}) were not run; the skip-capable rows of v1.10 (\texttt{state\_mapper}, \texttt{open\_face\_seed} route-B and stored-state rows, \texttt{low\_mach\_stress\_energy}, \texttt{transit\_census}) still skip, their inputs not copied.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{152. The coupled-block power row on v1.10 and the deletion of \texttt{EXHALE\_TRACE\_EXPERIMENT} (2026-10-17)}{152. The coupled-block power row on v1.10 and the deletion of EXHALE\_TRACE\_EXPERIMENT (2026-10-17)}}\label{152-the-coupled-block-power-row-on-v110-and-the-deletion-of-exhale_trace_experiment-2026-10-17}

User instruction 2026-10-17: resolve the two v1.10 rows left open in section 151. Every number below is MEASURED on 2026-10-17 unless marked otherwise.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{152.1 \texttt{coupled\_block\_jacobian}: the energy power row of v1.10}{152.1 coupled\_block\_jacobian: the energy power row of v1.10}}\label{1521-coupled_block_jacobian-the-energy-power-row-of-v110}

\textbf{Diagnosis.} At arc multiplier 10 the energy direction\textquotesingle s probe scalar is 1.2272 on v1.10. The direction is the column scale of E, \texttt{v\_E(j)\ =\ \textbar{}E\_j\textbar{}} (\texttt{state\_scales\_of\_cell}), so the central sample \texttt{Y\ -\ eps\ v} lowers every E of cells 300-312 to \texttt{(1\ -\ 1.2272)\ E\ \textless{}\ 0}. \texttt{eval\_residual} refuses it (\texttt{admissible\_conserved\_cell}, rho e \textgreater{} 0), its residual is NaN, every \texttt{cd1} entry of the file is NaN, the moved-row statistic is not written and the power is \texttt{unread} (\texttt{\#\ all\ central\ samples\ admitted:\ F}). The species box is not involved: the energy direction has no species component (box fraction \texttt{huge}). The gas bound is \texttt{t\_gas\ =\ min\_j\ rho\ e\_j\ /\ E\_j}; a lower bound from the seed (\texttt{rho\ e\ \textgreater{}=\ 1.5\ p}) reads 0.999977, i.e. kinetic energy is a few 1e-5 of E in this subsonic layer. Bracketed by single-arc runs of the diagnostic: v1.10 admitted at multiplier 8 (eps 0.9817), refused at 9 (1.1044); v1.00 admitted at 24 (0.9967), refused at 26 (1.0797). The bound in multipliers is \texttt{t\_gas/eps1}: 8.149 on v1.10, 24.08 on v1.00.

Why v1.10 differs on the same seed (the two mapped seeds differ only in their path header): \texttt{eps1\ =\ sqrt(epsilon)(1\ +\ \textbar{}\textbar{}Y\textbar{}\textbar{})/\textbar{}\textbar{}v\textbar{}\textbar{}}. v1.00 registers one carrier row (H2), v1.10 four (H2, H2+, H3+, HeH+; \texttt{nvar\_jac} 4 against 7), and the logarithms of the three ion densities (-21 to -54) raise \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Y\textbar{}\textbar{}} from 475.0 to 1405.6 (both from the carrier probe scalars, 1.9672e-6 and 5.8132e-6, and on v1.10 equal to the norm of the \texttt{carrier\ density\ coordinate} lines, 1405.6). The energy probe scalar at multiplier 1 rises by the same factor 2.955, from 0.041528 to 0.122715. So the failure is the ladder, not the action and not the fixture.

\textbf{Re-solved seed (item b).} The mapped seed was given to the v1.10 binary (\texttt{bff2ab50}, one thread, \texttt{Restart\ intent:\ stationary}, \texttt{build/tests/lhs1140b\_resolve\_trial/}). The solve was stopped by its 4 h limit (exit 124) after 133 JFNK iterations with \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}R\textbar{}\textbar{}} at 1.88 (1.68 at entry, smallest 1.15), so no re-solved state exists and the seed was not replaced; the trial directory is kept. Independently of that solve, a solved state is expected to give the same bound, since \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Y\textbar{}\textbar{}} is carried by the ion logarithms and \texttt{rho\ e\ /\ E} is 1 to O(1e-5) in a subsonic layer; that expectation was not measured.

\textbf{Repair} (\texttt{src/tests/coupled\_block\_jacobian/run.sh} and \texttt{README.md}, identical in both trees). One ladder per direction: \texttt{CARRIER\_LADDER} 0.01, 0.1, 1, 10 (unchanged; the carrier box bound is 13.91 in t, 2.39e6 multipliers on v1.10 and 7.07e6 on v1.00, so the old comment\textquotesingle s "12.6 times the production arc" read a step scalar as a multiplier and is corrected); \texttt{ENERGY\_LADDER} 0.001, 0.01, 0.1, 1, four points a decade apart so the turn-of-the-V argument and \texttt{EXPONENT\_TOL} 0.30 keep their meaning, and on the truncation branch down to 1e-4 (section 10.2 of \texttt{md/lhs1140b\_coupled\_action\_20260921.md}). New rows \texttt{the\_arcs\_of\_the\_ladder\_are\_admissible\_displacements\_carrier} and \texttt{\_energy}: the top of the ladder lies below the bound (species box for the carrier, the seed\textquotesingle s lower bound on \texttt{rho\ e\ /\ E} for the energy, divided by \texttt{eps1}) and no file of the ladder says a sample was refused (the line exists since v1.10). No production source changed.

All rows PASS on both trees.

Observation, not changed: with the default unscaled probe rule (\texttt{jv\_probe\_on\_the\_column\_scales} false) the probe of an energy-only direction moves E by 12 per cent on v1.10 (4 per cent on v1.00) because the ion logarithms set \texttt{\textbar{}\textbar{}Y\textbar{}\textbar{}}; its truncation at the production arc is 2.2e-2 on these cells. The production forward difference samples only \texttt{Y\ +\ eps\ v} and is not refused there. Whether the probe length should be set from the column-scaled norm (\texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_COLUMN\_SCALE=1}, the existing alternative) is a solver decision left to the user.

\subsubsection{\texorpdfstring{152.2 \texttt{EXHALE\_TRACE\_EXPERIMENT} deleted}{152.2 EXHALE\_TRACE\_EXPERIMENT deleted}}\label{1522-exhale_trace_experiment-deleted}

Section 151 found the control row \texttt{face\_state\_gap\_of\_the\_entry\_composition\_boundary} vacuous on v1.10 (the evaluate route builds the ghosts from the held composition, so suppressing the later \texttt{Apply\_BC} changes nothing). The key existed only for that control, which reproduces the corrected order on purpose; by the rule of section 148 both are deleted. In both trees: \texttt{boundary\_rebuild\_suppressed} (\texttt{boundary\_state\_trace.f90}) and its eight call sites in \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90} (each now \texttt{call\ Apply\_BC(u)}, the behavior with the key unset), the \texttt{boundary\_rebuild\_suppressed} field of \texttt{alternation\_stall\_evidence} (\texttt{coupled\_block\_handover.f90}, written and never read) and its assignment, the field\textquotesingle s line in \texttt{src/tests/coupled\_block\_handover/coupled\_block\_handover\_contract.f90}, and the entry run, its row and its diagnostic in \texttt{src/tests/boundary\_state/boundary\_of\_the\_installed\_composition.sh}; the invariant row \texttt{face\_state\_gap\_at\_assemble\_residual} stays, and a gap the trace does not print fails it. Script comments and \texttt{src/tests/boundary\_state/README.md} corrected. The two environment-variable surveys and the leftover survey record the deletion; recounted literal reads of \texttt{src/} (continuation lines joined): v1.00 186 names (184 \texttt{EXHALE\_*}, 175 with production readers, 11 test-only), v1.10 195 (193, 183, 12).
